# 《说春秋》之一《齐楚崛起》第11章到第20章中英对照

说明：
- 本文件按源文本中的 `第33节` 到 `第62节` 整理，计划对应第十一章“挑战中央”、第十二章“冒充中央军”、第十三章“楚国和南联盟”、第十四章“一国两制的破产”、第十五章“美女是祸水”、第十六章“孔父嘉的妙计”、第十七章“美男也是祸水”、第十八章“做人的难题”、第十九章“朋友靠不住”和第二十章“老婆更靠不住”。
- 当前已完成第十一章到第二十章，本文件已完成。
- 排版规则为“一句中文，一句英文”；遇到较长句子时，补充语法说明和常用短语。
- 英文以自然流畅为主，较难词汇在首次出现处标注中文义。
- 保留原书真实章号，本文件按源文本顺序继续整理。

## 第十一章 挑战中央
## Chapter 11: Challenging the Center

俗话说：人挪活树挪死。  
As the old saying goes, people thrive when they move, while trees die when they are moved.
可是，首都搬家绝对是一件伤筋动骨的事情。  
But moving a capital always shakes a state to its bones.
在周朝的伟大首都从西部搬到东面之后，中央的威权迅速衰落，诸侯们渐渐地开始不尿中央这一壶了。  
After the Zhou capital moved from the west to the east, the authority（权威） of the center weakened rapidly, and the feudal lords gradually stopped taking it seriously.
诸侯们互相攻杀 ，目无王法，周王室的威望江河日下。  
The lords fought and slaughtered one another, with no regard for royal law, and the prestige（威望） of the Zhou royal house kept sinking.
现在，来说说周王室的地位变化。  
Now let us look at how the standing of the Zhou royal house changed.
这，需要从周的祖先开始说起。  
To explain that, we have to begin with Zhou’s ancestors.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`时间状语从句`、`并列句`、`并列谓语`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `As the old saying goes, people thrive when they move, while trees die when they are moved.`。<br>
难点拆解：时间从句先给背景，再推进主句动作。<br>
常用短语：
- `from that point on`：从那以后
- `could only`：只能
- `with its own hands`：亲手

### 周朝的建立
### The Founding of the Zhou Dynasty

最早从黄帝说起就太久远了，从后稷说起也有些长。  
Starting all the way back with the Yellow Emperor would take too long; even starting with Hou Ji would still be a long story.
只说一点，周王室是黄帝的后裔，姓姬，与黄帝同姓。  
Let us note only this: the Zhou royal house claimed descent from the Yellow Emperor and bore the same Ji surname.
到公刘那一代，跟戎狄混居。  
By the time of Gong Liu, they were living mixed in among the Rong and Di peoples.
什么是戎狄？  
But who exactly were the Rong and the Di?
上古时期，华夏族将周边的民族按照方位分为四个部分：北边的是狄，南边的是蛮，西边的是戎，东边的是夷。  
In antiquity, the Huaxia people divided the surrounding peoples by direction: the Di to the north, the Man to the south, the Rong to the west, and the Yi to the east.
后来常 说南蛮南蛮，就是这么来的。  
That is where the later phrase "southern barbarians" came from.
那么，戎狄就是西北的民族了。  
So the Rong and the Di were the peoples of the northwest.
由于公刘人品好，乐于助人，种粮食的水准又高，基本上就是雷锋加袁隆平这样一个人，因此受到各族群众的拥戴，成为那一带的首领。  
Gong Liu was said to be kind, helpful, and highly skilled at growing grain, basically a blend of Lei Feng and Yuan Longping, so he won wide support and became the leader of that region.
到儿子庆节，周国正式成立，地点应该在今天的甘肃一带。  
By the time of his son Qing Jie, the state of Zhou had formally taken shape, probably in what is now Gansu.
几百年后，到了古公亶（音单）父那一代，戎狄起兵来攻打，要抢财物，古公亶父给他们财物。  
Hundreds of years later, by the time of Gugong Danfu, the Rong and Di came raiding for wealth, and he gave them goods.
之后戎狄又来，要抢地盘和人民。  
Later they came again, this time to seize land and people.
古公亶父又要给他们，老百姓不干了，想要打仗，古公亶父劝他们说：“鬼子来打仗，无非就是要土地和人民。  
Gugong Danfu was ready to yield again, but the people refused and wanted war, so he tried to persuade them: "When raiders come to fight, all they want is land and people.
想想看，大家跟着我也是过，跟着他们也是过，有什么区别？  
Think about it. Whether you live under me or under them, what difference is there?
如果大家为了我而战斗，为我死那么多人，我不忍心 。  
I can't bear it if everyone fights for me and so many people die for me.
” 多么伟大的情操啊，就是华盛顿也比不上。  
"What lofty sentiment. Even Washington might have had trouble matching it.
古公亶父带着自己的家属走了，一直来到了陕西，百姓们也都追随他去了陕西。  
Gugong Danfu took his family and moved into Shaanxi, and the people followed him there.
在那里，周围的部落们知道古公亶父十分贤明，也都过来归附。  
There, the surrounding tribes knew that Gu Gong Danfu was very wise, and they all came to join him.
于是古公亶父修建城墙，建立官府，为民服务。  
So Gugong Danfu built walls, set up offices, and started governing for the people.
说起来，算是真正的公仆。  
In that sense, he was a real public servant.
古公亶父的大儿子叫太伯，二儿子叫虞仲，小儿子叫季历。  
The eldest son of Gu Gong Danfu was called Taibo, the second son was called Yu Zhong, and the younger son was called Ji Li.
季历的儿子叫姬昌，大家都看好他。  
Jili's son was Ji Chang, and everyone thought highly of him.
于是太伯和虞仲为了把王位让给季历，双双逃往南方，后来建立了吴国，成为吴国国君，这是后话。  
So Taibo and Yu Zhong fled south together in order to leave the succession to Jili; later they founded Wu and became its rulers, though that is a later story.
后来季历继位，季历之后是姬昌，也就是周文王。  
Later Ji Li succeeded to the throne, and after Ji Li was Ji Chang, also known as King Wen of Zhou.
周文王发明了“周易”，据专家考证，这是自古以来最伟大的预测学，现在世界上有许多“周易”研究会，夜以继日地想搞懂几成，在可预见的将来，这个研究还将继续下去。  
King Wen of Zhou was credited with creating the *Book of Changes*. According to the experts, it was the greatest system of divination（占卜） ever devised, and people around the world are still studying it day and night, with no end in sight.
没有“周易”，后世的帝王连坟地选在哪儿心里都没谱儿，也就没有今天看上去宏伟壮观的这陵那陵，甚至连故宫都可能盖得乱七 八糟。  
Without the "Book of Changes", the emperors of later generations would have no idea where to choose their tombs, and there would be no mausoleums as majestic as they look today. Even the Forbidden City might have been built in a mess.
所以，周文王的出现，是中国历史上的一件大事。  
So the appearance of King Wen of Zhou was a major event in Chinese history.
周文王可没有他爷爷那么慈悲，先后多次讨伐戎狄，让鬼子闻风丧胆。  
King Wen was nowhere near as gentle as his grandfather. He campaigned against the Rong and Di again and again and made them tremble at his name.
文王之后，武王灭了商朝，建立周朝。  
After King Wen came King Wu, who destroyed the Shang and founded the Zhou dynasty.
需要提醒的是，据传文王有九十九个儿子，后来分封各地，成了许多姓氏的祖先。  
It should be reminded that it is said that King Wen had ninety-nine sons, who were later entrusted to various places and became the ancestors of many surnames.
在汉族常见的姓氏中，大约有一半是出自文王后代。  
Among the common surnames of the Han people, about half are descendants of King Wen.
或者说，当今的中国人，几乎人人身上都流着周文王的血液。  
Put another way, almost everyone in China today could be said to carry the blood of King Wen of Zhou.
读到这里，大家最好保持恭敬的态度。  
By this point, a certain amount of reverence might be in order.
举个例子，当年国共两党打得不可开交，毛泽东、周恩来与蒋介石、宋子文（本姓韩）形同水火，其实若是大家坐下来叙叙旧讲讲历史，就会发现大家都是周文王的子孙，甚至周恩来和蒋介石还都是周公的后代，大家本是一家人。  
For example, the Kuomintang and the Communist Party were at war with each other. Mao Zedong and Zhou Enlai were at odds with Chiang Kai-shek and Song Ziwen (originally Han). In fact, if we sit down and talk about the past and talk about history, we will find that we are all descendants of King Wen of Zhou. Even Zhou Enlai and Chiang Kai-shek are descendants of Duke Zhou. We are all one family.
所以，经常讲讲历史，是有利于社会和谐的。  
So yes, talking about history from time to time really can help social harmony.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`定语从句`、`并列谓语`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `For example, the Kuomintang and the Communist Party were at war with each other. Mao Zedong and Zhou Enlai were at odds with Chiang Kai-shek and Song Ziwen (originally Han). In fact, if we sit down and talk about the past and talk about history, we will find that we are all descendants of King Wen of Zhou. Even Zhou Enlai and Chiang Kai-shek are descendants of Duke Zhou. We are all one family.`。<br>
难点拆解：先用条件从句交代前提，再落到主句结果；定语从句继续补充前面的对象。<br>
常用短语：
- `from that point on`：从那以后
- `could only`：只能
- `with its own hands`：亲手

### 周朝的统治
### How Zhou Ruled

当时的周朝是很强大的，没有人可以对抗。  
At the time, Zhou was immensely strong, and no one could stand against it.
建国之初，诸侯国一个个都很老实，谁不老实就办谁。  
In the early years after its founding, the feudal states all behaved themselves, and anyone who did not was dealt with.
那时候四面的民族也都很老实，东夷很快就被消灭了，成了姜太公的臣民，也就成了华夏族的一个部分。  
At that time, the ethnic groups all around were also very honest. Dongyi was quickly wiped out and became a subject of Grand Duke Jiang, and became a part of the Huaxia tribe.
一部分逃走的 ，大致就渡海去了朝鲜。  
Some of those who escaped probably crossed the sea to North Korea.
所以，中朝一家亲啊，本来就是一家。  
So if one wants to joke that China and Korea are one family, the point is that they really did begin as one family.
南蛮在楚国的打击下，很快也就服服帖帖了，天上九头鸟，地下湖北佬，大概就是那个时候说起来的。  
Under the attack of the Chu State, the Southern Barbarians soon became submissive. The nine-headed bird in the sky and the Hubei guy in the underground were probably mentioned at that time.
戎狄呢？  
Where is Rong Di?
说起来，话就长一点。  
Speaking of which, the story will be a little longer.
按照《史记》的记载，戎狄们原本是夏朝的遗族，也就是说他们跟夏朝原本是一个民族。  
According to the records in "Historical Records", the Rong Di people were originally the survivors of the Xia Dynasty, which means that they and the Xia Dynasty were originally the same nation.
说来说去，那也就是炎黄子孙了。  
After all, they are the descendants of Yan and Huang.
而戎狄是匈奴的祖先，匈奴又是之后的北方民族的祖先，这么说来，什么蒙古族、朝鲜族、女真族等等，实际上也 都是炎黄子孙，没什么可争议的。  
The Rong Di are the ancestors of the Xiongnu, and the Xiongnu are the ancestors of the subsequent northern peoples. In this way, the Mongols, Koreans, Jurchens, etc. are actually descendants of the Yanhuang people. There is no dispute.
周朝初期，戎狄是要进贡的，叫做“荒服”。  
In the early Zhou period, the Rong and Di were expected to bring tribute（进贡）; this was called “huangfu.”
后来到了周穆王的时候，有两个爱好：第一喜欢旅游，第二喜欢打仗。  
Later, when King Mu of Zhou Dynasty came, he had two hobbies: first, he liked traveling, and second, he liked fighting.
周穆王旅游的事迹不多说了，他喜欢西部旅游，是如今西部旅游的祖师爷。  
I won’t go into details about King Zhou Mu’s travel deeds. He loved traveling to the west and was the founder of today’s travel to the west.
他曾经西部旅游到天山，亲切会见了西王母。  
He once traveled west to the Tianshan Mountains and met the Queen Mother of the West cordially.
打仗的事情说一说，不知道为什么，他就是看戎狄不顺眼，非要打人家，谁劝也不行，于是就打了，戎狄一看大军打来，一个个都跑了，穆王只抓了四头白狼四头白鹿回来，大概这八样东西是戎狄的吉祥物，相当于我们的奥运福娃。  
Let’s talk about the war. I don’t know why, but he just didn’t like Rong Di and insisted on beating others. No one could persuade him, so he fought. When Rong Di saw the army coming, everyone ran away. King Mu only caught four white wolves and four white deer and came back. Probably these eight things are Rong Di’s mascots, equivalent to our Olympic Fuwa.
为什么咱有五个奥运吉祥物，来源应该就是戎狄这 里。  
Why do we have five Olympic mascots? The source should be Rong Di.
抢了人家吉祥物，人家当然不干了。  
Once he seized their sacred emblems, of course they were furious.
就像谁抢了咱那五个福娃，咱也不爽啊。  
If somebody stole our five Olympic mascots, we would not be pleased either.
《史记》记载，从那以后，“荒服不至” ，不进贡了。  
The *Records of the Grand Historian* says that from then on, "huangfu no longer came" and no tribute（进贡） was offered.
不仅不进贡了，还跟你打游击了。  
Not only did they stop paying tribute（进贡）, they also began harrying Zhou in guerrilla fashion.
到宣王的时候，又要打戎狄了，结果这一回连吉祥物也没抢回来，反而打了败仗。  
By the time of King Xuan, Zhou wanted to fight the Rong and Di again, but this time it not only failed to seize their sacred emblems, it also suffered defeat.
从那以后，戎狄就彻底不怕周朝了。  
From that point on, the Rong and Di no longer feared Zhou at all.
到了幽王的时候，戎狄就攻破了伟大首都，幽王也被杀了。  
By the time of King You, the Rong and Di had already broken into the grand capital, and King You himself was killed.
于是，平王迁都洛邑。  
That was why King Ping moved the capital to Luoyi.
说来说去，不该抢人家的吉祥物。  
In the end, he never should have stolen their sacred emblems.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`定语从句`、`并列句`、`并列谓语`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `According to the records in "Historical Records", the Rong Di people were originally the survivors of the Xia Dynasty, which means that they and the Xia Dynasty were originally the same nation.`。<br>
难点拆解：定语从句继续补充前面的对象。<br>
常用短语：
- `from that point on`：从那以后
- `could only`：只能
- `with its own hands`：亲手

### 周朝的人质
### Zhou and the Hostages

周平王迁都，虽说一时太平无事了，但是也失去了诸侯的尊重。  
When King Ping of Zhou moved the capital, he bought a little temporary（临时的） peace, but he also lost the respect of the feudal lords.
为什么？  
Why?
你堂堂一个周朝天子，竟然被小鬼子逼得迁都，弟兄们的颜面往哪里放？  
You are a majestic emperor of the Zhou Dynasty, but you were forced to move the capital by little devils. Where can you put the dignity of your brothers?
你要不行你吱一声啊，哥几个帮你把小鬼子办了不就行了。  
If you can't do it, just say something, and the brothers can help you deal with the little devil.
眼看大家都靠不住了，周平王这个时候只能依靠一个人了。  
Seeing that no one else could really be relied on, King Ping had only one person left to lean on.
谁？  
Who?
郑庄公。  
Duke Zhuang of Zheng.
郑国紧挨着洛邑，有实力，而且是诸侯里面最 近的亲戚，亲上加亲的亲戚，不仅是堂弟，还是表弟。  
Zheng Guo is close to Luoyi, has strength, and is the closest relative among the princes. He is a close relative, not only a cousin, but also a cousin.
可是，有一个问题。  
But there was one problem.
郑庄公有日子不来了，在家里对付叔段呢。  
Duke Zhuang had not been coming to court for some time, because he was at home dealing with Shu Duan.
周平王心里就有些不舒服，心说你是个中央领导人，不能总是拿着中央的工资干私活啊，你这不是占着茅坑不拉屎吗？  
King Ping was not pleased. He thought to himself: you are a central official, you cannot keep drawing a salary from the center while tending only to your private affairs. That is just holding a post and doing no work.
正在这个时候，虢公忌父从虢国来了，说起来，他是那个虢石父虢总理的儿子，当年虢总理给幽王出了烽火戏诸侯的主意，后来自己也幸福地被犬戎给剁了，也追认烈士了。  
Just then Ji Fu, the Duke of Guo, arrived from Guo. He was the son of Guo Shifu, the old Guo grandee who had once suggested the beacon-fire farce to King You and later ended up hacked to pieces by the Quanrong.
不过，他的儿子就不再担任中央领导人了。  
His son, however, no longer held any top position at the Zhou center.
平王跟虢公聊得挺投缘，平王有了一个想法：“老虢啊，你看老郑这么多年来担任总理职务，时间长了，有些懈怠了，平常也不来中央办公了。  
King Ping got along well with the Duke of Guo, and before long an idea came to him: "Old Guo, Lao Zheng has served as chief minister for many years. Lately he has grown a bit lax and rarely comes to court anymore.
我看，你就继承你爹的遗志，取代老郑，担任总理吧。  
Why don't you carry on your father's legacy, replace Lao Zheng, and take over as chief minister?"
” “这这这这这不行，绝对不行。  
"N-no, no, absolutely not.
”本来好好地说话，听平王这么说，吓得虢公说话都不利索了。  
The Duke of Guo had been speaking just fine, but King Ping's proposal frightened him so badly that he began to stammer.
“有什么关系？  
"What does it matter?
” “大王啊，老郑人家那是实力派啊，我要是夺了他的权，那他不仅怨恨我，也会怨恨大王啊，我这诸侯还想不想干啊？  
"Your Majesty, Lao Zheng is a real power. If I take away his authority, he will resent not only me but you as well. How could a small lord like me go on living?"
”虢公说得实在，自己是个小国，惹不起。  
The Duke of Guo spoke frankly. His state was too small to offend Zheng.
当天晚上，虢公急忙溜了。  
That very night, the Duke of Guo slipped away in a hurry.
他怕夜长梦多，万一平王非要自己取代郑庄公呢？  
He feared that if he stayed any longer, trouble might grow, and King Ping might really force him to replace Duke Zhuang.
还是跑了最放心。  
Running away felt safest.
世上没有不透风的墙，看上去越是坚固的墙，就越是透风。  
No wall in the world is airtight; the more solid a wall looks, the more likely it is to leak.
按理说，天子与臣子的对话应该是严格保密的。  
Logically speaking, the conversations between the emperor and his ministers should be strictly confidential.
可是，郑庄公在第二天就知道了整个事情的经过。  
However, Duke Zhuang of Zheng knew what had happened the next day.
毫无疑问，有人以快 马加急的方式向他汇报了工作。  
Clearly, someone had sent him a report by fast horse.
郑庄公是什么人？  
And what kind of man was Duke Zhuang of Zheng?
别忘了他是春秋第一位猛男啊。  
Do not forget that he was the first real tough man of the Spring and Autumn period.
于是，郑庄公去中央了。  
So Duke Zhuang went straight to the Zhou center.
他要干什么？  
What was he going there for?
辞职。  
To resign.
“大王，您看，我家里有些乱七八糟的事情，总是脱不开身，再说，能力上也有问题。  
"Your Majesty, my household is in a mess and I can never get away. Besides, I am not really equal to the job.
您就让我辞职吧，省得占着茅坑 不拉屎。  
Please let me resign, so I do not go on holding a post while doing no work.
”郑庄公很诚恳地向平王提出辞职。  
"Duke Zhuang of Zheng sincerely submitted his resignation to King Ping.
“兄弟，这怎么行？  
"Brother, how can this be done?
好久不见，正想你呢，咱们多聊聊。  
Long time no see. I was thinking of you. Let’s talk more.
”平王挽留。  
"King Ping persuaded him to stay.
自从虢公给吓跑之后，平王认真思考过，思考的结果是谁都能得罪，就是不能得罪郑庄公。  
After the Duke of Guo fled in alarm, King Ping thought it over carefully and concluded that he could afford to offend anyone except Duke Zhuang of Zheng.
他还庆幸呢，庆幸虢公没有听自己的。  
He was glad that the Duke of Guo didn't listen to him.
“大王，听说您连人选都看好了，据说是虢公是吧？  
"Your Majesty, I heard that you have chosen a candidate. It is said that he is the Duke of Guo, right?
我也觉得他很合适啊，他爹不就当过总理吗？  
I also think he is very suitable. Didn’t his father once serve as prime minister?
说实话，他的能力比 我强多了。  
To be honest, his ability is much better than mine.
”郑庄公说得超真诚。  
"Duke Zhuang of Zheng said it very sincerely.
平王有些慌了，他看不出来郑庄公是说的真心话还是在讽刺自己。  
King Ping began to panic a little. He could not tell whether Duke Zhuang was speaking sincerely or mocking him.
说实话，没人知道，甚至郑庄公本人也说不清，一方 面他有些愤怒，另一方面他还真不想干了。  
To be honest, no one knew, not even Duke Zhuang of Zheng himself. On the one hand, he was a little angry, but on the other hand, he really didn't want to do it anymore.
“嗨，我说呢，怎么好好地就要辞职，原来是误会，误会啊。  
"Ah, so that's it. I was wondering why you suddenly wanted to resign. It was all a misunderstanding.
我的意思是老弟不在的时候，虢公临时来帮个忙什么的，算是借调，老弟一回来，那他就主动让位。  
What I meant was only that while my brother was away, the Duke of Guo could help out for a while on temporary assignment. Once my brother came back, he would yield the post at once.
再说了，能力上，他怎么能跟老弟相比？  
Besides, in terms of ability, how can he compare with his brother?
” 问题是这样的，你越是挽留，他就越是想走；  
” The problem is this, the more you try to keep him, the more he wants to leave;
他越是想走，你就越是真想挽留。  
The more he wants to leave, the more you really want to keep him.
郑庄公咬死了一定不干了，说什么也没用。  
Duke Zhuang dug in his heels and insisted on quitting. Nothing anyone said made any difference.
平王一看不行啊，怎么办？  
King Ping could see this was going badly. What was he supposed to do now?
“兄弟，你看，四十多年了，郑国对中央的贡献那是巨大的，我叔还有兄弟你的贡献那是没人能比的。  
"Brother, you see, for more than 40 years, Zheng Guo's contribution to the central government has been huge. The contribution of my uncle and brother is unmatched by anyone.
如今出了这么一个小小的误会，也不能怪你不信任我。  
Now that there is such a small misunderstanding, I can't blame you for not trusting me.
这样吧，为了表达我的诚意，我让太子去郑国为人质，你看如何？  
How about this: to show my sincerity, I will send the crown prince to Zheng as a hostage（人质）. What do you think?
”平王这人，整个就是遗传了他姥爷的二愣子脾气，一急了什么馊主意都敢想，什么后果都不管。  
King Ping had inherited his grandfather's thick-headed temper. The moment he panicked, any bad idea could pop into his head, and he never bothered about the consequences.
你说交换人质这样的事情，原本是诸侯国之间的事情，大家级别相当的。  
Exchanging hostages was originally something feudal states did among themselves, because they were equals.
就算是诸侯国，关系亲近一点的，也都不玩这个。  
Even the vassal states with a closer relationship do not play this game.
现在天子要跟诸侯交换人质，而且动不动就派太子做人质，这不是太荒唐了吗？  
Now the Son of Heaven wanted to exchange hostages with a feudal lord, and was even offering the heir apparent at that. Was that not absurd（荒唐的）?
郑庄公是什么人？  
And what kind of man was Duke Zhuang of Zheng?
他的第一反应就是不行，第二反应是绝对不行。  
His first reaction was no, and his second reaction was absolutely no.
一来这样要挨骂，二来太子这样的人质那是人质吗？  
Firstly, you will be scolded for doing this, and secondly, is a hostage（人质） like the prince a hostage?
就算平王今后把自己给炒了，自己能把太子怎么样？  
Even if Prince Ping fires himself in the future, what can he do to the prince?
第三，太子去了郑国，那还不得供着？  
Thirdly, the prince has gone to Zheng, so why should he not be offered sacrifices?
供好了那算不错，万一有个 三长两短，怎么交待？  
It's not bad if you pay for it, but if something happens to you, how will you explain it?
“大王啊，这千万使不得。  
"My lord, this is absolutely impossible.
知道的说是大王主动提出的，不知道的说是我要挟大王。  
Those who know say it was the king who proposed it, and those who don’t know say it was me who blackmailed the king.
大王要我留下来，那我就留下来好了，这用太子做人质的事情就免了吧。  
If the king wants me to stay, then I will stay, and there will be no need to use the prince as a hostage（人质）.
”郑庄公不傻，这烫手的山芋绝对不接。  
"Mr. Zheng Zhuang is not stupid. He will never accept this hot potato.
其实到这里，平王是成功的，又表达自己的诚意了，郑庄公也让步了，如果见好就收，那就完美了。  
In fact, at this point, King Ping was successful and expressed his sincerity（诚意） again. Duke Zhuang of Zheng also gave in. It would be perfect if he just accepted the offer.
可是，平王那是个二愣子，当初迁都也就一拍脑袋。  
However, Prince Ping was a fool, and he just slapped his head on the head when he moved the capital.
如今又拍了脑袋，你越推辞，他越来劲。  
But now he acted on another reckless impulse: the more Duke Zhuang refused, the more determined he became.
“不行，其实我让太子去做人质还有别的意思，郑国现在是模范国家，国家治理得好，我也让太子去现场学习一下，算是留个学吧。  
"No, actually I have other reasons for asking the prince to be a hostage（人质）. Zheng State is now a model country and the country is well governed. I also asked the prince to go and learn on site. It can be regarded as a study abroad trip.
就这么定了，啊。  
That’s it, ah.
”这一回平王真是下定了决心，不容郑庄公再说什么。  
"This time King Ping was really determined and wouldn't let Duke Zhuang of Zheng say anything else.
说完，走了。  
After that, he left.
郑庄公现在是推无可推，暗中叫苦。  
Duke Zhuang of Zheng now has no choice but to complain secretly.
本来是来讨公道的，谁知道讨回去个烫手山芋。  
I came here to seek justice, but who knew that I would get back a hot potato?
怎么办？  
What to do?
回到总理官邸，恰好祭足跟着来了，两人一商量，现在只有一个办法：把自己的世子先弄过来，至少吧，算是交换人质 。  
When they got back to the chief minister's residence, Jizu came in as well. After the two men talked it over, they decided there was only one answer: bring Zheng's own heir over first so that, at the very least, it could count as an exchange of hostages（人质）.
就这么着，郑庄公世子姬忽来到周朝做人质，平王太子姬狐随后去了郑国当人质。  
So that was how Ji Hu, the heir of Duke Zhuang of Zheng, came to Zhou as a hostage（人质）, while King Ping's crown prince Ji Hu was then sent on to Zheng as a hostage.
周郑交质，这是一个标志，标志着周朝的权威下了一个台阶，他们已经自己给自己降格了。  
This exchange of hostages between Zhou and Zheng was a marker: it showed that Zhou authority（权威） had dropped another step, and that the royal house had lowered its own rank with its own hands.
消息传出去，天下诸侯对周朝更加不放在眼里。  
Once the news spread, the feudal lords held the Zhou house in even less regard（重视；看待）.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`时间状语从句`、`定语从句`、`并列谓语`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `What I mean is that when my brother is away, the Duke of Guo comes to do a favor or something temporarily, which is regarded as a secondment. As soon as my brother comes back, he will take the initiative to give up his position.`。<br>
难点拆解：时间从句先给背景，再推进主句动作；定语从句继续补充前面的对象。<br>
常用短语：
- `from that point on`：从那以后
- `could only`：只能
- `with its own hands`：亲手

### 大王的麦子被抢了
### The King's Wheat Was Stolen

周平王在位五十一年（前720年）的时候，终于崩了。  
In the fifty-first year of his reign, in 720 BCE, King Ping of Zhou finally died.
总理郑庄公和人大委员长周公黑肩共同迎请太子狐登基，太子狐从郑国回来，那时候他也快五十了，身体还不太好，在平王灵前痛哭，结果这一哭就昏过去了，这一昏过去就没有醒过来，也崩了。  
Prime Minister Duke Zhuang of Zheng and Chairman of the National People's Congress Zhou Gong Heijian jointly welcomed Prince Hu to the throne. Prince Hu came back from Zheng State. He was almost fifty at that time and was not in good health. He cried bitterly in front of King Ping's spirit. As a result, he fainted after crying. After passing out, he did not wake up and collapsed.
于是，太子狐的儿子姬林继位，就是周桓王。  
So Ji Lin, the son of Crown Prince Hu, took the throne and became King Huan of Zhou.
周桓王很年轻，就因为自己老爹被派去郑国做人质这件事情，他对郑庄公一向没有好感。  
King Huan was young, and because his own father had once been sent to Zheng as a hostage（人质）, he had never liked Duke Zhuang.
他决定炒了他，炒他之前，桓王找来周公商量，周公劝他：“忍忍吧，郑侯没犯什么错，对国家也还尽力。  
He decided to fire him. Before firing him, King Huan asked Duke Zhou to discuss it. Duke Zhou advised him: "Be patient, Marquis Zheng has done nothing wrong, and he has done his best for the country.
” 可是桓王不愿意忍了，他毕竟还年轻。  
"But King Huan didn't want to endure it anymore. He was still young after all.
第二天早朝，桓王也没客气，上来就宣布：“郑侯年岁已大，辈分又高，本王不忍心让他老人家天天来上班，回家养老 去吧。  
In the morning of the next day, King Huan was not polite and announced: "Zheng Hou is old and senior. I can't bear to let him come to work every day, so he can go home and take care of himself."
”“多谢大王，老臣早有这样想法。  
"Thank you, Your Majesty. I have had this idea for a long time."
”郑庄公谢了恩，回到总理官邸，要紧的东西收拾一遍，当天就回郑国了。  
"Duke Zhuang of Zheng thanked him, returned to the Prime Minister's Residence, packed up his important things, and returned to Zheng Guo that same day.
其实呢，庄公也未必就愿意在这里当中央领导，退休回家也没什么问题。  
In fact, Duke Zhuang may not be willing to be a central leader here, and there is no problem in retiring and going home.
关键是，你不要这样生硬地让人家走，那太没面子。  
The real problem was not retirement itself, but being pushed out so bluntly. It was humiliating（丢脸的）.
譬如桓王给庄公搞个生日晚会，把侯爵晋级为公爵，反正也不要成本，这个时候好好跟人家说，装孙子就装孙子，何况本来也是孙子。  
For example, if King Huan holds a birthday party for Duke Zhuang and promotes a marquis to a duke, it will cost nothing anyway. At this time, he should tell others that if he pretends to be a grandson, he should pretend to be a grandson, not to mention that he is also a grandson.
那样的话，郑庄公走得也有面子，今后有什么事情还能照应你，多好？  
In that case, Duke Zhuang of Zheng will be able to leave with dignity, and he will be able to take care of you if anything happens in the future. How great?
周朝为什么后来不行呢？  
Why didn’t the Zhou Dynasty succeed later?
跟这些混账天子太有关系了。  
It has so much to do with these bastard emperors.
郑庄公灰溜溜回了郑国，大伙儿一看就知道心情不好，一问，原来是被桓王这孙子给赶回来的，大伙儿就急了。  
Duke Zhuang of Zheng returned to the state of Zheng in despair. Everyone knew at first sight that he was in a bad mood. When they asked, they found out that it was the grandson of King Huan who had driven him back. Everyone became anxious.
“他娘的，这不是忘恩负义吗？  
“Damn it, isn’t this ungrateful?
这不是欺负老实人吗？  
Isn’t this bullying an honest person?
主公，咱们立即出兵，攻打洛邑，废了那个孙子。  
My lord, let us send troops at once to strike Luoyi and depose that wretch.
”大夫高渠弥 第一个不干了，就要出兵。  
Grandee Gao Qumi was the first to lose his temper and demand troops.
“不行，攻打周王，那不是造反吗？  
"No, attacking the King of Zhou is not rebellion（造反）, right?
”颍考叔不同意。  
Ying Kaoshu disagreed.
是啊，地方打中央，那于理不通啊。  
Yes, it makes no sense for the local government to attack the central government.
庄公基本没搭理他们，这样考智商的问题，只有祭足才有办法。  
Duke Zhuang more or less ignored them. Questions like this required brains, and only Jizu had them.
“攻打周王，那肯定不行；  
Attacking the Zhou king was certainly out of the question;
可是就这么忍了，那心情又不爽。  
but swallowing the insult like this felt awful.
我看，不如出兵到温，就说今年歉收，朝他借粮。  
“Why not send troops to Wen and say that this year’s harvest was poor, so we have come to borrow grain?”
如果不借，那就是不够意思；  
If they refused, it meant they were not being decent;
如果借，咱们再狮子大开口，让他们难受。  
if they agreed, we could ask for an outrageously large amount and make them suffer.
”果然，祭足的主意就是好，温是周朝的自留地，不 打洛邑而在温闹事，意思就是你让我不爽，我也让你不爽。  
Sure enough, Jizu's plan was a good one. Wen was Zhou's private plot of land. If Zheng did not strike Luoyi itself but caused trouble at Wen instead, the message was simple: if you make me miserable, I will make you miserable too.
主意就这么定了，祭足亲自率军到了温，之后向温城大夫开口借粮。  
The plan was settled. Jizu personally led troops to Wen and asked the magistrate there to lend grain.
温城大夫也不是傻瓜，再加上事先有内参一类的东西过来，说是要提防郑国报复，因此温城大夫既不借粮，也不开城门 ，你爱干什么干什么，我就当没看见。  
The magistrate of Wen was no fool. On top of that, he had already received a warning to beware of Zheng retaliation, so he neither lent grain nor opened the gates. Do whatever you like, he thought. I will pretend not to see it.
祭足一看，一切都在意料之中。  
Jizu took one look and saw that everything was going exactly as expected.
“不借？  
"Don't borrow it?
麦子就在地里，你不借，我们自己割。  
The wheat is in the field. If you don't borrow it, we will cut it ourselves.
”祭足下令，来之前就给每个士兵发了镰刀，这时候正好用上。  
Jizu had ordered every soldier to be given a sickle before they set out, and now the time had come to use them.
正是麦熟季节，郑国军队就将温的麦子都给割了。  
It was exactly wheat season, so Zheng’s troops harvested all the wheat around Wen.
怎么割法？  
And how did they do it?
只割麦穗，不割麦杆，否则运不了。  
They cut only the ears of grain and left the stalks behind; otherwise it would have been too hard to carry away.
温城大夫在城楼上干 瞪眼看着，很生气，但是打不过。  
The magistrate of Wen could only glare down from the city wall, furious but unable to fight back.
割完麦子，郑军回去了。  
After harvesting the wheat, Zheng Jun returned.
再看麦田里，麦子还那么高，就是没有头了，你这不气人吗？  
Afterward the fields still stood tall, only headless. It was enough to make anyone furious.
又过了三个月，祭足照方拿药，把成周的早稻给割走了。  
Three months later, Jizu used the same prescription again and cut away the early rice around Chengzhou.
抢了麦子，抢了稻子，周桓王不知道？  
Did King Huan of Zhou not know that Zheng had carried off both wheat and rice?
当然知道。  
Of course he knew.
“我要讨伐郑国。  
I am going to punish Zheng.
”桓王大怒。  
"King Huan was furious.
“大王，算了，不就是点麦子吗？  
Your Majesty, let it go. Isn't it only a little wheat?
就当喂狗了吧。  
Just think of it as dog feed.
一来，为点麦子就打仗，事情太小，让人家笑话；  
First, to go to war over a little wheat is too petty and would make people laugh;
二来，割麦子的是郑国的边防军，说不定老郑根本就不知道；  
“Second, it was Zheng’s frontier（边境的） troops who cut the wheat. Old Zheng may not even know about it.”
三呢，咱的军队常年不打仗，哪里是郑国的对手？  
Third, our army never fights. How could it possibly match Zheng?
”周公黑肩第一个出来反 对。  
The Duke of Zhou, Hei Jian, was the first to object.
桓王想想，也有道理，再问大家，没人赞成出兵。  
King Huan thought about it, and it made sense. When he asked everyone again, no one agreed to send troops.
这下没办法了，出兵的事情就算没说过，桓王让周公给各地下了一道诏书，基本内容就是：有贼，看好自己的麦子。  
There was nothing to be done. The talk of sending troops was quietly dropped, and King Huan had the Duke of Zhou send out an order to the various places that boiled down to this: thieves are about, watch your wheat.
家门口的麦子被抢，却只能忍气吞声。  
His wheat was stolen right from his doorstep, yet he could only swallow the insult.
“唉。  
"Alas.
”天下诸侯都在为周王室叹气。  
All the feudal lords of the realm could only sigh for the Zhou royal house.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`时间状语从句`、`定语从句`、`并列谓语`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `Duke Zhuang of Zheng returned to the state of Zheng in despair. Everyone knew at first sight that he was in a bad mood. When they asked, they found out that it was the grandson of King Huan who had driven him back. Everyone became anxious.`。<br>
难点拆解：时间从句先给背景，再推进主句动作；定语从句继续补充前面的对象。<br>
常用短语：
- `from that point on`：从那以后
- `could only`：只能
- `with its own hands`：亲手

## 第十二章 冒充中央军
## Chapter 12: Passing Themselves Off as the Central Army

无数历史早就告诉我们。  
History has told us time and again  
鲜血凝成的交情。  
that friendships forged in blood  
往往也靠不住。  
can still be unreliable（靠不住的）.  
所谓同志加兄弟。  
That so-called mix of comradeship and brotherhood  
很多时候就是拿来忽悠人的。  
is often just a way to string people along.  
用得着你时。  
When people need you,  
你是兄弟。  
you are a brother.  
用不着你时。  
When they do not,  
你就成了隔壁谁家的远房亲戚。  
you turn into some distant nobody from next door.  
历史有个好处。  
History does have one virtue.  
看多了之后。  
After you have read enough of it,  
你对现实会坦然一点。  
you become a little calmer about real life.  
也更容易释然。  
And you find it easier to let things go.  
郑国和周朝的关系。  
The bond between Zheng and Zhou  
并不只是普通的中央和地方关系。  
went far beyond an ordinary relation between center and local state.  
他们真是兄弟。  
They really were kin.  
也确实同过患难。  
They had genuinely shared hardship.  
没有周朝。  
Without Zhou,  
就没有郑国。  
there would have been no Zheng.  
没有郑国。  
And without Zheng,  
周朝也会是另一副模样。  
Zhou itself would have looked very different.  
可即便如此。  
Even so,  
该忽悠的时候还是照样忽悠。  
when the time came to bluff one another, they still did it without hesitation.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`时间状语从句`、`条件状语从句`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `When people need you, you are a brother; when they do not, you turn into some distant nobody from next door`。<br>
难点拆解：这里两组 `when ...` 都是时间状语从句，用来对比“需要”和“不需要”两种处境；`Without Zhou ... / without Zheng ...` 又是条件色彩很强的省略表达；整段靠并列推进，把讽刺意味一层层推出来。<br>
常用短语：
- `forged in blood`：用鲜血凝成的
- `string people along`：忽悠人，吊着人
- `let things go`：释然，放下

### 郑庄公有点惭愧
### Duke Zhuang Feels a Little Ashamed

周朝的反应。  
Zhou's reaction  
让郑庄公很意外。  
took Duke Zhuang by surprise.  
他原本担心。  
He had originally feared  
中央军会来讨伐。  
that the royal army would come to punish him.  
就算不出兵。  
Even if troops did not come,  
至少也该有个名义上的处分。  
there should at least have been some formal punishment.  
所以他早就让祭足。  
So he had already asked Jizu  
草拟了一份情况说明。  
to draft an explanatory report,  
又草拟了一份检查。  
and also a written self-criticism.  
就等着看。  
He was simply waiting to see  
到时该交哪一份。  
which one would actually be needed.  
可谁知。  
But to his surprise,  
周朝竟一点动静也没有。  
Zhou made no move at all.  
好像丢麦子的。  
It was as if the stolen wheat  
不是他们自己。  
had not belonged to them,  
而是隔壁老张。  
but to some old neighbor next door.  
“宽容啊。”  
"How magnanimous（宽宏的）,"  
郑庄公忍不住感叹。  
Duke Zhuang could not help sighing.  
中央到底是中央。  
The center was the center after all.  
小错也不追究。  
It let even a small offense pass.  
中央不追究。  
Once the center chose not to pursue the matter,  
地方这边反倒开始反省。  
the local side began reflecting on itself instead.  
人往往就是这样。  
People are often like that.  
你来硬的。  
Come at them hard,  
他就跟你顶着干。  
and they push right back.  
你来点怀柔。  
Show a little restraint,  
再来点宽容。  
and a little leniency,  
他反而会不好意思。  
and they begin to feel ashamed.  
“老祭啊，看来咱们真做错了。”  
"Old Jizu, it seems we really were in the wrong."  
郑庄公这样说。  
That was what Duke Zhuang said.  
这还是他第一次。  
It was also the first time  
批评祭足。  
he had ever criticized Jizu.  
“主公，知错能改，善莫大焉。”  
"My lord, nothing is better than knowing one's fault and correcting it."  
“既然知道错了。”  
"Since we know it now,  
那就上门道歉。”  
we should go apologize in person."  
“道歉本身。”  
"An apology itself  
也是沟通的好办法。”  
is also a very good way to reopen communication."  
祭足总是这样。  
That was Jizu's talent.  
坏事到了他手里。  
Once trouble reached his hands,  
也能拐成好事。  
he could often twist it into something useful.  
郑庄公一听。  
Duke Zhuang liked the idea at once.  
自己本来也有点怀念。  
He had already been feeling a little nostalgic  
伟大首都的日子。  
for his days in the grand capital.  
于是决定亲自去洛邑。  
So he decided to go to Luoyi himself,  
朝见周桓王。  
pay court to King Huan of Zhou,  
顺便示个好。  
and show a little goodwill.  
当然。  
And of course,  
首都的葱油饼。  
the capital's scallion pancakes  
也很让人惦记。  
were worth missing too.  
可就在启程前。  
But just before he set out,  
又出了一件事。  
something else happened.  
宋国公子冯。  
Prince Feng of Song  
跑来避难了。  
came seeking refuge（避难）.  
而且是一身是血地跑来的。  
And he arrived covered in blood.  
原来郑庄公先前。  
Earlier Duke Zhuang had let him  
让他暂住长葛。  
stay for a while at Changge.  
谁知宋军突然闪击。  
Then Song troops struck in a lightning attack,  
一举拿下了长葛。  
and took Changge at one stroke.  
公子冯。  
Prince Feng  
是死里逃生才回来的。  
got back only by escaping death.  
郑庄公这回真火了。  
This time Duke Zhuang was genuinely enraged.  
当初州吁搞五国联军。  
Back when Zhouxu raised his five-state coalition,  
宋国就是最卖命的那个。  
Song had already been the most eager participant.  
如今又占我城池。  
Now it had taken Zheng territory again.  
这不是骑到头上撒野吗？  
That was practically climbing onto his head and making itself at home.  
没错。  
Fine,  
公子冯是宋国的人。  
Prince Feng belonged to Song.  
可人在郑国住着。  
But he had been living in Zheng.  
你凭什么就闯过来抓人？  
What right did Song have to barge in and seize him?  
真把自己当国际警察了？  
Did it really take itself for the world's policeman?  
“老祭，我要办宋国。”  
"Old Jizu, I am going after Song."  
郑庄公是真怒了。  
Duke Zhuang meant it.  
“你给我出个主意。”  
"Give me a good plan."  
祭足听了却笑了。  
Jizu, however, smiled.  
他一向讲效率。  
He had always believed in efficiency.  
出门办事。  
If you were going out to handle one matter,  
最好顺手多办几件。  
you might as well settle several.  
“主公啊。”  
"My lord,  
这下去首都。”  
this trip to the capital  
又多了一件事可办。”  
has now picked up one more purpose."  
庄公心里想。  
Duke Zhuang thought to himself  
我本想先办宋国。  
that he had wanted to deal with Song first  
再去洛邑。  
and only then go to Luoyi.  
怎么又被老祭抢了话头。  
How had Jizu stolen the line again?  
祭足接着解释。  
Jizu went on to explain.  
宋国不好打。  
Song was not easy to fight.  
先说爵位。  
For one thing, in rank  
郑是侯。  
Zheng was only a marquisate,  
宋却是公。  
while Song was a duchy.  
再说经验。  
Then there was experience.  
宋国这些年。  
Song had fought year after year.  
战绩虽然不漂亮。  
Its record was not impressive,  
可打仗经验不少。  
but it had plenty of battlefield experience.  
依祭足看。  
In Jizu's view,  
这事必须打中央旗号。  
this had to be done under the banner of the center.  
借周王之名。  
Invoke the Zhou king's name,  
号召各路诸侯一起讨伐。  
and summon the lords to a joint campaign.  
这样名义正。  
Then the cause would look legitimate,  
人也会多。  
and the numbers would look stronger.  
到那时。  
Only then  
办宋国才容易。  
would dealing with Song become easy.  
祭足想事一向周到。  
Jizu always thought things through thoroughly.  
至于他为什么这么聪明。  
As for why he was so clever,  
作者给出的答案很简单。  
the author gives a very simple answer.  
经常洗脚。  
Frequent footbaths, apparently.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`宾语从句`、`条件状语从句`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `He had originally feared that the royal army would come to punish him`。<br>
难点拆解：`that the royal army would come ...` 是宾语从句，补出“担心”的具体内容；中间 `Even if troops did not come` 带条件色彩，说明“就算不来兵，也该有处分”；后面多组短句并列推进，把郑庄公从惭愧转到愤怒的情绪线拉得很清楚。<br>
常用短语：
- `take someone by surprise`：让某人意外
- `pursue the matter`：追究此事
- `at one stroke`：一下子，一举
- `under the banner of`：打着……旗号

### 热脸贴上冷屁股
### A Warm Face Meets a Cold Shoulder

郑庄公和祭足。  
Duke Zhuang and Jizu  
一起去了洛邑。  
went together to Luoyi.  
这一趟有两件事。  
They had two purposes on this trip.  
第一。  
First,  
向周桓王赔个不是。  
they meant to apologize to King Huan of Zhou.  
第二。  
Second,  
希望桓王下旨。  
they wanted him to issue an edict  
让郑国率领联军讨伐宋国。  
authorizing Zheng to lead a coalition against Song.  
两人带足了礼物。  
They brought ample gifts,  
兴冲冲去了首都。  
and set off for the capital in high spirits.  
周桓王一听说郑庄公来了。  
The moment King Huan heard Duke Zhuang had come,  
先是吃了一惊。  
he was startled.  
他心里想。  
He thought to himself,  
这个不要脸的。  
what was that shameless fellow  
跑来干什么？  
doing here?  
可人既然到了。  
But once the man had arrived,  
总还是得见。  
there was no choice but to receive him.  
于是郑庄公入朝。  
So Duke Zhuang was admitted to court.  
爷孙两个见了面。  
When the two kinsmen met,  
都是皮笑肉不笑。  
both smiled without warmth.  
庄公献上礼物。  
Duke Zhuang presented the gifts.  
桓王一眼没看。  
King Huan did not even glance at them.  
只叫人收下。  
He merely had them taken away.  
寒暄过后。  
Once the formal greetings were over,  
桓王先开口。  
King Huan spoke first.  
“郑国今年收成如何？”  
"How was Zheng's harvest this year?"  
“托大王的福。”  
"Thanks to Your Majesty's blessing,  
天气好，收成也好。”  
the weather was good, and so was the harvest."  
桓王冷笑一下。  
King Huan gave a cold smile.  
“那就好。”  
"Good.  
温的麦子。  
Then perhaps Wen's wheat  
和成周的早稻。  
and Chengzhou's early rice  
这回总能留着自己吃了。”  
can stay on our own table this time."  
话说到这里。  
Once the conversation reached that point,  
郑庄公就知道。  
Duke Zhuang knew  
这一趟算是白来了。  
the trip had been wasted.  
再说下去。  
If he stayed any longer,  
非翻脸不可。  
they would surely fall out completely.  
于是他随便找了个。  
So he made up some excuse,  
肚子疼之类的借口。  
something about stomach pain,  
便退了出来。  
and withdrew.  
回到国宾馆。  
Back at the guesthouse,  
祭足连忙来问。  
Jizu hurried over and asked,  
“主公，怎么样？”  
"My lord, how did it go?"  
“白来了。”  
"For nothing."  
庄公把经过一说。  
Duke Zhuang told him everything.  
桓王毫无善意。  
King Huan had shown no goodwill at all.  
别说让他下旨打宋。  
Forget getting an edict against Song,  
连缓和关系都没戏。  
even mending relations was hopeless.  
祭足听完。  
After hearing that,  
也没有别的办法。  
Jizu had no better answer.  
按理说。  
By custom,  
到了晚上。  
when evening came,  
桓王本该赐宴。  
King Huan should have hosted a banquet.  
吃点野味。  
Game on the table,  
看看歌舞。  
songs and dances for entertainment,  
场面总该摆出来。  
at least the formalities should have been there.  
可桓王连这个都省了。  
But King Huan did not even bother with that.  
没办法。  
So with nothing else to do,  
祭足只好带着庄公。  
Jizu simply took Duke Zhuang  
出去找地方洗脚。  
out to find a footbath.  
第二天。  
The next day,  
桓王派人送来十车谷子。  
King Huan sent over ten cartloads of grain.  
说是给郑国。  
He said it was for Zheng  
备战备荒用的。  
to prepare for war and famine,  
免得以后。  
so that next time  
又跑这么远来割麦子。  
it would not have to travel so far to harvest wheat again.  
郑庄公听得哭笑不得。  
Duke Zhuang did not know whether to laugh or curse.  
桓王这一下。  
This time  
实在太损了。  
King Huan had gone too far.  
可这是人家的地盘。  
Yet this was the king's own territory.  
给你东西。  
If he gave you something,  
你还不能不接。  
you could not refuse it.  
更不能扔下不带走。  
Nor could you simply leave it behind.  
可堂堂一个诸侯。  
But for a feudal lord,  
又是前任总理。  
and a former chief minister at that,  
按辈分还是天子的叔爷。  
and in kinship terms even the king's elder grand-uncle,  
赶着十车谷子回家。  
to drive home ten carts of grain  
丢不丢人？  
was that not humiliating（丢脸的）?  
正发愁时。  
While he was still worrying over it,  
周公派人来了。  
a messenger from the Duke of Zhou arrived.  
原来周公也知道。  
The Duke of Zhou had also heard  
桓王这回太羞辱人。  
how badly King Huan had humiliated him.  
不招待也就算了。  
No hospitality was one thing.  
还硬塞十车谷子。  
Ten carts of grain were another.  
所以周公自己。  
So he personally sent  
又送了两车绸缎来补救。  
two carts of silk to make amends.  
祭足一看见绸缎。  
The moment Jizu saw the silk,  
立刻就乐了。  
his face lit up.  
庄公不懂。  
Duke Zhuang did not understand.  
“你笑什么？”  
"What are you grinning at?"  
“这绸缎比谷子值钱？”  
"Is silk really worth more than grain?"  
在庄公看来。  
As Duke Zhuang saw it,  
天下人都知道。  
everyone knew  
自己是来京城的。  
he had come to the capital.  
就算添上两车绸缎。  
Two extra carts of silk  
也盖不住那十车谷子的羞辱。  
could never cover the insult of ten carts of grain.  
祭足却说。  
Jizu only said,  
“主公先去洗脚。”  
"My lord, go wash your feet first.  
看我怎么处置。”  
Leave this to me."  
他说完就退了出去。  
With that, he stepped away.  
等庄公洗完脚出来。  
When Duke Zhuang came out after his footbath,  
一看之下大吃一惊。  
he was stunned.  
为什么？  
Why?  
谷子不见了。  
The grain was gone.  
眼前只剩下。  
All that remained in sight  
十车绸缎。  
were ten carts of silk.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`时间状语从句`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `If he stayed any longer, they would surely fall out completely`。<br>
难点拆解：这里的 `If ...` 是条件状语从句，说明“再说下去”的后果；`The moment ...` 和 `When ...` 都是时间状语从句，用来带出情绪变化；中间多组短句并列，保持了这段“上门示好却吃冷脸”的节奏。<br>
常用短语：
- `in high spirits`：兴冲冲地
- `have no choice but to`：别无选择，只能
- `make amends`：补救，赔补
- `light up`：一下高兴起来


### 冒充中央军
### Passing Themselves Off as the Central Army

祭足总有办法。  
Jizu always had a way out.  
这一点得记住。  
That is the one thing worth remembering.  
他总能想出招。  
He could always come up with a trick.  
可两车绸缎。  
But how had two carts of silk  
变成十车？  
turned into ten?  
那时还没有魔术。  
There were no magic tricks（魔术） in those days,  
祭足也不是戏法师。  
and Jizu was no conjurer（变戏法的人）.  
他只是脑子快。  
He was simply quicker-witted（机敏的） than most.  

祭足把周公送的两车绸缎。  
Jizu took the two carts of silk the Duke of Zhou had sent  
全盖在十车谷子上。  
and spread it over the ten carts of grain.  
于是十车谷子。  
Just like that, the ten grain carts  
看着就成了十车绸缎。  
looked like ten carts of silk.  
办法就是这么简单。  
The trick was that simple.  

“这有什么用？”  
"What good does that do?"  
“底下不还是谷子？”  
"Isn't it still grain underneath?"  
庄公一时没转过弯。  
Duke Zhuang did not quite see it at first.  
祭足解释说。  
Jizu explained that  
只要赶着这些车上路。  
as long as they drove those wagons home,  
谁知道下面盖的是谷子？  
who would know there was grain under the silk?  
一路上只要对外说。  
All they had to do was tell people on the road  
周王待郑国极厚。  
that the Zhou king had treated Zheng very generously（慷慨地）,  
赏了十车绸缎。  
rewarding it with ten carts of silk,  
又命郑国组织联军。  
and had ordered Zheng to organize a coalition（联军）  
去讨伐宋国。  
to punish Song.  

你若赶着十车谷子回国。  
If you rolled home with ten carts of grain  
还说周王命你当统帅。  
and claimed the king had made you commander,  
傻子都不会信。  
only a fool would believe it.  
可十车绸缎就不一样了。  
But ten carts of silk were another matter.  
那看着就像真的。  
That at least looked convincing.  
庄公一听。  
The moment Duke Zhuang heard this,  
也觉得这招绝妙。  
he knew it was brilliant.  
咱不是真中央军。  
Zheng was not the real central army,  
装一回总可以。  
but pretending once was certainly possible.  

第二天。  
The next day,  
郑国使团离开洛邑。  
the Zheng mission left Luoyi.  
一路招摇。  
All the way home it swaggered（招摇而行） about,  
一边走一边放话。  
spreading the story as it went.  
“看见没有？  
"See these?  
周王赏了十车绸缎。  
The king rewarded us with ten carts of silk,  
还命我们联络诸侯。  
and ordered us to rally the lords  
一起讨伐宋国。”  
to punish Song together."  
消息很快传开。  
The news spread quickly across the realm.  
那时没有电报。  
There were no telegraphs then,  
更没有互联网。  
much less the internet.  
消息全靠人传。  
News moved entirely by word of mouth（口口相传）.  
一传十。  
One person told ten,  
十传百。  
and ten told a hundred.  
大家都信了。  
Everyone believed it.  
谁也没想到。  
No one imagined  
这竟然是假的。  
that it was a lie.  

庄公回到郑国以后。  
After Duke Zhuang returned to Zheng,  
立刻派使者去各国。  
he immediately sent envoys（使者） to the various states  
假传周桓王的命令。  
carrying forged（伪造的） orders in King Huan's name.  
有人问。  
If anyone asked  
没有王家的印怎么办。  
what to do without a royal seal,  
办法也简单。  
the answer was simple.  
找个萝卜。  
Find a turnip,  
自己刻一个。  
and carve one yourself.  

于是齐国和鲁国。  
And so Qi and Lu,  
这两个大国都答应出兵。  
both major powers, agreed to send troops.  
其实它们跟宋国。  
In fact, their relations with Song  
本来都还不错。  
had been fairly decent.  
之前还装模作样。  
They had even gone through the motions（装样子）  
签过互不侵犯的约。  
of signing non-aggression（互不侵犯） pacts.  
可郑国又答应。  
But Zheng promised them  
打下来的城归他们。  
that any captured cities would be theirs.  
于是他们就心动了。  
That was enough to tempt（诱使） them.  

国际事务里。  
In international affairs,  
没有永远的朋友。  
there are no eternal（永远的） friends,  
只有永远的利益。  
only eternal interests（利益）.  
春秋这时候。  
The Spring and Autumn world  
已经把这个道理。  
had already made that lesson  
讲得很明白。  
perfectly clear.  

至于假借天子名义。  
As for borrowing the Son of Heaven's name  
去号令诸侯。  
to command the lords,  
这招正是郑庄公和祭足。  
that trick was exactly what Duke Zhuang and Jizu  
一起想出来的。  
worked out together.  
后世一代代。  
Later generations  
又把它一路传了下去。  
would pass it down（传下去） again and again.  
后来联军在郑庄公主导下。  
Later, under Duke Zhuang's leadership（领导）, the coalition  
把宋国打得很惨。  
would hit Song hard.  
不过那是后话。  
But that is a later story.  
后面再讲。  
We will come to it in due course.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `If you rolled home with ten carts of grain and claimed the king had made you commander, only a fool would believe it.`。<br>
难点拆解：`If ...` 先交代这套说辞为什么站不住；`claimed the king had made you commander` 里有宾语从句；后面连续并列短句，把“造势、传话、拉盟友”的节奏一路推快。<br>
常用短语：
- `word of mouth`：口口相传
- `go through the motions`：装样子，走过场
- `tempt someone`：诱使某人
- `pass down`：传下去

### 天子要出征
### The Son of Heaven Marches to War

郑国冒充中央军。  
After Zheng passed itself off as the central army  
去打了宋国。  
and used that banner to attack Song,  
天下也就明白了。  
the whole realm realized  
那道王命是假的。  
that the royal order had been fake.  
齐国和鲁国。  
Qi and Lu,  
拿了好处还卖乖。  
after cashing in（捞到好处）, still played innocent.  
都说自己也受了骗。  
Both claimed they too had been deceived（欺骗）.  
周桓王气得不轻。  
King Huan was furious.  
张口就骂郑庄公是骗子。  
He opened his mouth and called Duke Zhuang a fraud（骗子）.  
可惜那时。  
A pity there were no press conferences then.  
不然他一定会开一个。  
Otherwise he would surely have held one  
专门谴责郑国。  
just to denounce（谴责） Zheng.  

一转眼。  
In the blink of an eye,  
又过去了五年。  
another five years went by.  
这五年里。  
During those five years,  
郑庄公再没去过洛邑。  
Duke Zhuang never went to Luoyi again.  
上回去一趟。  
The last time he went,  
只换回十车谷子。  
he came back with ten carts of grain,  
跟打发叫花子差不多。  
as if he had been paid off like a beggar.  
谁还愿意再去？  
Why would he willingly go again?  

偏偏他不去了。  
Yet once he stopped going,  
桓王反倒开始惦记。  
King Huan started brooding over it.  
“五年不来朝见了。  
"Five years without coming to court.  
整整五年。  
A full five years.  
孩子都能生一窝了。  
Enough time to raise a whole brood of children.  
郑国离得这么近。  
Zheng is so close,  
竟然还不来朝拜。  
and still it does not come to pay respects.  
这不是目无中央吗？”  
Is that not blatant contempt（轻视） for the center?"  
桓王嘴上官腔十足。  
King Huan dressed the complaint in full official language,  
心里却气得很。  
but inwardly he was seething（愤怒的）.  

天子一发怒会怎样？  
What happens when the Son of Heaven gets angry?  
后来的秦始皇会说。  
Qin Shi Huang would later put it this way:  
伏尸遍野。  
corpses would lie everywhere,  
血流千里。  
and blood would run for a thousand li.  
说白了。  
Put plainly,  
就是打仗。  
it meant war.  
于是桓王决定。  
So King Huan decided  
讨伐郑国。  
to punish Zheng.  
连当年假冒中央军的旧账。  
He meant to settle at the same time  
也一起算了。  
the old score over the fake central army.  

周公一听要打。  
As soon as the Duke of Zhou heard this,  
马上来劝。  
he came at once to urge caution.  
桓王不听。  
King Huan would not listen.  
后来接替郑庄公旧职的虢公。  
Then the Duke of Guo, who had taken Duke Zhuang's former post,  
也来劝。  
tried to stop him as well.  
桓王还是不听。  
King Huan still refused to listen.  
最高统治者。  
Once the highest ruler  
既然铁了心要打。  
had set his heart on（铁了心） war,  
那也只能打了。  
war it would be.  

按理说。  
In theory,  
假的天子名义都这么好使。  
if a fake royal order could work so well,  
真的天子岂不更厉害？  
should not the real Son of Heaven be even stronger?  
假中央军都能打赢。  
If a fake central army could win,  
真中央军怎么会输？  
how could the real one possibly lose?  
偏偏还真会。  
And yet it could.  
天子让人假冒过。  
Once the Son of Heaven has been successfully impersonated（冒充）,  
身价就会跌。  
his authority takes a hit.  
中央军让人冒充过。  
Once the central army has been counterfeited（仿冒） too,  
战斗力也像打了折。  
its fighting power feels discounted as well.  
所以说。  
That is why  
假冒伪劣真能害人。  
counterfeit（假冒的） goods can do real damage.  

桓王向各路诸侯。  
King Huan then sent out mobilization（动员） orders  
发出征召令。  
to the various lords.  
结果怎样？  
And what was the result?  
秦国忙着抗戎。  
Qin was busy holding off the Rong,  
没空。  
and had no time.  
晋国内乱不断。  
Jin was in internal turmoil（动乱）,  
没精力。  
and had no energy to spare.  
齐鲁正忙着联姻。  
Qi and Lu were busy arranging a marriage alliance（联姻）,  
没兴趣。  
and had no interest.  
至于小国。  
As for the smaller states,  
有的装傻。  
some played dumb,  
有的装糊涂。  
and others pretended not to understand.  
总之能不来就不来。  
In short, whoever could stay away did.  

费了半天劲。  
After all that effort,  
只有蔡、陈、卫三国。  
only Cai, Chen, and Wei  
说愿意听命中央。  
said they would obey the center  
出兵助战。  
and send troops.  
仗还没打。  
Before the campaign even began,  
桓王其实已经先输一阵。  
King Huan had in a sense already lost a round.  
堂堂天子。  
For all his title as Son of Heaven,  
号召力竟然只有这样。  
his power to rally support was no more than this.  
还不如郑国卫国。  
It was not even better than that of Zheng or Wei.  

怎么办？  
What now?  
桓王已经骑虎难下。  
King Huan was riding a tiger and could not dismount（骑虎难下）.  
不打。  
If he did not fight,  
以后更没人听他。  
even fewer people would listen to him later.  
所以只能打。  
So he had to fight.  
而且只能赢。  
And he had to win.  
赢了。  
If he won,  
还能借机重树威信。  
he might rebuild his prestige（威望） in one stroke.  
输了呢？  
And if he lost?  
桓王不敢想。  
King Huan did not dare think about it.  
正因为这样。  
Precisely for that reason,  
他决定御驾亲征。  
he decided to lead the campaign in person.  
这就是一场赌博。  
It was a gamble, plain and simple.  
像被套牢后继续加仓的人。  
Like an investor trapped in a bad position and doubling down,  
越陷越深。  
he only sank deeper.  
通常。  
And usually,  
也会输得更惨。  
that only ends in a worse loss.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `If he did not fight, even fewer people would listen to him later.`。<br>
难点拆解：`If he did not fight ...` 说明桓王为什么被逼到必须出兵；`realized that the royal order had been fake` 里有宾语从句；中间多组并列短句把“以为自己很强”和“现实根本不买账”的反差写得很清楚。<br>
常用短语：
- `cash in`：捞到好处
- `set one's heart on`：铁了心
- `in the blink of an eye`：转眼之间
- `lead the campaign in person`：御驾亲征

### 中央军一败涂地
### The Central Army Is Thoroughly Routed

郑庄公三十七年。  
In the thirty-seventh year of Duke Zhuang,  
也就是前707年。  
that is, in 707 BCE,  
周朝联合卫、蔡、陈。  
Zhou joined forces with Wei, Cai, and Chen  
一起进攻郑国。  
and marched on Zheng.  
大军直逼荥阳。  
The allied host pressed straight toward Xingyang.  
周桓王亲自带队。  
King Huan led the campaign himself.  
周公和虢公。  
The Duke of Zhou and the Duke of Guo  
也都跟着来了。  
both came with the army as well.  
郑国完全没想到。  
Zheng had never expected  
桓王会摆出这么大的阵仗。  
King Huan to put on such a grand display（阵仗）.  
用今天的话说。  
In modern terms,  
几乎所有高层都到了。  
almost all the top brass（高层） had shown up.  

庄公急忙开会。  
Duke Zhuang hurriedly called a meeting  
商量眼前局势。  
to discuss the situation at hand.  
接下来还是老样子。  
What followed was the old pattern.  
大臣分成两派。  
The ministers split into two camps.  
一派主和。  
One favored peace,  
一派主战。  
and the other war.  
来回分析半天。  
After arguing it from every angle,  
最后还是主战派占了上风。  
the war faction prevailed（占上风） in the end.  
大家形成共识。  
A consensus（共识） emerged.  
这一仗得打。  
This battle had to be fought.  
而且要快打。  
And it had to be fought quickly,  
免得拖久了。  
so that delay would not  
再惹来更多外部压力。  
bring in even more outside pressure.  

祭足开始部署。  
Jizu then began to make his arrangements.  
先看联军布置。  
First he studied the allied deployment（部署）.  
按谍报看。  
According to the intelligence（情报）,  
联军分三路进攻。  
the allied army would advance in three columns.  
左路是陈军。  
Chen held the left wing,  
由周公指挥。  
under the Duke of Zhou.  
右路是卫军和蔡军。  
Wei and Cai made up the right wing,  
由虢公统领。  
under the Duke of Guo.  
中路则是周军精锐。  
The center was formed by Zhou's elite（精锐） troops,  
由桓王亲自率领。  
led by King Huan himself.  

再看战斗力。  
Then came the question of actual fighting strength.  
陈军最弱。  
Chen was the weakest.  
周公也不想真拼。  
And the Duke of Zhou had no wish to fight too hard.  
右路那边。  
On the right wing,  
蔡军最弱。  
Cai was the feeblest（最弱的） link,  
卫军虽有点本事。  
while Wei had some real ability,  
斗志却不算强。  
but not much fighting spirit.  
只有中路周军。  
Only the central Zhou force  
才算真正精锐。  
counted as genuine elite troops.  

打法于是定下。  
So the battle plan took shape.  
郑军要各个击破。  
Zheng would break the enemy one wing at a time.  
右翼由曼伯率领。  
On Zheng's right, Manbo would lead the charge  
先冲垮陈军。  
and smash Chen first.  
等陈军一散。  
Once Chen fell apart,  
左翼的高渠弥再上。  
Gao Qumi on the left would move in  
去打卫蔡联军。  
against the Wei-Cai force.  
重点先打蔡军。  
The first target would be Cai.  
等蔡军一溃。  
Once Cai collapsed,  
再合击卫军。  
Zheng would combine against Wei.  
等两翼都垮了。  
After both allied wings were broken,  
左右两路再夹向中军。  
the two Zheng wings would close in on the center.  
那时周军必然后退。  
At that point the Zhou center would surely fall back.  
庄公亲率的正面部队。  
Then Duke Zhuang's frontal force  
再顺势追上。  
would press forward and pursue.  

祭足的打算很清楚。  
Jizu's intention was very clear.  
陈军只求击溃。  
Chen only needed to be routed（击溃）.  
赶走就够了。  
Driving it off would be enough.  
卫军却要重创。  
Wei, by contrast, was to be badly mauled（重创）.  
至于周军。  
As for Zhou's own troops,  
重点是警告。  
the point was mainly to issue a warning.  
这套盘算。  
The whole calculation  
细得像老练的赌徒。  
was as exact as that of a seasoned（老练的） gambler.  
每一步都算得很细。  
Every step had been carefully worked out.  

现在大家都明白。  
Now everyone knew  
自己该做什么。  
what he had to do.  
也都明白。  
And everyone knew as well  
这一仗只能赢。  
that this battle had to be won.  
一旦输了。  
If they lost,  
人人都要背上重罪名。  
every man would carry a grave charge（重罪名）,  
人人都得死。  
every man would die,  
子孙后代。  
and their descendants  
也只能去做奴隶。  
would be left as slaves.  

郑军随即出动。  
The Zheng army then moved out.  
在城外扎营。  
It pitched camp outside the city  
与联军对垒。  
and faced the allied force across the field.  
第二天。  
The next day,  
联军列阵待战。  
the allies drew up their lines and waited for battle.  
按规矩。  
By custom,  
郑军也该出营列阵。  
Zheng should have come out and formed ranks as well.  
可规矩是人定的。  
But rules are made by people,  
也就能被人利用。  
and people can use them to their own advantage.  
郑军一动不动。  
Zheng did not move an inch.  
营门紧闭。  
Its camp gates stayed shut.  
就是不出战。  
It simply would not come out to fight.  

桓王本来就烦。  
King Huan was already irritated.  
连骂庄公的话都想好了。  
He had even prepared the words with which to curse Duke Zhuang.  
可他怎么也没想到。  
What he had never expected  
庄公根本不露面。  
was that Duke Zhuang would not show himself at all.  
一个上午过去。  
A whole morning passed.  
联军又渴又饿。  
The allied troops grew thirsty and hungry.  
郑军还是没动静。  
Zheng still did nothing.  
桓王正犹豫。  
King Huan was hesitating  
到底是撤还是强攻。  
over whether to withdraw or storm the camp  
忽然郑营鼓声大作。  
when drums suddenly thundered inside the Zheng camp.  

紧接着。  
At once,  
郑营右翼大门洞开。  
the right gate of the Zheng camp flew open.  
战车呼啸而出。  
Chariots burst out with a roar.  
不列阵。  
No formal lines.  
不废话。  
No speeches.  
像猛虎下山。  
Like tigers charging down a mountain,  
直扑联军左翼。  
they tore straight into the allied left.  
陈军哪见过这个。  
Chen had never seen anything like it.  
郑军还没杀到跟前。  
Before Zheng even reached them,  
他们已经四散逃命。  
Chen's men were already scattering for their lives.  
联军左翼当场崩了。  
The allied left collapsed on the spot.  

桓王大吃一惊。  
King Huan was stunned.  
急忙下令。  
He hastily ordered  
中军左侧去顶住。  
the left side of the center to hold the blow.  
可还没等他缓过神。  
But before he could recover,  
郑军左路也杀出来了。  
Zheng's left wing burst out as well.  
这一波更猛。  
This assault was even fiercer（凶猛的）.  
直冲卫蔡阵地。  
It crashed straight into the Wei-Cai positions.  
卫军还能撑一阵。  
Wei held for a while,  
蔡军却一下就垮了。  
but Cai collapsed almost at once.  
接着郑军合击卫军。  
Then Zheng combined its attack against Wei.  
卫军大败。  
Wei suffered a crushing defeat.  
死伤无数。  
Its dead and wounded were countless.  

两翼都没了。  
With both wings gone,  
郑军左右夹向中军。  
Zheng's left and right closed in on the center.  
正面主力也压了上来。  
Its main force surged forward from the front as well.  
到了这一步。  
At that point,  
桓王知道没法再打。  
King Huan knew the battle could not be saved.  
只能撤退。  
Retreat was the only option.  

有人总说。  
People often say  
打败仗时总是领导先跑。  
that leaders are always the first to run in a defeat.  
其实未必。  
That is not always true.  
桓王下令。  
King Huan ordered  
后队改前队。  
the rear ranks to become the vanguard（前军）  
先行撤退。  
and withdraw first.  
前队改后队。  
The former front ranks  
留下掩护。  
were left behind to cover them.  
他自己反倒压在最后。  
He himself remained at the very back.  

后来郑军所谓合击。  
When Zheng later "closed in,"  
其实更多是合而不击。  
it closed in more than it struck.  
再后来的追击。  
And its later "pursuit"  
也是追而不打。  
was more pursuit than attack.  
人家已经给了面子。  
Zheng was already giving the king face.  
特意留了条路。  
It had deliberately left him a way out.  
照理就该快走。  
Under the circumstances, he should have left at once.  
可桓王偏不。  
But King Huan refused to do so.  
见郑军没真追上来。  
Seeing that Zheng was not pressing hard,  
他还以为对方怕了。  
he actually thought they were afraid of him.  
于是慢悠悠往回撤。  
So he withdrew at an almost leisurely pace  
一路还破口大骂。  
while cursing all the way.  
“老贼，有种出来。  
"Old traitor, come out if you dare.  
咱们再决个胜负。”  
Let us settle this properly."  

庄公和祭足。  
Duke Zhuang and Jizu  
对桓王多少还有顾忌。  
still felt some restraint toward the king.  
可郑军诸将。  
But Zheng's generals  
没有这些顾忌。  
felt no such restraint.  
他们只对庄公负责。  
They answered to Duke Zhuang alone.  
你桓王又算什么？  
What was King Huan to them?  
既然给脸不要。  
If he would not accept the face he had been given,  
那就不用再客气。  
there was no reason to be polite.  
大将祝聃。  
General Zhu Dan  
拈弓搭箭。  
raised his bow and set an arrow,  
根本不管后果。  
caring nothing for the consequences（后果）.  
瞄着桓王就是一箭。  
He aimed straight at King Huan and let fly.  

桓王正骂得起劲。  
King Huan was still cursing away  
忽然见一箭飞来。  
when he suddenly saw an arrow flying toward him.  
想躲。  
He tried to dodge,  
却没躲开。  
but failed.  
那箭扎进肩膀。  
The arrow buried itself in his shoulder.  
这下他不骂了。  
That ended the shouting at once.  
他终于明白。  
He finally understood  
对方是来真的。  
that Zheng meant business.  
那还能怎么办？  
What else could he do now?  
跑。  
Run.  

桓王的战车。  
King Huan's chariot  
立刻狂奔起来。  
immediately bolted away.  
很快追上大队人马。  
It quickly caught up with the main body.  
郑军在后面。  
Behind them, Zheng's soldiers  
鼓也敲。  
beat drums,  
喊也喊。  
shouted loudly,  
却就是不真追。  
yet never truly pressed the pursuit.  
摆明了。  
It was plain for anyone to see  
他们是在放周王走。  
that they were letting the Zhou king go.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`时间状语从句`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `If they lost, every man would carry a grave charge, every man would die, and their descendants would be left as slaves.`。<br>
难点拆解：`If they lost ...` 先把这一战“只能赢不能输”的压力说透；中段大量并列短句是故意模拟战场推进的速度；`Seeing that ...`、`when ...` 这些时间或伴随结构又把战局变化卡得很紧。<br>
常用短语：
- `hold for a while`：撑一阵
- `at an almost leisurely pace`：慢悠悠地
- `care nothing for`：毫不顾及
- `mean business`：来真的

### 哑巴吃黄连
### Swallowing a Bitter Dose in Silence

周桓王挨了一箭。  
King Huan took an arrow.  
好在甲胄厚。  
Fortunately, his armor（甲胄） was thick,  
又隔得远。  
and the shot came from a distance.  
只擦破一点皮。  
It only broke the skin a little.  
并无大碍。  
There was no serious harm.  

现在的问题是。  
The real question now was  
还打不打。  
whether the campaign could continue.  
陈军已经散了。  
Chen's troops had scattered.  
跑到哪儿都不知道。  
No one even knew where they had gone.  
蔡军也散了。  
Cai's troops had scattered too.  
卫军被打残一半。  
Wei had lost about half its force.  
真正还完整的。  
The only force still intact  
只剩周军自己。  
was Zhou's own.  
而这还是因为。  
And even that was only because  
郑国不好意思下死手。  
Zheng had felt awkward about striking too hard at the king.  
再打。  
If they kept fighting,  
已经打不过了。  
they could not win.  
撤吧。  
But to withdraw  
又实在太丢人。  
was humiliating.  

桓王这时才后悔。  
Only now did King Huan regret  
当初没听周公和虢公的话。  
not listening to the Duke of Zhou and the Duke of Guo in the first place.  
可后悔也没用。  
But regret was useless.  
正在这时。  
Just then,  
郑庄公派人来了。  
Duke Zhuang sent someone over.  
谁？  
Who was it?  
祭足。  
Jizu.  

祭足来做什么？  
And what had Jizu come for?  
送粮食。  
To bring grain.  
还带了牛羊。  
He brought cattle and sheep as well.  
祭足说。  
Jizu said  
这其实是一场误会。  
that the whole thing had been a misunderstanding（误会）.  
天子讨伐诸侯。  
If the Son of Heaven punishes a feudal lord,  
那本来就是应该的。  
that is only proper.  
我们主公原本。  
Their lord had originally meant  
只是闭门自省。  
merely to shut his gates and reflect on himself.  
谁知道手下几员大将。  
Who could have guessed  
不懂事。  
that several commanders would act so rashly（鲁莽地）,  
擅自出营迎战。  
march out on their own,  
还伤了大王。  
and even wound the king?  
我们主公已经。  
Their lord had already  
把他们撤了职。  
removed them from command.  
又派我前来。  
And he had sent Jizu  
给大王赔罪。  
to apologize to His Majesty,  
顺便劳军。  
offer supplies to the army,  
若大王赏脸。  
and, if His Majesty would grant the honor（赏脸）,  
还请进城歇息。  
invite him into the city to rest.  

祭足这番话。  
Jizu delivered all this  
说得不卑不亢。  
without sounding either servile（卑微讨好的） or rude.  
有理。  
It had reason,  
有利。  
it had usefulness,  
也有分寸。  
and it kept perfect measure.  

其实郑国这边。  
In truth, on Zheng's side,  
听说桓王中箭以后。  
once they heard that King Huan had been hit,  
也有点慌。  
they were uneasy as well.  
大家一商量。  
After talking it over,  
认定桓王肯定也想撤。  
they concluded that the king surely wanted to withdraw（撤退） too.  
不如主动给个台阶。  
So it was better to offer him a way down first.  
这样双方都好看。  
That would let both sides save face.  
于是祭足才来了。  
That was why Jizu had been sent.  

台阶既然摆出来了。  
Now that the ladder had been put in place,  
桓王还能不下吗？  
how could King Huan refuse to climb down（下台）?  
就这样。  
And so,  
中央军撤了。  
the royal army withdrew.  

回国以后。  
After returning home,  
桓王心里还是憋着气。  
King Huan was still burning with anger.  
堂堂天子。  
For the Son of Heaven  
竟被诸侯打败。  
to be beaten by a feudal lord  
实在太丢脸。  
was simply too humiliating.  
于是他又动了心思。  
So he began thinking again  
想再来一次讨伐。  
about launching another punitive campaign（讨伐）.  
他把公卿都叫来。  
He summoned the nobles  
说自己准备再次征召诸侯。  
and said he meant to summon（征召） the lords once more  
去打郑国。  
to attack Zheng.  
底下先是一阵。  
The first response from below  
“切。”  
was a collective snort.  
然后大家都笑了。  
Then everyone laughed.  
桓王更挂不住脸。  
King Huan found it even harder to save face.  

周公先出来劝。  
The Duke of Zhou spoke first.  
他说这回虽然打输了。  
He said that although they had lost this time,  
好在郑国给了台阶。  
Zheng had at least given them a way down,  
又赔礼又道歉。  
with gifts and apologies,  
事情还没闹大。  
so the matter had not spread too far.  
如今本来知道败仗的人。  
As things stood,  
并不算多。  
not that many people knew about the defeat.  
如果现在再征召天下诸侯。  
If they now summoned all the lords again,  
那就等于告诉天下。  
it would amount to telling the whole world  
自己已经输过一回。  
that they had already lost once.  
那不是。  
Would that not be  
自己又绕着圈丢人？  
making fools of themselves in public all over again?  

桓王没吭声。  
King Huan said nothing.  
兵败之后。  
After the defeat,  
他的底气也没那么足了。  
his confidence was no longer what it had been.  
虢公也接着劝。  
The Duke of Guo then added his advice.  
上次征召。  
Last time,  
本来响应的就没几个。  
only a handful had answered the call.  
这次若一个也不来。  
If this time no one came at all,  
岂不是更难堪？  
would that not be even more embarrassing?  
桓王最后只能叹气。  
In the end, King Huan could only sigh.  
“散了吧。”  
"Dismissed."  
这件事也只好认栽。  
On this matter, he had no choice but to admit defeat（认栽）.  
从那以后。  
From that point on,  
直到去世。  
all the way until his death,  
桓王再没提过一个“郑”字。  
King Huan never again uttered the word "Zheng."  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`时间状语从句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `If they now summoned all the lords again, it would amount to telling the whole world that they had already lost once.`。<br>
难点拆解：`If they now summoned ...` 是条件状语从句；`telling the whole world that ...` 里 `that ...` 是宾语从句；前面 `once they heard ...`、`After talking it over ...` 这些时间结构，又把双方如何顺势下台交代清楚了。<br>
常用短语：
- `grant the honor`：赏脸
- `climb down`：下台，让步
- `all the way until`：一直到
- `admit defeat`：认输，认栽

## 第十三章 楚国和南联盟
## Chapter 13: Chu and the Southern Alliance

中央出兵失利。  
The central court had failed on campaign before.  
这不是头一回。  
This was hardly the first time.  
宣王打戎。  
King Xuan once campaigned against the Rong  
结果打输了。  
and lost.  
戎人的势力。  
That defeat  
因此长得更快。  
only made the Rong grow stronger.  
再往前。  
Earlier still,  
昭王南征楚国。  
King Zhao marched south against Chu,  
过江时却翻船而死。  
only to die when his boat capsized on the river.  
从那以后。  
From that point on,  
楚国几乎彻底。  
Chu was, for all practical purposes（实际上）,  
脱离了中央。  
beyond the center's control.  
既然说到了楚国。  
Since Chu has come up,  
那就得接着说。  
we had better keep going.  

### 楚国的来源
### The Origins of Chu

楚国在南方。  
Chu lay in the south.  
建国于荆。  
It first rose in Jing,  
大致是今天的荆州一带。  
roughly around today's Jingzhou.  
国都在丹阳。  
Its capital was Danyang,  
大致是今天的枝江。  
probably near present-day Zhijiang.  
照例。  
As usual,  
先从祖先说起。  
we begin with the ancestors.  

最上头还是黄帝。  
At the very top stands the Yellow Emperor,  
这里就略过。  
but we can skip him here.  
再往后。  
Further down the line,  
他们家因擅长用火。  
one of Chu's forebears was famed for mastering fire  
和处理燃料。  
and the handling of fuel.  
因此得了“祝融”的名号。  
He was therefore given the title Zhurong.  
祝融是什么？  
And what was Zhurong?  
就是上古传说里的火神。  
The ancient fire god of Chinese legend.  
所以古人若说。  
So if an ancient person told you,  
“祝融到你家了”。  
"Zhurong has visited your house,"  
你多半得哭。  
you would probably start crying,  
因为那意思是着火了。  
because it meant your home was on fire.  
这位祝融。  
This very Zhurong  
就是楚国的祖先。  
was an ancestor of Chu.  
所以说。  
Which is why  
有一门手艺。  
having a practical skill  
真的很重要。  
really matters.  

祝融的儿子叫陆终。  
Zhurong's son was named Lu Zhong.  
陆终娶了鬼方国君的女儿。  
Lu Zhong married the daughter of the ruler of Guifang.  
鬼方在哪儿？  
Where was Guifang?  
历来说法很多。  
Scholars have never agreed.  
有人说在西北。  
Some place it in the northwest,  
有人说在西南。  
others in the southwest.  
总之。  
In any case,  
那是个偏远地方。  
it was a remote region.  

鬼方公主很厉害。  
The princess of Guifang was remarkable.  
她一胎怀了六个。  
She carried sextuplets（六胞胎） in a single pregnancy.  
放到今天。  
Even by today's standards,  
这都算大新闻。  
that would count as world-class news.  
六胞胎是什么概念？  
And what did that mean in practice?  
肚子大得快要炸开。  
Her belly was nearly bursting.  
想顺产。  
A natural delivery  
基本没戏。  
was out of the question.  
于是只能剖腹产。  
So the birth had to be by caesarean（剖腹产） section.  
《史记》记成。  
The Records of the Grand Historian phrases it as  
“坼剖而产焉”。  
"the womb was cut open and the children were delivered."  
也不知是谁下的刀。  
No one knows who wielded the knife.  
一刀下去。  
One cut brought out three babies.  
还不够。  
That was still not enough.  
再来一刀。  
So came another cut,  
右边又出了三个。  
and three more came out from the other side.  
这说明。  
Which suggests  
中国上古时期。  
that ancient China  
就已经懂剖腹产了。  
already knew how to perform caesarean delivery.  

这六个孩子。  
All six children  
还全是儿子。  
were boys.  
不但都是儿子。  
And not only boys,  
个个也都很争气。  
each of them turned out well.  
老六叫季连。  
The youngest was Ji Lian.  
他姓芈。  
His surname was Mi.  
到了商朝末年。  
By the end of the Shang dynasty,  
季连的后人里。  
among Ji Lian's descendants  
出了个鬻熊。  
there appeared a man named Yuxiong.  
这人很不简单。  
He was no ordinary man.  
“鬻”是什么意思？  
What does `yu` mean?  
就是“卖”。  
It means "to sell."  
所以鬻熊要照字面翻。  
So if you translate Yuxiong literally,  
就是“卖熊的人”。  
it becomes "the man who sells bears."  
放到今天。  
In modern terms,  
听着简直像偷猎者。  
that almost sounds like a poacher（偷猎者）.  
后来鬻熊的后代。  
Later, Yuxiong's descendants  
就改姓熊。  
took Xiong as their surname.  
不过不能简单推理。  
But you cannot reason too mechanically.  
也别因此就觉得。  
So do not conclude from that  
姓马的祖先。  
that everyone surnamed Ma  
就是卖马的。  
must descend from horse traders.  

照理说。  
In theory,  
卖熊像体力活。  
selling bears sounds like brute labor,  
不像读书人的事。  
not the work of a scholar.  
可鬻熊偏偏有学问。  
Yet Yuxiong was learned（有学问的）.  
周文王和周武王。  
King Wen and King Wu of Zhou  
都曾向他请教。  
both sought his advice.  
《史记》还说。  
The Records even says  
他“子事文王”。  
that he "served King Wen as a son."  
换句话说。  
In other words,  
他像文王的干儿子。  
he was something like King Wen's adopted son  
也是武王的干哥哥。  
and King Wu's sworn elder brother.  

到了周成王时。  
By the reign of King Cheng of Zhou,  
王室分封先贤后代。  
the court was enfeoffing（分封） the descendants of worthy men.  
于是鬻熊的孙子的儿子熊绎。  
So Xiong Yi, the grandson's son of Yuxiong,  
被封到楚。  
was enfeoffed in Chu  
爵位是子。  
with the rank of viscount.  
那时候熊绎本人。  
At that time, Xiong Yi himself  
还留在都城辅佐成王。  
remained in the royal capital to assist King Cheng.  
楚国则交给儿子打理。  
Chu itself was left for his son to manage.  
那时的楚地。  
In those days, Chu  
还属荆蛮之地。  
still lay in what the central states called the wild Jing region  
谈不上开化。  
and was far from "civilized" in their eyes.  

熊绎还是许多姓氏的祖先。  
Xiong Yi was also the ancestor of many surnames.  
熊、苗、楚、上官、伍、屈。  
Xiong, Miao, Chu, Shangguan, Wu, Qu,  
潘、班、查、庄、严、卓。  
Pan, Ban, Zha, Zhuang, Yan, Zhuo,  
莫、景、成等等。  
Mo, Jing, Cheng, and many others  
都出自这一支。  
all trace back to this line.  
以上这些姓的读者。  
Readers bearing those surnames  
此处请保持敬意。  
may wish to stay respectfully attentive.  

### 楚独
### Chu Independence

什么叫天高王远？  
What does it mean when the ruler is far away?  
那时该说。  
In those days, one should have said,  
天高周王远。  
"the Zhou king is far away."  
什么叫天地大？  
And what is real room to grow?  
前面全是机会。  
It is having opportunity spread out before you.  

周朝宗室和功臣。  
Zhou princes and meritorious（有功的） ministers  
当初大多封在中原。  
were mostly enfeoffed in the Central Plain at first.  
那时黄河流域。  
Back then the Yellow River basin  
还是块好地方。  
was still rich country.  
所以他们日子都不差。  
So they lived quite well.  
可也有问题。  
But there was a problem.  
他们没多少扩张空间。  
They had little room to expand.  
更糟的是。  
Worse still,  
他们都在王室眼皮底下。  
they lived under the royal court's eye,  
自主权很少。  
with very little autonomy（自主权）.  

楚国却不同。  
Chu was different.  
它远在荆蛮。  
It sat deep in the Jing south.  
周王懒得管。  
The Zhou king could hardly be bothered to control it,  
也未必管得着。  
nor could he easily do so.  
往南往西往东。  
To the south, west, and east,  
到处有地可占。  
there was land to seize,  
到处有人可征。  
and peoples to subdue（征服）.  
带着中原更先进的文化。  
Armed with the more advanced culture of the Central Plain,  
熊绎的后人。  
Xiong Yi's descendants  
在南方简直如鱼得水。  
thrived in the south like fish in water.  
没人是他们的对手。  
They had no real rivals.  
他们不理周朝那些规矩。  
They paid little attention to Zhou ritual rules,  
也懒得管中原诸侯的勾心斗角。  
and cared nothing for the scheming among the central lords.  
他们只做一件事。  
They focused on one thing only:  
向外扩张。  
outward expansion.  
楚国很快占了湖北大部。  
Chu soon took most of Hubei,  
后来又吞下湖南大部。  
and later swallowed much of Hunan as well.  
没过多久。  
Before long,  
它就成了地盘最大的诸侯国。  
it had become the largest feudal state in territory.  

楚国还是当时。  
Chu was also, at that time,  
最好战的国家。  
the most bellicose（好战的） state of all.  
若连续七年。  
If it went seven straight years  
不对外动兵。  
without attacking someone,  
他们都觉得丢人。  
it would treat that as a national humiliation（耻辱）.  
谁做楚国的邻居。  
To be Chu's neighbor  
都是件苦差事。  
was miserable work,  
因为它像老虎。  
because Chu was like a tiger,  
总得找人磨牙。  
always needing something to sharpen its teeth on.  

周王室渐渐忘了。  
The Zhou royal house gradually forgot  
还有这么一个诸侯国。  
that such a state even existed.  
中原诸侯更看不起它。  
The central states looked down on it even more.  
他们不屑。  
They disdained（不屑于） it  
跟这个南方蛮国来往。  
dealing with what they saw as a backward southern power.  

到了周夷王时。  
By the time of King Yi of Zhou,  
王室号召力已大不如前。  
the royal house had lost much of its authority（号召力）.  
楚国这边。  
In Chu,  
则到了熊渠执政。  
Xiong Qu had come to power.  
有一天。  
One day,  
熊渠高声说。  
Xiong Qu declared aloud,  
“我蛮夷也。  
"We are people of the southern marches.  
不与中国之号谥。”  
We do not accept the titles of the Central States."  
这话翻成今天的话。  
Put in modern language,  
差不多就是。  
it amounted to saying,  
我们要自己来。  
"We will go our own way."  
说白了。  
To put it bluntly,  
这就是“楚独”。  
this was "Chu independence."  
熊渠还真这么干了。  
And Xiong Qu meant it.  

他嫌自己这么强。  
He felt that a power this strong  
却还只是子爵。  
should not still be stuck with the rank of viscount.  
于是索性自己称王。  
So he simply declared himself king.  
不但自己称王。  
Not only that,  
还把三个儿子。  
he also made his three sons  
一并封王。  
kings as well.  
大儿子熊康。  
His eldest son, Xiong Kang,  
封句亶王。  
became King of Judan.  
二儿子红。  
His second son, Hong,  
封鄂王。  
became King of E.  
小儿子执疵。  
His youngest, Zhici,  
封越章王。  
became King of Yuezhang.  

不过熊渠也有点虚。  
Still, Xiong Qu lacked complete confidence.  
到了周厉王时。  
When King Li of Zhou came to the throne,  
因为厉王暴虐。  
and his brutality（暴虐） became notorious（臭名昭著的）,  
熊渠有点怕了。  
Xiong Qu grew uneasy.  
他怕厉王真来讨伐。  
He feared a real punitive expedition（讨伐）.  
于是主动取消王号。  
So he dropped the royal title on his own.  
第一次“楚独”。  
Chu's first bid for independence  
就这样失败了。  
ended just like that.  
更没意思的是。  
What made it more awkward  
不管他称王。  
was that when he declared himself king  
还是后来撤回。  
and when he later withdrew the claim,  
竟然都没人理他。  
almost nobody cared.  
完全是自己唱独角戏。  
He was performing to an empty room.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`原因状语从句`、`分词结构`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `If it went seven straight years without attacking someone, it would treat that as a national humiliation.`。<br>
难点拆解：`If ...` 先给出极端条件，说明楚国好战到什么程度；`without attacking someone` 是介词短语，补充“七年没出兵”的状态；后面 `because Chu was like a tiger, always needing ...` 又用原因结构和分词结构继续解释。<br>
常用短语：
- `like fish in water`：如鱼得水
- `look down on`：看不起
- `go one's own way`：走自己的路
- `on one's own`：自行地，自己来

### 一国两制的构想
### A Vision of One Country, Two Systems

时间到了熊通执政。  
Time moved on to the reign of Xiong Tong.  
熊通很不爽。  
Xiong Tong was deeply annoyed.  
因为他还不是王。  
He was still not a king.  
这么多年过去。  
Generation after generation had passed.  
别的诸侯爵位有的升了。  
Some other lords had risen in rank.  
楚国却还只是子爵。  
Chu was still only a viscountcy.  
他一直在等。  
He had been waiting  
等一个机会。  
for an opportunity,  
一个称王的机会。  
an opportunity to claim kingship.  

有一天。  
One day,  
有人从洛邑回来。  
someone came back from Luoyi.  
准确说。  
To be exact,  
楚国在洛邑一直有耳目。  
Chu had long kept informants（耳目） in Luoyi,  
也可以叫驻京办。  
or, in modern slang, a liaison office.  
当然。  
Of course,  
那是地下的。  
it was unofficial and underground.  
周朝的中央精神。  
Even if royal commands  
未必真能管到楚国。  
no longer truly bound Chu,  
该听的风声。  
it still needed  
楚国还是要听。  
to stay informed.  

来人汇报说。  
The report was simple:  
周桓王讨伐郑国。  
King Huan had campaigned against Zheng,  
结果被打得很惨。  
and been beaten badly.  
如今连再打郑国。  
Now he would not even dare  
都不敢再提。  
bring up attacking Zheng again.  
熊通听完就笑了。  
Xiong Tong smiled when he heard it.  
原来周王室。  
So this was all  
也不过这点本事。  
the Zhou royal house was worth.  
“老子要称王。”  
"I am going to be king,"  
熊通说。  
he said.  
他要搞“楚独”。  
He was ready for a Chu breakaway.  

“慢着。”  
"Not so fast."  
令尹斗伯比开口了。  
Then Dou Bobi, the Lingyin, spoke up.  
令尹是什么？  
What was the Lingyin?  
大致就相当于。  
Roughly speaking,  
中原诸侯的上卿。  
it matched the chief minister among the central states,  
也就是总理。  
a kind of prime minister.  
熊通瞪着他说。  
Xiong Tong glared at him.  
“你反对？”  
"You object?"  
熊通脾气很暴。  
Xiong Tong had a fierce temper,  
当场就急了。  
and flared up at once.  

斗伯比却说。  
But Dou Bobi said,  
“我怎么会反对？  
"Why would I object?  
楚国地大物博。  
Chu is vast and rich,  
物产丰饶。  
abundant（丰富的） in produce,  
人才也多。  
rich in talent,  
兵甲十万。  
with a hundred thousand armored troops,  
天下少有敌手。  
and few rivals under Heaven.  
主公如此英明。  
With a ruler as brilliant as you,  
称王有什么不行？  
what is wrong with becoming king?  
不过。”  
But."  
世上最吓人的。  
The two most alarming  
常常就是这两个字。  
words in the world are often those two:  
“不过”。  
"But."  
因为后面。  
Because what follows  
往往才是重点。  
is usually the real point.  
熊通爱听前半段。  
Xiong Tong enjoyed the flattery at the front,  
也想听后半段。  
and wanted the rest too.  

斗伯比说。  
Dou Bobi continued,  
贸然称王。  
to claim kingship rashly  
还是不太妥。  
would not be wise.  
那等于公开搞分裂。  
It would amount to open secession（分裂）.  
这样既难听。  
That would sound bad,  
也麻烦多。  
and it would bring trouble.  
他接着说。  
Then he added,  
自己有个更好的主意。  
that he had a better idea.  
熊通当然有兴趣。  
Xiong Tong was, of course, interested.  
斗伯比确实聪明。  
Dou Bobi was genuinely shrewd（精明的）,  
大致就像楚国版的祭足。  
almost a Chu version of Jizu.  

“我的主意。  
"My idea,  
叫一国两制。”  
is one country, two systems."  
这话听着很现代。  
It sounds startlingly modern,  
可放在当时。  
but in that age  
意思其实也差不多。  
the logic was surprisingly similar.  
怎么个“一国两制”？  
So what would that mean?  
名义上。  
In name,  
仍尊周朝为中央。  
Zhou would still remain the center.  
但同时。  
At the same time,  
要让周朝封楚国为王。  
Zhou would recognize Chu as king  
让楚国统管整个南方。  
and let it oversee the whole south.  
楚国反过来。  
Chu could in return  
再帮周朝。  
help Zhou  
维持中原秩序。  
keep order in the Central Plain.  

说白了。  
Put bluntly,  
周朝表面还是老大。  
Zhou would still look like the senior power on the surface,  
实际大家都称王。  
but in reality both sides would be kings.  
各管各的地盘。  
Each would rule its own territory.  
各用各的制度。  
Each would keep its own system.  
彼此互不干涉。  
And neither would interfere with the other.  
对外则还算一个周朝。  
Outwardly, it could still be called one Zhou world.  
更妙的是。  
Better yet,  
一旦这样安排。  
once the arrangement was in place,  
楚国的地位。  
Chu's rank  
立刻压过所有中原诸侯。  
would rise above every central-state lord.  
以后不管齐晋郑宋。  
From then on, whether Qi, Jin, Zheng, or Song,  
见了楚国。  
whenever they met Chu,  
都得先矮半截。  
they would have to bow half a step lower  
老老实实装孙子。  
and behave themselves.  
斗伯比当年没想到。  
Dou Bobi could not have known  
自己这个构想。  
that this political design（构想）  
几千年后。  
would still echo  
还会有人再用。  
thousands of years later.  
熊通一听大喜。  
Xiong Tong was delighted.  
“你真有才。”  
"You really are brilliant."  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`并列谓语`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `If Zhou recognized Chu as king and let it oversee the whole south, Chu could in return help Zhou keep order in the Central Plain.`。<br>
难点拆解：`If ...` 先给出政治交易成立的条件；`recognized ... and let ...` 是两个并列动作，说明周朝需要同时做什么；后面 `help Zhou keep order ...` 用动词原形说明“帮助做什么”。<br>
常用短语：
- `in return`：作为回报
- `on the surface`：表面上
- `keep order`：维持秩序
- `rise above`：凌驾于……之上

### 一国两制的推进
### Advancing the One-Country-Two-Systems Plan

设想当然很好。  
The idea was excellent, of course.  
可真要推起来。  
But turning it into reality  
得讲技巧。  
required finesse（技巧）.  
楚国不可能自己跑去周朝。  
Chu could not simply go to Zhou on its own  
开口求封王。  
and ask to be made king.  
几百年不朝贡了。  
It had not paid tribute（朝贡） for centuries.  
一去就要跟周王平起平坐。  
To show up and demand equal status with the Zhou king  
那是自讨没趣。  
would only invite humiliation（难堪）.  
更何况周朝那边。  
Besides,  
能说会道的人太多。  
Zhou had plenty of eloquent（能言善辩的） men.  
真要摆出一通大道理。  
If they launched into lofty arguments,  
说不定能把你说得。  
they might talk you into  
当场心虚。  
feeling ashamed on the spot.  
那该谁去说？  
So who should do the talking?  
这就成了学问。  
That was the real problem.  

斗伯比说。  
Dou Bobi said  
楚国北面是中原诸国。  
that to the north of Chu lay the central states,  
西面南面。  
while the west and south  
都差不多是楚的势力范围。  
were already more or less within Chu's sphere.  
眼下北面的郑国正强。  
For the moment, Zheng to the north was strong,  
不宜轻碰。  
and not to be provoked lightly.  
东北方向。  
To the northeast,  
有个随国。  
there was the state of Sui.  
随国一直和中原来往。  
Sui maintained ties with the central states  
却不怎么跟楚国玩。  
and kept its distance from Chu.  
它又是周王室宗支。  
It also belonged to the Zhou royal line,  
爵位还是侯。  
and held the rank of marquis.  
若能让随国。  
If Sui could be made  
出面去和周朝谈。  
to speak with Zhou on Chu's behalf,  
那就最理想。  
that would be ideal.  
熊通问。  
Xiong Tong asked,  
“主意是好。  
"The idea is good,  
可随国凭什么替我们办事？”  
but why would Sui do this for us?"  

斗伯比说。  
Dou Bobi replied  
办法很简单。  
that the method was simple.  
先派人去。  
First send envoys  
要求与它签和约。  
to ask for a peace treaty（和约）.  
它若不签。
