If Sui refused,  
我们就有理由打它。  
Chu would then have a reason to attack.  
等把它打服。  
Once it was subdued（打服）,  
条件自然就好提了。  
the terms would be much easier to raise.  
这就是第一步。  
That was step one,  
也就是先礼后兵。  
the classic sequence of courtesy first, force later.  
楚国虽然被叫蛮国。  
Though Chu was called a barbarian state,  
可统治者毕竟。  
its ruling house after all  
也是从中原去的。  
had roots in the Central Plain.  
祖上还很有学问。  
And its ancestors had been well educated.  
所以中原的规矩。  
So Chu understood the rules of the central states  
它懂。  
perfectly well.  
蛮夷那一套。  
It also understood  
它也懂。  
how to deal as a frontier power.  
打南边的蛮子小国。  
When it fought smaller southern tribes,  
它通常很直接。  
it was usually blunt:  
打你就是打你。  
an attack was simply an attack.  
懒得找借口。  
No excuses were needed.  
可若要打中原国家。  
But if it meant striking a central-state polity,  
就得先找个名目。  
it first needed a proper pretext（借口）.  
这一套。  
In that sense,  
楚国两手都准备了。  
Chu prepared both hands,  
而且两手都很硬。  
and both were strong.  

前706年。  
In 706 BCE,  
熊通亲率大军。  
Xiong Tong led a great army in person  
直抵随国边境。  
to the borders of Sui,  
到了瑕地。  
reaching Xia.  
随后派大夫薳章。  
He then sent the grandee Wei Zhang  
去见随国。  
to Sui  
要求缔结和平条约。  
to demand a treaty of peaceful relations.  
若随国不肯。  
If Sui refused,  
那就直接开打。  
war would follow at once.  

楚国的国书。  
Chu's letter  
很快送到随国。  
soon reached Sui.  
随侯一向瞧不起楚国。  
The Marquis of Sui had always looked down on Chu,  
可对它的实力。  
though he did fear  
多少也有些忌惮。  
its strength to some degree.  
现在一看楚使上门。  
So when he saw envoys from Chu,  
他先以为。  
his first thought was  
不是来勒索。  
that they were either here to extort（勒索）  
就是来敲诈。  
or to bully him.  
可等读完国书。  
But once he read the letter,  
发现对方讲的是。  
he found it spoke of  
睦邻友好。  
good-neighborly relations（睦邻友好）.  
他反倒放心了。  
That actually reassured him.  

这时季梁提醒。  
At that point Ji Liang warned him,  
“别高兴太早。  
"Do not relax too soon.  
楚是大国。  
Chu is a great power.  
我们是小国。  
We are a small one.  
它无缘无故来讲友好。  
If it suddenly talks friendship for no reason,  
背后一定有图谋。”  
there must be an ulterior motive（图谋） behind it."  
随侯一听。  
The Marquis of Sui heard this  
觉得有理。  
and thought it made sense.  
君臣随后展开讨论。  
Ruler and ministers discussed the matter,  
又仔细研究国书。  
and studied the letter closely.  
最后得出的结论是。  
Their final conclusion was this:  
国书里看不出陷阱。  
there was no obvious trap in the wording.  
既然不签的坏处。  
Since the danger of refusing  
摆在眼前。  
was plain to see,  
而签了的坏处。  
while the danger of signing  
眼下还看不出来。  
was not yet visible,  
那就先签。  
they might as well sign.  

于是随侯回了一封。  
So the Marquis sent back  
热情洋溢的回信。  
a warm and enthusiastic reply.  
他表示愿与楚国。  
He said Sui wished to build  
世代交好。  
lasting friendship with Chu.  
信写好后。  
Once the letter was ready,  
随侯派大夫少师。  
he dispatched the grandee Shaoshi  
前往楚营回报。  
to the Chu camp with the response.  

得知随侯派少师来。  
When Dou Bobi learned that Sui was sending Shaoshi,  
斗伯比笑了。  
he smiled.  
“大王。  
"My lord,  
随国虽然不大。  
Sui may not be large,  
可也不算太小。  
but it is not tiny either.  
在汉东一带。  
East of the Han River,  
它是小国的头。  
it leads the smaller states.  
若它发现我军很强。  
If it realizes our army is formidable（强大的）,  
必然会害怕。  
it will grow afraid  
也会拉拢周边小国。  
and rally the surrounding states  
一起对付我们。  
against us.  
可若让它以为。  
But if we make it think  
我们只是纸老虎。  
we are only a paper tiger（纸老虎）,  
它就不会去团结别人。  
it will not bother to unite the others.  
到那时再收拾它。  
Then dealing with it  
就容易得多了。”  
will be much easier."  
所以斗伯比建议。  
So Dou Bobi proposed  
把精兵藏起来。  
hiding the elite troops  
只拿老弱病残。  
and showing only the old, weak, sick, and worn-out men  
给少师看。  
to Shaoshi.  
他研究随国多年。  
He had studied Sui for years  
知道少师。  
and knew Shaoshi  
是个靠裙带上来的废物。  
was an incompetent（无能的） man risen by patronage（裙带关系）.  

熊通问。  
Xiong Tong asked,  
“主意倒是好。  
"It is a clever idea.  
可季梁看不穿吗？”  
But won't Ji Liang see through it?"  
季梁是随国贤臣。  
Ji Liang was Sui's ablest minister,  
足智多谋。  
resourceful（足智多谋） and shrewd,  
熊通也知道。  
and Xiong Tong knew it.  
他甚至还想过。  
He had even thought  
哪天把这人请到楚国来。  
about bringing him to Chu someday.  
斗伯比说。  
Dou Bobi replied,  
“不碍事。  
"That is no obstacle.  
我们只要对少师好一点。  
If we treat Shaoshi especially well,  
他回去一说。  
then once he reports back,  
随侯反而会更信他。  
the Marquis of Sui will trust him even more.  
这对我们是好事。”  
That only helps us."  

熊通听了。  
Xiong Tong agreed.  
他热情接待少师。  
He received Shaoshi warmly  
还请他检阅三军。  
and even let him inspect the troops.  
少师一看。  
What Shaoshi saw  
就更得意了。  
made him smug.  
在他眼里。  
To his eyes,  
楚军全是杂牌。  
the Chu army was a rabble（乌合之众）,  
根本没什么战斗力。  
with no real fighting power.  
他回到随国便说。  
Once back in Sui, he declared,  
“楚军尽是老弱病残。  
"The Chu troops are all worn-out weaklings.  
只要我们出击。  
If we strike,  
一定能灭掉他们。”  
we can wipe them out."  
随侯听完。  
The Marquis of Sui heard this  
果然动心了。  
and was tempted.  
可这时季梁又开口。  
But Ji Liang spoke again.  
这个人确实了不起。  
He was a remarkable man indeed.  
连楚国君臣。  
Even Chu's ruler and ministers  
都佩服他。  
respected him.  
只可惜。  
The pity was  
他生在了太弱的小国。  
that he had been born in a state too weak to protect him.  
季梁说。  
Ji Liang said,  
“楚国的强。  
"Chu's strength  
不是吹出来的。  
is not empty talk.  
他们摆明是在诱我们出战。  
They are plainly luring us into the field.  
守好城池。  
Holding our cities  
才是正道。”  
is the proper course."  
随侯听了季梁。  
The Marquis listened to Ji Liang,  
第二天。  
and the next day,  
楚军就撤了。  
the Chu army withdrew.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`时间状语从句`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `If Sui refused, Chu would then have a reason to attack, and once it was subdued, the terms would be much easier to raise.`。<br>
难点拆解：前半句的 `If ...` 说明楚国何时就能名正言顺动手；后半句的 `once it was subdued` 是时间状语从句，交代“打服之后”才进入谈条件的阶段；整句用并列把“开战”和“谈条件”两个步骤串了起来。<br>
常用短语：
- `pay tribute`：朝贡
- `invite humiliation`：自讨难堪
- `courtesy first, force later`：先礼后兵
- `a proper pretext`：合适的借口

### 成立南联盟
### Founding the Southern Alliance

“老斗啊。  
"Old Dou,  
这回算白跑一趟了。”  
so this trip was for nothing?"  
熊通有点失望。  
Xiong Tong sounded disappointed.  
斗伯比却说。  
But Dou Bobi replied,  
“都在意料里。  
"Exactly as expected.  
别急。  
Do not hurry.  
咱们还有第二步。”  
We still have step two."  
斗伯比下棋。  
Dou Bobi played politics  
至少看三步。  
at least three moves ahead.  

第二步是什么？  
What was step two?  
盟会。  
A conference of alliance.  
为何隔一年才开？  
Why wait a year to hold it?  
因为斗伯比。  
Because Dou Bobi  
得到了准信。  
had received reliable intelligence:  
少师升成了随国总理。  
Shaoshi had risen to become Sui's chief minister.  
在他看来。  
In his view,  
傻子当权。  
once a fool comes to power,  
机会也就来了。  
opportunity has arrived.  

于是楚国发出邀请。  
So Chu sent invitations  
请周边各国。  
to the surrounding states,  
来沈鹿开同盟大会。  
asking them to attend an alliance conference at Shenlu.  
名义上的主题。  
The official theme  
无非是加强合作。  
was simply stronger cooperation,  
共同发展。  
shared development,  
大家一起壮大。  
and mutual prosperity.  
可谁都看得出来。  
But anyone could see  
真正的重点。  
what the real point was:  
就是推盟主。  
choosing a hegemonic leader.  
而那个盟主。  
And that leader  
当然只能是楚国。  
could only be Chu.  
这时候。  
At this stage,  
中原诸侯还没收到邀请。  
the central states were not yet invited.  

邀请函一发。  
Once the invitations went out,  
周边小国纷纷前来。  
the surrounding small states came one after another.  
而且多是国君亲自到场。  
In many cases their rulers came in person.  
来的是谁？  
Who came?  
巴、庸、濮、邓、耰、绞。  
Ba, Yong, Pu, Deng, You, Jiao,  
罗、郧、贰、珍、申、江。  
Luo, Yun, Er, Zhen, Shen, Jiang,  
还有十多个国家。  
plus more than a dozen others.  
对他们而言。  
For them,  
楚国就是天然老大。  
Chu was the natural overlord（宗主）.  
楚国放个屁。  
If Chu broke wind,  
他们都得说香。  
they would still call it fragrant.  
楚国开会。  
If Chu called a meeting,  
他们哪敢不来？  
how dared they stay away?  
只有两个受邀国。  
Only two invited states  
没有出席。  
failed to appear:  
黄国和随国。  
Huang and Sui.  

大会举行得很热烈。  
The conference unfolded in a warm and lively atmosphere.  
熊通热情待客。  
Xiong Tong played the generous host.  
斗伯比负责主持。  
Dou Bobi presided over the proceedings.  
各方就共同关心的事务。  
All sides exchanged views  
广泛交换意见。  
on matters of common concern.  
最后由巴国和江国倡议。  
In the end, at the proposal of Ba and Jiang,  
各国一致赞同。  
and with the approval of all,  
南方蛮夷联盟。  
a Southern Alliance  
正式成立。  
was formally founded.  
简称南联盟。  
It was called the Southern Alliance for short.  
各国共推楚国。  
The member states jointly acknowledged Chu  
为盟主。  
as their alliance leader.  
大会极其圆满。  
The conference was judged a complete success.  
会后熊通设宴。  
Afterward, Xiong Tong held a banquet  
款待各国国君。  
for the assembled rulers.  
这一切都很顺。  
Everything went splendidly.  
熊通也很高兴。  
Xiong Tong was delighted.  
他以盟主身份讲话。  
He spoke as alliance leader,  
看着众小国君主。  
watching the rulers of the smaller states  
一个个陪笑。  
smile and flatter him,  
听着四面八方。  
while praise rose  
此起彼伏的吹捧。  
from every side.  
他心里很受用。  
He enjoyed every second of it.  
可即便这样。  
Even so,  
他还是想称王。  
he still wanted to be king.  
而且更想了。  
In fact, he wanted it even more.  

南联盟的谴责信。  
The Southern Alliance soon sent letters of censure（谴责）  
很快送到黄国和随国。  
to Huang and Sui.  
信里指责他们。  
The letters accused them  
破坏地区团结。  
of undermining regional unity  
又警告他们。  
and warned them  
必须立刻改错。  
to correct their error at once  
加入南联盟大家庭。  
and join the Southern Alliance family.  
否则。  
Otherwise,  
后果自负。  
they would bear the consequences.  

黄国国君一看。  
The ruler of Huang took one look  
就知道事情不妙。  
and knew the situation was serious.  
黄国本就是小国。  
Huang was a small state to begin with.  
还是嬴姓子爵。  
It was merely a viscountcy of the Ying line.  
无论如何。  
Under no circumstances  
它都不敢得罪楚国。  
did it dare offend Chu.  
先前没来。  
It had missed the conference earlier  
只是因为心情不好。  
partly because the ruler had been in a bad mood  
一时糊涂。  
and partly because he had been foolish.  
现在看到谴责信。  
But once he saw the rebuke,  
立刻就清醒了。  
he sobered up at once.  
于是黄国国君。  
So the ruler of Huang  
亲自跑去楚国。  
personally hurried to Chu  
当面解释。  
to explain himself in person.  
无非就是说。  
His excuses were simple:  
时间弄错了。  
the date had been misunderstood,  
最近又忙着抗洪救灾。  
and flood control had kept him occupied.  
总之。  
In the end,  
他不仅来了。  
he not only came,  
还认错认罚。  
but admitted fault and accepted punishment,  
哭得一把鼻涕一把泪。  
weeping and sniffling all the while.  
熊通本来。  
Xiong Tong had never really wanted  
也没打算收拾黄国。  
to crush Huang anyway.  
他更想做的。  
What he wanted more  
是拿黄国给随国做样子。  
was to make an example of Huang for Sui to see.  
所以他表现得很宽厚。  
So he acted graciously.  
说了些场面话。  
He offered a few lofty phrases,  
又让黄国。  
then had Huang  
在盟书上签了字。  
sign the alliance document,  
便放它回去。  
and sent it home.  
随国国君。  
The ruler of Sui,  
却始终没来。  
however, still did not come.  

少师对使者说。  
Shaoshi told the envoy,  
“什么南联盟。  
"What Southern Alliance?  
说得好听。  
Nice words.  
我看不过是一帮南蛮子。”  
To me it is just a pack of southern barbarians."  
他根本瞧不起这群人。  
He looked down on them completely.  
斗伯比听后却笑了。  
Dou Bobi, on hearing this, simply smiled.  
第三步。  
Step three  
这就可以开始了。  
was now ready to begin.  

### 南联盟北伐
### The Southern Alliance Marches North

南联盟第一次军事行动。  
The Southern Alliance's first military action  
这就开始了。  
had begun.  
以楚国为首。  
Under Chu's leadership,  
南联盟组成联军。  
the alliance formed a joint force.  
说是联军。  
It was called a coalition army,  
其实主力还是楚军。  
but the main strength was still Chu.  
其他小国。  
The smaller states  
要么出几百人。  
either contributed a few hundred men  
要么干脆只派特使。  
or simply sent envoys  
充当观察员。  
to act as observers.  
他们来这一趟。  
Their real task  
主要不是打仗。  
was not really to fight.  
而是看楚国怎么打。  
It was to watch Chu fight  
好让自己今后。  
and learn to obey it  
更死心塌地听话。  
more wholeheartedly afterward.  

这支南联盟军。  
This alliance army  
由熊通亲自带队。  
was led by Xiong Tong himself.  
浩浩荡荡渡过汉江。  
It crossed the Han River in grand force  
直逼随国。  
and advanced straight on Sui.  
季梁一看。  
Ji Liang saw the danger immediately.  
他劝随侯说。  
He advised the Marquis of Sui,  
“不妨先派人讲和。  
"Better send envoys first and seek peace.  
若楚国肯答应。  
If Chu agrees,  
那自然最好。  
that is best of all.  
若楚国不答应。  
If Chu refuses,  
那就说明它轻视我们。  
it will prove that it despises us.  
到那时。  
And then,  
正义在我们这边。  
justice will be on our side,  
士兵会更愤怒。  
the soldiers will fight with more anger,  
百姓也会更齐心。  
and the people will unite more firmly.  
此长彼消。  
Their spirit will rise as Chu's advantage falls,  
我们未必不能一战。”  
and we may still be able to fight."  
季梁看形势。  
Ji Liang's reading of the situation  
其实相当准。  
was in fact remarkably accurate.  

可少师却不耐烦。  
But Shaoshi had no patience for this.  
“讲什么和？  
"Why talk peace?  
送上门的肉。  
Meat delivered to the door  
哪有不吃的道理？  
is meat you eat.  
主公马上出兵。  
My lord, strike at once.  
慢了他们就跑了。”  
Delay and they will get away."  
他根本瞧不起楚军。  
He did not respect Chu's army at all.  
于是随侯。  
So the Marquis of Sui  
亲自率军出城。  
personally led his forces out of the city  
迎击来敌。  
to meet the invaders.  
战争。  
War  
一触即发。  
was about to erupt at any moment.  

史书里。  
In the grand histories,  
这不算最有名的大战。  
this is not counted among the most famous battles.  
可它的意义。  
Yet its significance（意义）  
却非常大。  
was immense.  
对楚国来说。  
For Chu,  
这既是一场战争。  
this was certainly a war,  
也是一次试探。  
but it was also a test.  
它要借这一战。  
Through this battle  
摸清中原国家。  
Chu wanted to sound out  
到底有多少分量。  
how much weight the central states truly carried.  
若楚国败了。  
If Chu lost,  
那将是它第一次。  
it would mark its first  
向中原扩张的失败。  
failed push into the central world.  
这会重创。  
Such a defeat would badly wound  
它的信心和野心。  
both its confidence and its ambition.  
南方各国。  
The southern states  
也会重新估量楚国。  
would begin to recalculate Chu's strength.  
南联盟。  
And the Southern Alliance  
很可能就此散掉。  
might collapse on the spot.  
所以这一仗。  
That is why this battle  
对楚国只能赢。  
was one Chu had to win.  

对随国来说。  
For Sui,  
敌人已在家门口。  
the enemy was already at the doorstep.  
赢了。  
Victory  
就能保住家园。  
would preserve the state.  
输了。  
Defeat  
就可能亡国。  
might mean extinction（灭亡）.  
这一战。  
This battle,  
谁胜谁负？  
who would win it?  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`并列句`、`并列谓语`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `If Chu lost, it would wound both its confidence and its ambition, and the Southern Alliance might collapse on the spot.`。<br>
难点拆解：`If Chu lost ...` 先把“这仗为什么输不起”讲透；`would wound ... and ... might collapse ...` 是并列推进，把失败带来的两个后果连在一起；中间 `both ... and ...` 又把“信心”和“野心”并列出来。<br>
常用短语：
- `at the doorstep`：就在家门口
- `sound out`：试探，摸底
- `recalculate`：重新估量
- `collapse on the spot`：当场散掉

## 第十四章 一国两制的破产
## Chapter 14: The Collapse of One Country, Two Systems

前704年。  
In 704 BCE,  
楚国第一次。  
Chu fought its first formal war  
和中原国家正式交手。  
against a state of the Central Plain.  
对阵双方。  
The two sides were  
是楚国领头的南联盟。  
the Southern Alliance led by Chu  
和中原的随国。  
and the central-state polity of Sui.  
开打之前。  
Before the battle began,  
先得讲讲。  
we need to say a few words  
春秋时怎么打仗。  
about how war was fought in the Spring and Autumn age.  
因为地域不同。  
Because regions differed,  
打法也不一样。  
their methods of fighting differed too.  
这里先说。  
Here we will start  
中原诸侯的打法。  
with the way the central states fought.  

那时主战方式。  
At the time, the main form of warfare  
是车战。  
was chariot warfare.  
一国出兵。  
When a state took the field,  
通常不先说人数。  
it did not usually announce headcount first.  
只说多少乘战车。  
It spoke instead of how many chariots it had.  
原因有两个。  
There were two reasons for that.  
一来。  
First,  
每乘战车的编制。  
the configuration（编制） of each chariot  
基本固定。  
was basically fixed.  
二来。  
Second,  
战车多少。  
the number of chariots  
本身就代表实力。  
itself represented military strength.  

按《司马法》。  
According to the *Sima Fa*,  
一乘战车。  
one chariot  
配甲士三人。  
carried three armored warriors  
和步卒七十二人。  
and seventy-two infantrymen.  
这些步卒。  
Those infantrymen  
叫徒兵。  
were called foot followers.  
这种搭配。  
This arrangement  
有点像后来的。  
was a little like the later pairing  
坦克配步兵。  
of tanks and infantry.  

车上三个人。  
The three men on the chariot  
各有分工。  
each had a separate job.  
左边叫“射”。  
The man on the left was the archer,  
负责远程攻击。  
responsible for ranged（远程的） attacks.  
中间叫“御”。  
The one in the middle was the driver,  
负责驾车。  
who handled the reins.  
一车四马。  
A chariot was drawn by four horses.  
中间两匹。  
The middle pair  
叫“服”。  
were the inner team,  
两边两匹。  
and the two on the sides  
叫“骖”或“騑”。  
were the outer horses.  
右边那个。  
The man on the right  
叫“车右”。  
was the right-side warrior.  
他拿戈和盾。  
He carried a dagger-axe and shield  
负责近战防御。  
and handled close combat defense.  

这三个人里。  
Of these three,  
“射”和“御”。  
the archer and the driver  
都受过贵族教育。  
were trained as nobles.  
周人的六艺。  
The Zhou six arts -  
礼、乐、射、御、书、数。  
rites, music, archery, driving, writing, and numbers -  
本就是贵族课程。  
were aristocratic（贵族的） subjects to begin with.  
至于“车右”。  
As for the right-side warrior,  
那更得是。  
he had to be  
孔武有力。  
physically powerful,  
忠诚果敢。  
loyal, and fearless.  
所以车上这三位。  
So the three men on a chariot  
都是精英。  
were all elite（精英） fighters.  
这也说明。  
That also shows  
战车有多重要。  
how important the chariot really was.  
不是谁都能上去。  
Not just anyone  
站到上面挥两下。  
could climb aboard and wave a weapon.  
说得直白点。  
To put it bluntly,  
车战很像后来。  
chariot warfare resembled  
一种贵族运动。  
an aristocratic sport.  
战车后面的徒兵。  
The foot followers behind the chariot,  
才是普通士兵。  
by contrast, were ordinary soldiers,  
甚至还有奴隶。  
and sometimes even slaves.  

甲士三人的位置。  
The positions of those three warriors  
平时是固定的。  
were normally fixed.  
但也有例外。  
But there was an exception.  
若国君或主将。  
If the ruler or commander  
亲自坐上这辆车。  
rode on the chariot himself,  
中间位置。  
the middle place  
就归主将。  
belonged to him.  
御者改到左边。  
The driver shifted to the left,  
车右位置不变。  
while the right-side warrior stayed where he was.  
因为主将在车上。  
Because the commander was on that chariot,  
车右就更关键。  
the right-side warrior became even more critical,  
差不多像。  
almost like  
贴身卫队长。  
a chief bodyguard.  
而御者侧身驾车。  
And the driver, controlling the horses from the side,  
技术也得更高。  
needed even finer skill.  
所以《左传》写战争。  
That is why when the *Zuo Zhuan* describes a battle,  
常会顺手交代。  
it often pauses to note  
主将的御者和车右是谁。  
who the commander's driver and right-side warrior were.  

车战开始时。  
When chariot battle began,  
双方战车。  
the chariots of both sides  
先朝对方冲去。  
drove straight at one another.  
先远射。  
First came archery at range.  
车一靠近。  
Once the chariots closed,  
车右便用戈矛交手。  
the right-side warriors fought with polearms.  
随后两车擦身而过。  
The two chariots then passed each other  
继续往前冲。  
and kept driving on.  
拉开一点距离后。  
After opening some distance,  
再来一轮箭。  
another round of arrows followed.  
这个过程。  
That whole pass  
叫“合”。  
was called a meeting pass.  
等车冲远了。  
Once the chariots had gone far enough,  
双方都得再兜回来。  
both sides had to wheel around  
准备下一次冲击。  
for the next run.  
这个掉头过程。  
That turning maneuver  
叫“回”。  
was called the return.  
然后再“合”。  
Then came another pass.  
一个“回”。  
Every return  
后面都跟着一个“合”。  
was followed by a meeting pass.  
战斗就这样。  
So the battle went on  
在“回”和“合”之间。  
in a repeated rhythm  
不断反复。  
of turning and striking.  
直到一方败了。  
Only when one side broke  
它才会在一次“合”之后。  
would it, after a pass,  
不再回头。  
fail to turn back  
而是直接逃跑。  
and instead flee outright.  

正因为逃跑的一方。  
Precisely because the fleeing side  
是在“合”后直接跑的。  
ran straight off after a pass,  
它不用减速。  
it did not need to slow down,  
所以有速度优势。  
which gave it a speed advantage.  
而胜方这边。  
The winning side, by contrast,  
得先减速回头。  
had to slow, turn,  
才能发现对手已经跑了。  
and only then realize the enemy had broken away.  
随后才能再追。  
Only after that could it give chase.  
这样一来。  
That left it  
既吃亏在距离。  
at a disadvantage in distance  
也吃亏在速度。  
and another disadvantage in speed.  
所以古代车战里。  
That is why in ancient chariot warfare,  
想活捉对方主将。  
capturing the enemy commander alive  
其实很难。  
was actually very difficult.  
除非他特别倒霉。  
Unless he was exceptionally unlucky,  
比如车坏了。  
say his chariot broke  
或者掉进坑里。  
or pitched into a ditch.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`原因状语从句`、`时间状语从句`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `Because the fleeing side ran straight off after a pass, it did not need to slow down, and the winning side was left at a disadvantage in both distance and speed.`。<br>
难点拆解：`Because ...` 先解释车战里为什么败军更容易跑掉；中间 `Once the chariots had gone far enough ...` 这类时间结构交代战车动作顺序；后半句再用并列把“距离劣势”和“速度劣势”一起点明。<br>
常用短语：
- `at range`：在远距离上
- `wheel around`：兜回来，掉头
- `give chase`：开始追击
- `break away`：脱离，逃开

### 南蛮完胜
### The Southern Barbarians Win Decisively

随侯这回。  
This time the Marquis of Sui  
几乎押上全国本钱。  
threw in almost the full strength of his state.  
他带来一百五十乘战车。  
He fielded one hundred and fifty chariots.  
粗粗一算。  
By rough calculation,  
总兵力一万出头。  
that meant a little over ten thousand men.  

两军摆开阵势。  
The two armies drew up.  
随侯先看楚军阵地。  
The Marquis of Sui first studied Chu's position.  
这一看。  
What he saw  
让他放心了些。  
put him a little more at ease.  
他本来心里还有点虚。  
He had been uneasy before  
是硬着头皮出来迎战。  
and had come out more on nerve than confidence.  
现在倒真有了点底气。  
Now he genuinely felt steadier.  

楚军这边。  
On Chu's side,  
战车大约一百乘。  
there seemed to be about a hundred chariots.  
不过每乘战车后面。  
But each chariot  
跟着的步兵更多。  
had more infantry behind it,  
大约一百人。  
about a hundred men each.  
总数算下来。  
So in total,  
也还是一万人出头。  
they too came to a little over ten thousand.  
战车数量上。  
In chariots,  
随国占优。  
Sui had the edge.  
总人数上。  
In total manpower,  
双方差不多。  
the two sides were close.  

再看阵型。  
Then came the formations.  
随军分成上下两军。  
Sui's army was divided into upper and lower wings,  
中间空当很明显。  
with a clear gap between them.  
楚军却没有分得这么整。  
Chu's array was not split so neatly.  
再看队列。  
And if you looked at the lines,  
随军像是更训练有素。  
Sui seemed better drilled（训练有素的）,  
队伍整整齐齐。  
its ranks crisp and orderly.  
楚军则显得松散。  
Chu's troops looked loose and scattered.  
兵器也不同。  
Their weapons looked different too.  
随军清一色用长戈。  
Sui's men carried long dagger-axes in uniform style.  
楚军那边。  
Chu's troops, by contrast,  
长枪短刀都有。  
were an untidy mix of spears and short blades.  
随侯见了便说。  
Seeing all this, the Marquis of Sui said,  
“果然是乌合之众。  
"They really are a rabble.  
蛮夷之人。  
What great thing  
能成什么事？”  
could such barbarians accomplish?"  

这时有人提醒。  
At that point one of his officers said,  
“楚人崇左。  
"Chu honors the left.  
楚王一定在左边。  
The king of Chu must be on the left,  
精锐也一定在左边。  
and their elite must be there too.  
我们集中兵力。  
Let us mass our strength  
猛冲他们右路。  
and crash into their right wing.  
只要一举打垮。  
If we break it in one blow,  
整个楚阵都会被带乱。  
the whole Chu line will be dragged into disorder.  
然后再乘乱取胜。”  
Then we can finish them in the confusion."  
到了这个时候。  
Even at this moment,  
季梁其实还有机会。  
Ji Liang still had a chance  
避免随军惨败。  
to spare Sui a disastrous defeat.  
可少师却说。  
But Shaoshi scoffed,  
“不当王，非敌也。”  
"If I do not face their king, they are not worth calling an enemy."  
在他看来。  
In his eyes,  
不和楚王正面对上。  
a victory without facing the king of Chu  
赢了都不过瘾。  
would not even be satisfying.  
楚国先骗了少师。  
Chu had fooled Shaoshi,  
少师又骗了随侯。  
and Shaoshi in turn fooled the Marquis of Sui.  

战鼓一响。  
The drums sounded.  
随军率先进攻。  
Sui attacked first.  
才打了几个回合。  
After only a few exchanges,  
楚军就开始溃退。  
the Chu army seemed to crumble and flee.  
“追。”  
"Chase them,"  
随侯立刻下令。  
ordered the Marquis of Sui at once.  
传说这种东西。  
Legends are tricky things.  
不能全信。  
You cannot believe them completely,  
也不能全不信。  
but you cannot dismiss them completely either.  
随侯当时没想到。  
The Marquis never stopped to think  
若楚军真这么不堪。  
that if Chu were truly this fragile（脆弱的）,  
那些凶名。  
its fearsome reputation  
又怎么会传得那么广？  
would never have spread so far.  

南蛮一向狡猾。  
The southern states had always been cunning.  
随军追了十里左右。  
Sui pursued for about ten li  
便追进一片丘陵。  
and ran into a stretch of hills,  
还是树木很密的丘陵。  
wooded hills at that.  
到了这里。  
Once there,  
战车已经跑不快了。  
chariots could no longer move fast.  
再快就容易翻车。  
Push harder and they would overturn.  
长戈也突然没用了。  
Long polearms also became almost useless.  
因为它们总会。  
They kept getting  
挂在树枝上。  
caught in the branches.  

楚国人本来就不爱车战。  
Chu had never liked chariot warfare in the first place.  
为什么？  
Why?  
因为荆楚之地。  
Because the land of Jing and Chu  
山多河多。  
had many hills and rivers,  
平原却少。  
but little flat open ground.  
雨多晴少。  
It saw more rain than clear weather,  
车本来就难走。  
so chariots were hard to use.  
真到打仗时。  
In actual war,  
双方往往都凑不出。  
there was often not enough level ground  
一块像样的平地。  
for a proper formal formation.  
所以他们最熟的。  
So what Chu knew best  
是乱战和混战。  
was broken fighting and close chaos.  
而这种打法。  
And in that kind of fighting,  
短兵器更占便宜。  
short weapons had the advantage.  
也因此。  
That was why  
楚军单兵作战很强。  
Chu's soldiers were strong in individual combat,  
尤其擅长。  
especially on complicated terrain（地形）.  

正因如此。  
For exactly that reason,  
楚军这次头回对中原用兵。  
in its very first war against a central-state army,  
既然没把握打车战。  
once it lacked confidence in formal chariot battle,  
就干脆用了。  
it simply used  
诱敌深入的办法。  
a lure-the-enemy-in strategy.  
先把随军引进。  
First it drew Sui's troops  
不利于车战的丘陵地。  
into hill country hostile to chariot fighting.  
然后伏兵四起。  
Then hidden troops burst out from every side.  
再靠单兵优势。  
Relying on superior individual fighting skill,  
狠狠干随军。  
they fell hard upon Sui's army.  

到了这时。  
Only then  
少师才知道上当了。  
did Shaoshi realize he had been duped.  
以前只听说。  
He had heard before  
南蛮子很狡猾。  
that the southern barbarians were cunning.  
现在才知道。  
Now he discovered  
那不是一般的狡猾。  
that they were cunning on a much larger scale.  
怎么办？  
What now?  
还没等他想清。  
Before he could think it through,  
四面八方。  
from every direction,  
楚军伏兵就杀出来了。  
Chu's hidden troops came surging out.  
人人手持短刀。  
They carried short blades,  
嘴里喊着。  
shouting  
听不清的口号。  
slogans he could not even make out.  
声势震天。  
The uproar shook the hills.  
随便一扫。  
One quick glance told him  
楚军人马。  
that Chu's men  
至少是随军三倍。  
outnumbered Sui's by at least three to one.  

少师一看不妙。  
Shaoshi saw the danger at once.  
立刻下了决心。  
He made up his mind immediately.  
什么决心？  
What decision?  
逃命。  
To run for his life.  
可对战车来说。  
But for a chariot,  
逃跑本就不容易。  
retreat is never simple.  
“掉头，快掉头。”  
"Turn around, quickly, turn around!"  
少师急忙下令。  
Shaoshi shouted in panic.  
御者连忙回车。  
The driver tried to wheel the chariot around.  
可就算在平地。  
Yet even on flat ground,  
车开快了急转。  
a fast chariot turning sharply  
都可能翻。  
could overturn.  
何况这里还是丘陵。  
And this was hill country.  
少师的车。  
Shaoshi's chariot  
转得太急。  
turned too hard  
当场翻了。  
and flipped on the spot.  
亏得他身手还行。  
Fortunately he was agile enough.  
他拿长戟一撑。  
He braced himself with his halberd  
从车上跳下来。  
and jumped clear.  
可还没站稳。  
But before he could steady himself,  
楚军已经扑到。  
the Chu soldiers were already on him.  

随军本就不如楚军。  
Sui's army had never matched Chu's strength to begin with.  
这时又中了埋伏。  
Now it had also walked into an ambush（埋伏）.  
战车几乎派不上用场。  
Its chariots were nearly useless.  
怎么可能不乱？  
How could it not fall apart?  
顷刻之间。  
In moments,  
队伍喊爹叫娘。  
men were screaming in terror,  
乱成一团。  
and the whole army dissolved into chaos.  
楚军冲上来。  
Chu swarmed over them  
像切瓜砍菜一样。  
and cut them down as if chopping melons and vegetables.  
随军很快。  
Sui's troops  
就被打得七零八落。  
were scattered to pieces.  

少师还拎着长戟。  
Shaoshi still clutched his long halberd.  
他想扔了逃命。  
He thought about throwing it away and running,  
又觉得丢脸。  
but could not bear the shame.  
可拿着这东西。  
Yet with it in hand,  
他又跑不快。  
he could not run fast.  
战场上最怕的。  
On a battlefield, the most dangerous thing  
就是犹豫。  
is hesitation.  
他这一犹豫。  
In that instant of wavering,  
楚军就看出。  
Chu's soldiers noticed  
这里有个随军大将。  
that a major Sui commander was standing there.  
于是一起扑上来。  
So they rushed him as one.  
到这一步。  
By then,  
他连逃的机会。  
he no longer had even the chance  
也没有了。  
to escape.  
他的长戟没舞几下。  
He had scarcely swung his halberd a few times  
楚军已经贴到身前。  
before the Chu soldiers were in close.  
一阵乱刀。  
A frenzy of blades followed,  
把他砍成了十八段。  
and they cut him into eighteen pieces.  

还好随侯本人。  
The Marquis of Sui himself, fortunately,  
本来就押在最后。  
had been hanging back at the rear.  
一看势头不对。  
The moment he saw disaster coming,  
便早早弃车逃命。  
he abandoned his chariot and fled.  
后面还有季梁接应。  
Ji Liang covered his retreat from behind.  
两人这才逃回城中。  
That was how they made it back to the city.  
第一次正面对抗。  
In this first head-on clash,  
南蛮完胜。  
the southern army won decisively.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`原因状语从句`、`时间状语从句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `If they pushed harder, the chariots would overturn, and because the terrain favored close fighting, Chu simply lured Sui in and sprang the ambush.`。<br>
难点拆解：`If they pushed harder ...` 先点出丘陵地形为什么让战车失灵；`because the terrain favored close fighting ...` 再解释楚军为何有把握用诱敌深入；中间多处 `Once ...`、`Before ...` 这样的时间结构，把战场变化压得很紧。<br>
常用短语：
- `fall apart`：崩掉，乱掉
- `hang back`：留在后面
- `in close`：逼近到近身
- `walk into an ambush`：走进埋伏圈

### 和平谈判
### Peace Talks

少师死了。  
Shaoshi was dead.  
战车也折了一半。  
Half of Sui's chariots were gone as well.  
到了这时。  
Only now  
随侯才知道。  
did the Marquis of Sui realize  
自己被忽悠惨了。  
how thoroughly he had been played.  
怎么办？  
What now?  
这时候除了投降。  
At this point, short of surrender（投降）,  
确实没别的路。  
there was no other road.  
于是季梁。  
So Ji Liang  
作为谈判代表。  
came to the Chu camp  
来到楚营。  
as Sui's negotiator（谈判代表）.  

谈吧。  
Well then, talk.  
可兵临城下。  
But with the enemy at the gates,  
还能真谈出什么？  
what real bargain could there be?  
黄鼠狼和鸡。  
What could a weasel and a chicken  
难道能谈成平等？  
possibly negotiate as equals?  
不过别忘了两点。  
Still, two facts mattered.  
第一。  
First,  
楚国君臣很敬重季梁。  
Chu's ruler and ministers respected Ji Liang deeply.  
第二。  
Second,  
楚国根本没打算。  
Chu had never intended  
真把随国灭掉。  
to wipe Sui out completely.  
他们还要随侯。  
They still needed the Marquis of Sui  
去洛邑替他们。  
to go to Luoyi for them  
推动那套一国两制方案。  
and push the one-country-two-systems proposal.  

“不谈。  
"No talks.  
现在谈什么？  
What is there to discuss now?  
早干什么去了？”  
Where were you earlier?"  
熊通先摆架子。  
Xiong Tong put on airs first.  
季梁一听。  
Ji Liang heard this,  
起身就走。  
stood up, and moved to leave.  
先鞠一躬。  
He bowed,  
拍了拍衣服。  
dusted himself off,  
转身便走。  
and turned away.  
斗伯比一把拉住他。  
Dou Bobi grabbed him at once.  

“老季。  
"Old Ji,  
话还没说。  
we haven't even begun.  
怎么就走了？”  
Why are you leaving already?"  
季梁也不绕。  
Ji Liang did not bother to circle around the point.  
他说。  
He said  
随国其实一直。  
that Sui had always  
对楚国心怀敬意。  
respected Chu.  
之前不还签过。  
Had the two states not already signed  
和平共处的条约？  
a peace treaty?  
本来随国也不想打。  
Sui had never wanted this war.  
都是少师那个蠢人。  
It had been that fool Shaoshi  
非要开战。  
who insisted on fighting.  
如今打成这样。  
And now that things had turned out like this,  
随国是真心来求和。  
Sui had come in genuine good faith to seek peace.  
若楚国还是不肯。  
If Chu still refused,  
那他们也没办法。  
then Sui had no choice.  
只好接着打。  
It would have to keep fighting.  
好在他们已经。  
Fortunately,  
郑国、齐国、卫国那边。  
messages had already gone out  
都派人去求援了。  
to Zheng, Qi, and Wei for help.  
三天之内。  
Within three days,  
援兵一定到。  
reinforcements would surely arrive.  
所以只求楚国。  
So all he asked  
给他们三天。  
was that Chu give them three days.  
三天之后。  
After three days,  
再来决一死战。  
they could settle matters in a final battle.  

季梁是什么人？  
Who was Ji Liang?  
这种话术。  
A line like that  
他一个月前就想好了。  
he had prepared a month earlier.  
刚才故意慢吞吞起身。  
That deliberate slowness just now,  
装着要走。  
when he pretended to leave,  
就是为了。  
had been designed precisely  
把这番话。  
to make this speech  
说得更有分量。  
hit harder.  
熊通一下有点懵。  
Xiong Tong was momentarily thrown off balance.  
他没想到季梁。  
He had not expected Ji Liang  
完全不吃自己那一套。  
to be untouched by his bluff.  
随国虽败。  
Sui had lost, yes,  
可守城守三天。  
but holding the city for three days  
多半没问题。  
would probably not be a problem.  
真等到援兵到。  
If reinforcements truly arrived,  
后面就难说了。  
the outcome afterward would become uncertain.  
很显然。  
Clearly,  
这会儿更需要台阶的。  
the one who now needed a way down  
反而是熊通。  
was Xiong Tong.  

斗伯比立刻递台阶。  
Dou Bobi promptly handed him one.  
“主公你看。  
"My lord, look.  
少师这种蠢货死了。  
A fool like Shaoshi is dead,  
老季这种贤臣。  
while a worthy man like Old Ji  
反倒被重用了。  
has now risen to importance.  
这说明天意。  
That shows Heaven's will.  
天不想让随国灭。  
Heaven does not want Sui destroyed.  
天意不可违。  
Heaven's will cannot be defied.  
我看。  
I say  
还是和平为上。”  
peace is better."  
顺手他还。  
At the same time,  
把季梁也夸了一通。  
he slipped in a compliment to Ji Liang.  
熊通马上接住。  
Xiong Tong seized on it immediately.  
“好。  
"Fine.  
既然斗总理这么说。  
Since Prime Minister Dou says so,  
那就给季先生个面子。  
I will give Master Ji this face.  
说老实话。  
To tell the truth,  
若不是季先生亲来。  
if Master Ji had not come in person,  
我本来一定要灭随国。”  
I would certainly have destroyed Sui."  
台阶既到。  
Once the ladder was there,  
熊通下得飞快。  
Xiong Tong climbed down very quickly.  

于是和平到来。  
So peace arrived.  
两国重新签了。  
The two states signed  
新的和平共处条约。  
a new peace treaty.  
不过和平从来。  
But peace has always  
都是有条件的。  
come with conditions.  
楚国开出的条件。  
Chu set two of them.  
第一。  
First,  
季梁要去楚国。  
Ji Liang must go to Chu  
做一名人质。  
as a hostage（人质）.  
出于人道考虑。  
Out of humanitarian（人道主义的） concern,  
他可以带家属。  
he would be allowed to bring his family.  
说白了。  
Put plainly,  
就是楚国看上了他。  
Chu had taken a liking to his talent  
想把人才弄回去。  
and wanted to bring him over.  
第二。  
Second,  
随国国君必须亲赴洛邑。  
the ruler of Sui had to go personally to Luoyi  
向周天子递交。  
and submit to the Son of Heaven  
那份一国两制方案。  
the one-country-two-systems proposal,  
还要把南方诸国。  
together with the joint signatures  
的联署签名一并带上。  
of the southern states.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `If Chu still refused, Sui would have to keep fighting, and if reinforcements arrived within three days, the outcome would become uncertain.`。<br>
难点拆解：前半句和后半句都是条件状语从句，分别说明“求和不成”和“援军赶到”会带来什么后果；中间 `He had not expected Ji Liang to be untouched by his bluff` 这类句子里，后半部分是在补充熊通没料到的内容；整段大量并列短句让谈判时的进退试探更明显。<br>
常用短语：
- `put on airs`：摆架子
- `in good faith`：真心诚意地
- `a way down`：台阶，下台办法
- `seize on`：立刻抓住，顺势利用

### 一国两制的破产
### The Plan Falls Apart

带着那套方案。  
Carrying that proposal  
和南方多国的联署。  
and the joint signatures of many southern states,  
随侯去了洛邑。  
the Marquis of Sui went to Luoyi  
朝见周桓王。  
to appear before King Huan of Zhou.  
别看随国小。  
Sui was small,  
可随侯也是。  
but its marquis was still  
周文王的后代。  
a descendant of King Wen of Zhou.  
真论起辈分。  
If you actually counted seniority,  
他还是桓王的爷爷辈。  
he belonged to King Huan's grandfather's generation.  
没办法。  
There was no helping it.  
周天子历来。  
Successive Zhou kings  
都按嫡长子继承。  
had always inherited by eldest legitimate sons,  
辈分就这样。  
and that kept pushing their generation lower.  
到后来。  
By later times,  
随便来个诸侯。  
almost any lord who turned up  
都能算天子的长辈。  
could claim to be an elder of the Son of Heaven.  

桓王接见了他。  
King Huan received him.  
随侯先献土产。  
The Marquis first presented local specialties,  
问了安。  
offered his greetings,  
桓王也照例。  
and King Huan, by routine,  
关心了几句随国百姓。  
asked a few polite questions about Sui's people.  
寒暄完。  
Once the courtesies ended,  
就进正题。  
they moved to the real matter.  
随侯鼓起勇气说。  
The Marquis gathered his courage and said  
楚国近来在南面。  
that Chu had lately been  
所向无敌。  
unstoppable in the south  
还常来骚扰。  
and often disturbed the smaller states.  
如今大家商量着。  
So the smaller states had discussed the matter  
不如请天子。  
and thought it might be best  
封楚国为王。  
for the Son of Heaven to invest Chu as king.  
这样一来。  
That way,  
楚国也许就不闹了。  
perhaps Chu would stop making trouble.  
说到底。  
After all,  
这也算一国两制。  
this could still count as one country, two systems:  
虽然楚国也称王。  
Chu might also bear the royal title,  
可对外大家。  
yet outwardly all would still belong  
仍属大周。  
to Great Zhou.  
说这些话时。  
As he spoke,  
随侯到底还是。  
the Marquis was still  
要一点面子的。  
trying to save face.  
他不肯承认。  
He would not admit  
自己是吃了败仗。  
that he had come because he was beaten  
才被逼上门。  
and forced into the trip.  
说完之后。  
When he finished,  
他把草案递上去。  
he handed up the draft proposal.  
桓王粗粗一看。  
King Huan glanced through it.  
脸立刻铁青。  
His face turned livid（铁青的） at once.  
他一句话不说。  
Without saying a word,  
直接把草案摔到地上。  
he threw the draft straight to the floor.  

“天无二日。  
"There are not two suns in heaven.  
国无二主。  
There are not two masters of a state.  
哪来的两个王？  
Where would two kings come from?  
一笔还能写出两个姬字吗？  
Can one stroke write two Ji names?  
你这不是吃里扒外？  
Is this not betrayal from within?  
楚国这样公开分裂。  
Faced with Chu's open push toward division,  
你不去拦。  
not only do you fail to stop it,  
反倒还去帮它。  
you actually help it along.  
这不就是助纣为虐。  
That is aiding evil  
为虎作伥吗？  
and serving as a tiger's accomplice.  
你对得起祖宗？  
How can you face your ancestors?  
又对得起你的爵位？”  
How can you face your title?"  
桓王这一通骂。  
King Huan's outburst  
把随侯骂得。  
left the Marquis of Sui  
满头是汗。  
dripping with sweat.  
一句也回不上来。  
He could not answer a word.  

退朝之后。  
After court,  
随侯仰天长叹。  
the Marquis sighed up at the sky.  
他现在就是。  
He was now like  
风箱里的老鼠。  
a rat in a bellows,  
两头受气。  
squeezed from both sides.  
可也没别的法子。  
But he had no other choice.  
他只好买点洛邑特产。  
So he bought a few Luoyi specialties,  
灰溜溜回了随国。  
and slunk back to Sui.  
随后又亲自去楚国。  
After that, he personally went to Chu  
说明来龙去脉。  
to explain everything  
请求对方谅解。  
and beg for understanding.  

熊通气坏了。  
Xiong Tong was furious.  
手里的葱油饼。  
The scallion cakes in his hands  
都被他摔了一地。  
ended up thrown all over the floor.  
费了这么大劲。  
After all that effort,  
结果还是竹篮打水。  
the whole thing had come to nothing.  
“老随。  
"Old Sui,  
这事我不怪你。  
I do not blame you for this.  
不过你得表个态。  
But you must make your position clear.  
你是跟楚。  
Will you stand with Chu  
还是跟周？”  
or with Zhou?"  
随侯立刻答。  
The Marquis answered at once,  
“那还用说？  
"Do I even need to say it?  
周桓王那孙子。  
That grandson King Huan  
根本不是个东西。  
is no kind of man at all.  
我当然愿意。  
Of course  
效忠楚国。”  
I am willing to serve Chu."  
熊通听完。  
When Xiong Tong heard that,  
又问斗伯比。  
he turned to Dou Bobi  
接下来怎么办。  
and asked what should come next.  
其实这时。  
In truth,  
他对老斗。  
he was already feeling  
多少有点怨气。  
a little resentment toward old Dou.  

可斗伯比一点不急。  
But Dou Bobi was not anxious at all.  
因为这一切。  
This outcome,  
本就在他计算之中。  
too, had been within his calculations.  
他慢吞吞地说。  
He said slowly,  
“大王。  
"My king,  
正该如此。”  
this is exactly how it should be."  
熊通一愣。  
Xiong Tong blinked.  
“什么？  
"What?  
你叫我大王？”  
You call me king?"  
一国两制都失败了。  
One country, two systems has failed.  
谁封的我？”  
Who has invested me?"  
斗伯比便说。  
Dou Bobi answered,  
“正因为如此。  
"Precisely because of that.  
我们先前提一国两制。  
When we first proposed one country, two systems,  
是不想分裂天下。  
it was because we wished to avoid dividing the realm.  
如今周王自己不肯。  
Now the Zhou king himself refuses.  
那就怪不得我们了。  
Then the blame is no longer ours.  
若天下真要分裂。  
If the realm now divides,  
责任在他。  
the responsibility lies with him.  
既然如此。  
In that case,  
大王还等什么册封？  
why wait for investiture（册封） any longer?  
自己称王便是。”  
Become king by your own declaration."  
说来说去。  
After all the circling around,  
最后还是落到这里。  
this was where the whole argument had been heading.  
古人其实很重名分。  
The ancients cared a great deal about legitimacy（名分）.  
就算像楚国这样。  
Even a state like Chu,  
真要走到分裂这一步。  
if it was truly going to step into secession（分裂）,  
也得先找个由头。  
still wanted a proper excuse first.  
于是前704年。  
So in 704 BCE,  
熊通自立为王。  
Xiong Tong declared himself king,  
这就是楚武王。  
becoming King Wu of Chu.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`并列句`、`并列谓语`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `If the realm now divides, the responsibility lies with him, and in that case we no longer need to wait for investiture.`。<br>
难点拆解：`If the realm now divides ...` 是条件状语从句，用来给“自立为王”找理由；后半句用并列把“责任归周王”和“楚国可以自行称王”连起来；整段很多短并列句，是为了把斗伯比的政治逻辑一步步推出来。<br>
常用短语：
- `save face`：保住面子
- `slunk back`：灰溜溜回去
- `make one's position clear`：表明立场
- `within one's calculations`：在某人的算计之中

### 弱小就要被灭
### The Weak Will Be Destroyed

楚武王称王后。  
After proclaiming himself king,  
扩张得更凶了。  
King Wu of Chu expanded even more aggressively.  
目的也很明白。  
The aim was plain enough:  
和周朝全面对抗。  
to confront Zhou on a larger scale.  
武王之后。  
After King Wu wore himself out in service,  
熊资即位。  
Xiong Zi took the throne as King Wen.  
文王不比父亲差。  
King Wen was no less formidable than his father.  
他几乎天天。  
He seemed to live  
都以灭别国为乐。  
for the destruction of other states.  
也是在他手里。  
It was also under him  
楚都迁到了郢。  
that Chu moved its capital to Ying,  
也就是今天的江陵。  
in what is now Jiangling.  
这两代楚王。  
Between these two kings,  
灭掉的国家数都数不清。  
Chu destroyed states almost too numerous to count.  
有名有姓的。  
Among those clearly recorded  
就有申、邓、息、权等国。  
were Shen, Deng, Xi, Quan, and others.  
其中最耐人寻味。  
Among them, the most thought-provoking  
是邓国和息国的下场。  
were the fates of Deng and Xi.  
毛主席说过。  
Mao once said  
落后就要挨打。  
that backwardness invites blows.  
这话确实没错。  
That is certainly true.  
弱小就要被灭。  
The weak are liable to be destroyed.  
这话也没错。  
That too is often true.  
若既弱小。  
And if a state is weak  
又手里还有好东西。  
while also holding something desirable,  
那就更容易被灭。  
it is even more likely to be wiped out.  

楚文王二年。  
In the second year of King Wen of Chu,  
也就是前688年。  
that is, 688 BCE,  
楚国出兵伐申。  
Chu campaigned against Shen.  
从地理上看。  
Geographically speaking,  
楚和申并不接壤。  
Chu and Shen did not even share a border.  
可楚就是要打。  
But Chu meant to attack anyway.  
不但要打。  
Not only did it attack,  
还是文王亲自带队。  
King Wen led the expedition in person.  

楚军要去申国。  
On the road to Shen,  
就得经过邓国。  
the Chu army had to pass through Deng.  
邓国在今天邓州一带。  
Deng lay around present-day Dengzhou.  
楚军借道。  
Chu asked for passage.  
邓国热烈欢迎。  
Deng welcomed it warmly.  
一来是怕楚国。  
Partly because it feared Chu,  
二来两国还是亲戚。  
and partly because the two houses were related.  
什么亲戚？  
How were they related?  
楚武王的夫人。  
King Wu of Chu's wife  
是邓祁侯的姐姐。  
was the elder sister of the Marquis of Deng.  
也就是说。  
That meant  
邓祁侯是楚文王的舅舅。  
the Marquis of Deng was King Wen's maternal uncle,  
而且是亲舅舅。  
and a true one at that.  

外甥和舅舅见面。  
Nephew and uncle met,  
先狠狠干了几杯。  
and naturally drank several hard cups together first.  
再说些家常。  
Then came the usual family talk:  
“你母亲可好？”  
"How is your mother?"  
“你外婆很想你。”  
"Your grandmother misses you."  
说到底。  
But to be honest,  
这还是他们头一回见。  
this was actually the first time they had met.  
感情并不会真有多深。  
No deep affection could have existed yet.  
在楚文王眼里。  
In King Wen's eyes,  
邓国就是个小国。  
Deng was merely a petty state.  
他高兴时能灭。  
He could destroy it in a good mood,  
不高兴时也能灭。  
and just as easily in a bad one.  
可在邓祁侯眼里。  
Yet in the Marquis of Deng's eyes,  
这个外甥。  
this nephew  
简直像天上掉下来的靠山。  
looked like a patron dropped from heaven.  
以后就指着他罩自己了。  
He hoped to live under Chu's protection from then on.  

酒过三巡。  
After several rounds of wine,  
楚文王话越来越多。  
King Wen of Chu grew more talkative.  
口气也越来越大。  
His tone grew rougher too.  
拍着舅舅肩膀。  
He slapped his uncle on the shoulder  
竟叫起了“老弟”。  
and even started calling him "little brother."  
酒一多。  
With more wine,  
尿也多。  
there comes more need to relieve oneself.  
邓祁侯去解手时。  
When the Marquis of Deng stepped away to relieve himself,  
骓甥、聃甥、养甥。  
three men called Zhui Sheng, Dan Sheng, and Yang Sheng  
也跟了过去。  
followed him.  
看样子。  
Judging by the names,  
他们大概也是他的外甥。  
they too were probably his nephews.  
看来邓祁侯的姐妹。  
It seems the Marquis of Deng  
还真不少。  
had quite a few sisters.  
这样算起来。  
If so,  
他们和楚文王。  
they were also  
也是表兄弟。  
King Wen's cousins.  

这三人劝他说。  
The three men urged him,  
“将来灭邓国的。  
"The man who will destroy Deng  
多半就是这个人。  
will most likely be this very one.  
若不早作打算。  
If we do not act early,  
将来一定后悔。  
we will certainly regret it later.  
今天正是机会。  
Today is exactly the chance.  
不如趁现在。  
Why not use it now  
把他杀了？”  
and kill him?"  
《左传》就是这么记的。  
That is how the *Zuo Zhuan* records it.  
意思其实很直白。  
And the meaning is blunt enough.  
可邓祁侯没有答应。  
But the Marquis of Deng refused.  
十年之后。  
Ten years later,  
楚国出兵灭了邓国。  
Chu marched out and destroyed Deng.  
亲戚靠得住吗？  
Can kinship be trusted?  
邓国死得冤吗？  
Did Deng die unjustly?  
可后面还有。  
There was worse still to come.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`定语从句`、`条件状语从句`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `The man who will destroy Deng will most likely be this very one, and if we do not act early, we will regret it later.`。<br>
难点拆解：`who will destroy Deng` 是定语从句，用来限定“这个人”是谁；`If we do not act early ...` 是条件状语从句，说明为什么他们劝邓侯立刻动手；后半句再用并列把“识人”和“后果”连到一起。<br>
常用短语：
- `ask for passage`：借道而行
- `to be honest`：说到底，说实话
- `live under someone's protection`：靠某人罩着
- `worse still`：更糟的是，更进一步说

## 第十五章 美女是祸水
## Chapter 15: Beauty Brings Disaster

天下这种东西。  
The realm is the sort of thing  
终究要有德的人。  
that only a virtuous（有德的） person  
才守得住。  
can truly hold.  
这话什么意思？  
What does that mean?  
就是说你若无德。  
It means that if you lack virtue,  
就算一时得了天下。  
even if you seize the realm for a time,  
最后也还是会失去。  
you will lose it in the end.  
其实不只天下。  
And this applies to far more than a throne.  
任何美好的东西。  
Anything beautiful in life,  
你若护不住。  
if you cannot protect it,  
最后带给你的。  
will in the end bring you  
多半不是福。  
not blessing,  
而是噩梦。  
but nightmares（噩梦）.  
美女尤其如此。  
Beautiful women are especially like that.  
所以说。  
Which is why people say  
美女是祸水。  
beauty brings disaster.  
想娶漂亮老婆之前。  
Before you marry a stunning wife,  
最好先看看钱包。  
look at your purse,  
看看身板。  
look at your build,  
再摸摸脖子。  
and then touch your neck.  
若钱包不够鼓。  
If your purse is not full enough,  
身板不够硬。  
if your body is not sturdy enough,  
脖子也不够硬。  
and if your neck is not tough enough,  
那就还是算了。  
then you had better forget it.  
艳福不是人人都受得住。  
Not everyone can bear that kind of romantic fortune（艳福）.  
下面这个故事。  
The story below  
最能说明问题。  
makes the point perfectly.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`并列句`、`并列谓语`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `If your purse is not full enough, if your body is not sturdy enough, and if your neck is not tough enough, then you had better forget it.`。<br>
难点拆解：这里连用了三个 `If ...`，把条件一层层往上加，语气就更重；后面的 `then you had better forget it` 是主句结论，直接给出劝告；这种排比很适合记住英语里的条件推进。<br>
常用短语：
- `in the end`：最后，终究
- `look at your purse`：看看自己的钱包
- `had better`：最好，还是应该
- `bear that kind of`：承受那样的

### 美女是祸水
### Beauty Brings Disaster

蔡哀侯娶了陈侯的女儿。  
Marquis Ai of Cai married a daughter of the Marquis of Chen.  
息侯也娶了陈侯的女儿。  
The Marquis of Xi married another daughter of the Marquis of Chen.  
这样一来。  
That made Cai and Xi  
蔡国和息国。  
relatives by marriage.  
成了拐弯亲。  
They became in-laws one step removed.  
民间有个说法。  
In common speech,  
叫“挑担”。  
that sort of tie was jokingly called "shoulder-pole kin."  
因为陈国姓妫。  
Since the Chen house bore the surname Gui,  
蔡侯的夫人。  
the wife of the Marquis of Cai  
叫蔡妫。  
was called Lady Cai Gui,  
息侯的夫人。  
and the wife of the Marquis of Xi  
叫息妫。  
was called Lady Xi Gui.  

楚文王六年。  
In the sixth year of King Wen of Chu,  
也就是前684年。  
that is, 684 BCE,  
息妫说要回陈国。  
Lady Xi Gui said she wanted to return to Chen  
探望娘家。  
to visit her family.  
路上正好经过蔡国。  
On the way she passed through Cai.  
蔡侯一看。  
The Marquis of Cai saw that  
小姨子要路过。  
his wife's younger sister would be passing by,  
自然热情接待。  
and naturally gave her a warm welcome.  
他设宴接风。  
He held a banquet in her honor  
顺便还想。  
and even meant  
给老丈人捎点土产。  
to send his father-in-law some local gifts.  
坏就坏在。  
The trouble was  
这份热情过了头。  
that his enthusiasm went too far.  

从周幽王那会儿起。  
Ever since the days of King You of Zhou,  
头号美女是褒姒。  
the number-one beauty had been Bao Si,  
她害了幽王。  
who ruined King You  
也害了西周。  
and helped ruin Western Zhou.  
第二号美女是宣姜。  
The second great beauty had been Xuan Jiang,  
她把卫国折腾得。  
who threw Wei  
十年动乱。  
into ten years of turmoil（动乱）.  
如今第三号美女。  
Now a third great beauty  
登场了。  
had appeared.  
谁？  
Who was she?  
息妫。  
Lady Xi Gui.  

蔡侯见到息妫。  
The Marquis of Cai saw Xi Gui  
整个人都看呆了。  
and went blank with amazement.  
他自己的老婆。  
His own wife  
已经够漂亮了。  
was already beautiful enough.  
可和这位小姨子一比。  
But beside her younger sister,  
那简直没法看。  
she hardly seemed worth looking at.  
息妫到底美到什么程度？  
How beautiful was Xi Gui?  
真要用古人的套话。  
If we fall back on old cliches  
像什么樱桃小口柳叶眉。  
about cherry lips and willow brows,  
就太没意思了。  
it gets dull fast.  
还是给个现代参照。  
Better to give a modern reference.  
可以想想赵雅芝。  
Think of Zhao Yazhi,  
就是《上海滩》里。  
the actress who played Feng Chengcheng  
演冯程程的那位。  
in *The Bund*.  

蔡侯本来就好色。  
The Marquis of Cai was lustful（好色的） to begin with.  
如今看见小姨子。  
Now that he saw this younger sister-in-law,  
国色天香。  
radiant beyond compare,  
不吃饭都快饱了。  
he felt nearly full without eating.  
他狂咽口水。  
He kept swallowing hard.  
又多喝了几杯。  
Then he drank a little too much,  
嘴里便开始。  
and his words began  
带起挑逗。  
to turn teasing and suggestive（挑逗的）.  
只是当着老婆的面。  
Only with his wife beside him,  
他还不敢太放肆。  
he did not dare go too far.  
毕竟蔡小陈大。  
Cai was smaller than Chen, after all,  
老丈人惹不起。  
and he could not afford to offend his father-in-law.  

过了一会儿。  
After a while,  
蔡妫去洗手。  
Lady Cai Gui went to wash her hands.  
蔡侯一看机会到了。  
The Marquis of Cai saw his chance.  
他连忙挪过去。  
He quickly slid closer,  
贴到息妫身边。  
pressed in beside Xi Gui,  
开始动手动脚。  
and started pawing at her.  
息妫是什么人？  
But who was Xi Gui?  
那可是春秋大戏里。  
She was one of the major heroines  
排得上号的女主角。  
in the whole drama of the Spring and Autumn age.  
她一边挡。  
She fended him off  
一边轻声说。  
and said softly,  
“姐夫，急什么？  
"Brother-in-law, why rush?  
当初姐姐出嫁时。  
When my sister was married to you,  
我还真想。  
I truly wanted  
陪嫁过去呢。  
to go with her.  
你若真喜欢我。  
If you really want me,  
就别急这一时。  
do not be impatient now.  
万一让姐姐发现。  
If my sister finds out,  
大家都难看。  
everyone will be embarrassed,  
还会闹出外交风波。  
and it could even stir up an international dispute（争端）.  
这样吧。  
How about this?  
你等我一个月。  
Wait one month for me.  
等我回来的路上。  
When I return,  
我先派人通知你。  
I will send word first.  
你在西边找个小城。  
Find a small town in the west,  
我们在那里见。  
and we will meet there  
好好亲热。”  
and enjoy ourselves properly."  
她一边说一边挡。  
She kept speaking while pushing him away,  
听着倒像。  
and from the sound of it,  
也早就看上了这个姐夫。  
she almost seemed to have fallen for him already.  

“也好，也好。”  
"Good, good,"  
蔡侯一想。  
said the Marquis of Cai after a moment's thought.  
这样确实更稳妥。  
This did sound safer.  
既然小姨子都答应了。  
Since the girl had agreed,  
又何必急在今晚？  
why rush it tonight?  
他又咽了咽口水。  
He swallowed again,  
把手收回来。  
pulled back his hands,  
乖乖坐回原位。  
and obediently went back to his seat.  
第二天。  
The next day,  
息妫匆匆上路。  
Xi Gui hurried on her way.  

蔡侯开始等。  
The Marquis of Cai began to wait.  
他在陈蔡交界。  
On the Chen-Cai border,  
找了个小城。  
he prepared a small town,  
把偷情的地方。  
a place ready  
都安排好了。  
for their affair.  
可一个月很快过去。  
But a month passed quickly,  
息妫那边。  
and from Xi Gui  
一点消息没有。  
not a word came.  
蔡侯等得心焦。  
The Marquis grew frantic with impatience.  
他派人去陈国打听。  
He sent people to Chen to inquire.  
这才知道。  
Only then did he learn  
息妫早就回息国了。  
that Xi Gui had long since returned to Xi.  
“上当了。”  
"I've been fooled."  
直到这时。  
Only then  
蔡侯才明白。  
did he understand  
自己被狠狠放了鸽子。  
that he had been stood up completely.  
他万万没想到。  
He had never imagined  
这位小姨子。  
that this younger sister-in-law  
还有这样的脑子。  
had such brains.  
可除了偷偷流口水。  
But aside from drooling in private from time to time,  
他也没别的办法。  
there was nothing else he could do.  
他常想。  
He often thought to himself,  
“老子要是有楚国那么大。  
"If only I were as powerful as Chu,  
我就直接出兵。  
I would march out at once  
把人抢回来。”  
and carry her off by force."  

运气偏偏很好。  
As it happened, fortune answered him soon enough.  
他的愿望。  
His wish  
没多久就要实现。  
was about to come true.  
楚国开始攻打息国。  
Chu launched an attack on Xi,  
而息国。  
and Xi  
连忙向姐夫国蔡国。  
hurried to its brother-in-law state of Cai  
求援。  
for help.  
蔡侯一想。  
The Marquis of Cai thought to himself,  
若我帮息国。  
If I help Xi  
打退楚国。  
beat back Chu,  
岂不是正好。  
won't that give me the perfect chance  
顺手把息妫抢走？  
to seize Xi Gui on the spot?  
这算盘打得。  
It sounded like an excellent calculation.  
甚至可以说。  
In a way,  
连楚国都像在帮他。  
even Chu seemed to be helping him.  
他并不怕楚国。  
He was not especially afraid of Chu.  
为什么？  
Why not?  
因为蔡国和齐国有亲。  
Because Cai was related to Qi,  
他觉得自己背后。  
and he believed  
有齐国撑腰。  
that with Qi behind him,  
楚国总会有些忌惮。  
Chu would think twice.  
于是蔡国出兵。  
So Cai sent troops.  

蔡军浩浩荡荡。  
Cai's army marched in full force  
开到了息国。  
to Xi.  
可四下一看。  
But when they looked around,  
哪有半个楚军影子？  
there was no sign of Chu at all.  
蔡侯心里一喜。  
The Marquis of Cai was delighted.  
这下更好。  
This was better still.  
连楚军都不用打。  
He would not even have to fight Chu.  
直接抢人就行。  
He could simply snatch the lady.  
息国都城城门大开。  
The gates of Xi's capital stood wide open.  
息侯还站在城楼上。  
The Marquis of Xi stood atop the walls  
高声招呼。  
and called down,  
“老挑，楚军撤了。  
"Brother-in-law, the Chu troops have withdrawn.  
快进城来。  
Come into the city  
喝几杯再说。”  
and have a few drinks."  
蔡侯大喜。  
The Marquis of Cai was overjoyed.  
他带着先头部队。  
He led the vanguard  
就先进了城。  
straight inside.  
可才进去三乘车。  
But only three chariots had entered  
城门就猛地关上。  
before the gates slammed shut.  
再一看。  
Then he looked again  
城中涌出无数楚军。  
and countless Chu soldiers surged out from within the city.  
中埋伏了。  
It was an ambush（埋伏）.  
蔡侯就这样。  
That was how  
被活捉了。  
the Marquis of Cai was taken alive.  
前面说过。  
As mentioned before,  
车战时代。  
in the age of chariot warfare,  
主将被活捉并不容易。  
capturing an enemy commander alive was not easy.  
这种瓮中捉鳖。  
Trapping him inside a closed city  
正是少数办法之一。  
was one of the few ways to do it.  

原来当初。  
The truth was that back then,  
息妫在蔡国脱身后。  
after Xi Gui slipped free in Cai,  
在陈国只待了十几天。  
she stayed in Chen only a little over ten days  
便悄悄回了息国。  
before quietly returning to Xi.  
一回国。  
The moment she came back,  
她就把在蔡国。  
she told the Marquis of Xi  
被姐夫调戏的事。  
how her brother-in-law had molested her.  
息侯听完大怒。  
He flew into a rage.  
可怒也没用。  
But rage was useless.  
他又打不过蔡国。  
He could not defeat Cai.  
怎么办？  
So what could he do?  
到了这时。  
At that point,  
息侯想出了个馊主意。  
the Marquis of Xi came up with a rotten plan,  
很像当年申侯。  
a lot like the scheme in which the Marquis of Shen  
引犬戎进镐京那一套。  
had once invited the Rong into Haojing.  
他的办法是。  
His idea was simple:  
请楚国假装来打息国。  
invite Chu to pretend to attack Xi,  
再让息国去向蔡国求援。  
then have Xi call on Cai for aid.  
等蔡军一到。  
Once the Cai army arrived,  
这边设好陷阱。  
they would spring a trap  
把蔡侯活捉。  
and capture the Marquis of Cai alive.  
楚国当然愿意。  
Chu was happy to cooperate.  
它早看蔡国不顺眼了。  
It had long disliked Cai,  
更何况。  
and besides,  
息国还送了不少礼。  
Xi had plenty of gifts to offer.  
这种便宜事。  
Only a fool  
傻子才不干。  
would refuse a bargain like that.  

### 美女真是祸水
### Beauty Truly Brings Disaster

蔡哀侯被押到了郢。  
Marquis Ai of Cai was taken in chains to Ying.  
按楚文王的意思。  
King Wen of Chu's first idea  
本来是把他杀了。  
was to kill him,  
洗干净了。  
clean him up,  
拿去祭祖。  
and offer him in ancestral sacrifice（祭祀）.  
这时季梁出来劝。  
At that point Ji Liang stepped forward to dissuade him.  
“大王不可。  
"Your Majesty must not.

Whatever else he may be,

不吉利。

再说。  
Besides,  
你若真杀了他。  
if you really kill him,  
中原诸侯。  
the lords of the Central Plain  
很可能就会。  
will likely  
因此同心来对付楚国。  
unite against Chu.  
不如放他回去。  
Better to let him go home  
让他替大王。  
and let him spread abroad  
宣扬威严与仁慈。”  
your majesty's authority and mercy."  
季梁如今已是。  
Ji Liang was now a Chu grandee,  
可还是不忍。  
yet he still could not bear  
看着中原诸侯白白被杀。

楚文王一想。  
King Wen thought it over

and decided it made sense.  
于是蔡侯不但不杀。

to entertain him warmly

before sending him off.  

欢送晚宴上。

宾主谈得挺热闹。  
host and guest talked quite cheerfully.  
楚文王用蛮歌蛮舞。

Cai could only smile along.

便问他。  
So he asked,  
“老蔡。  
"Old Cai,  
你说实话。  
tell me honestly:  
是你们中原的美女。  
are the beauties of the Central Plain  
更漂亮。  
the prettier ones,  
还是我这里的美女。  
or are the women here in my lands  
更漂亮？”  
more beautiful?"  
蔡哀侯一听。

and his mind flashed with an idea.  
他心里说。

老挑啊老挑。  
"Ah, old brother-in-law,  
你不仁。  
you were faithless to me,  
就别怪我不义。  
so do not blame me for returning the favor.  
你不把我当亲戚。  
If you do not treat me as kin,  
我也不把你当亲戚。”  
I will not treat you as kin either."  

于是他说。  
So he said,  
“大王。  
"Your Majesty,  
真要论起来。  
if we are speaking frankly,  
你这里的美女。  
the beauties here are indeed lovely.  
不过中原有一个。  
But there is one in the Central Plain  
那才真是绝色。  
who is a true unmatched beauty.  
恕我直言。  
Forgive my bluntness,  
大王这里这些美女。  
but compared with her,  
若和她一比。  
the women here  
简直都不值一提。”  
hardly count at all."  
楚文王眼睛一亮。  
King Wen's eyes lit up.  
哪个男人不爱美女？  
What man does not love beauty?  
“那人在哪儿？”  
"And where is she?"  
他立刻追问。  
he asked at once.  
蔡侯还故意拿一拿。  
The Marquis of Cai deliberately held back.  
“算了吧。  
"Better let it rest.  
就算我告诉大王。  
Even if I tell you,  
大王也未必弄得到。”  
you may not be able to get her."  
“快说。”  
"Speak."  
楚文王急了。  
King Wen grew impatient.  
蔡侯还装犹豫。  
Cai pretended to hesitate.  
“这……”  
"Well..."  
“再不说。  
"If you still won't speak,  
我就杀了你。”  
I'll have you killed."  
这下效果够了。  
That was exactly the effect Cai had wanted.  
他笑了笑。  
He smiled and said,  
“那人就是。  
"The woman is  
我小姨子。  
my younger sister-in-law,  
息侯的夫人息妫。”  
Lady Xi Gui, the wife of the Marquis of Xi."  
“真的？”  
"Truly?"  
楚文王又追问。  
King Wen pressed him again.  
蔡侯说道。  
Cai answered,  
“臣哪敢骗大王？  
"How would I dare deceive Your Majesty?  
上回见了她。  
After I saw her last time,  
我三天茶饭不思。  
for three days I could neither eat nor drink in peace.  
整整一个月。  
For a whole month  
我都没碰女人。  
I did not touch a woman.  
到现在。  
Even now,  
我做梦还会梦见她。  
I still dream of her.  
大王你看。  
Your Majesty, just look -  
我一提到她。  
the moment I say her name,  
我就忍不住咽口水。”  
I can't stop swallowing."  
楚文王听完。

只点了点头。  
he only nodded.  
可那之后。

he had no mind left

for drinking.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`让步状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`时间状语从句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `Even if I tell you, you may not be able to get her.`。<br>
难点拆解：`Even if ...` 是让步状语从句，表示“就算告诉你也未必有用”；后面多次用 `the moment ...`、`after I saw her ...` 这样的时间结构，把蔡侯如何一步步勾起楚文王欲望写得很紧。<br>
常用短语：
- `in his own right`：以其自身身份
- `in peace`：安安稳稳地
- `hold back`：故意按住不说
- `the moment`：一……就

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`时间状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `The instant King Wen laid eyes on Xi Gui, the decision was already made: Xi would be destroyed, and Xi Gui would be taken away.`。<br>

常用短语：
- `lay eyes on`：一看见
- `that was that`：事情就这么定了
- `without restraint`：毫无节制地
- `hardly be bothered to`：懒得去

The younger boy was clever,  
大度。

也果断。

她怎么看。

都觉得比老大顺眼。

我们算一算。
