这一次。  
This time,  
周王室态度很客气。  
the Zhou court was strikingly polite.  
为什么？  
Why?  
因为要分裂时。  
Because when someone is still trying to secede,  
当然要严厉阻止。  
you stop him sharply.  
可若已经分出去。  
But once he has already broken away  
又主动回来。  
and now comes back on his own  
说愿意搞一国两制。  
speaking of one country, two systems,  
那就成了回归。  
that begins to look like a return,  
祖国的怀抱。  
and the mother state  
自然该张开。  
is expected to open its arms.  
打个比方。  
To put it plainly,  
楚国第一次。  
Chu's first request  
提一国两制。  
for one country, two systems  
算分裂行为。  
counted as separatism（分裂行为）  
所以必须拦。  
and had to be checked.  
第二次再提。  
The second request, however,  
就被说成回归。  
could be called a return,  
自然也就欢迎。  
and so it could be welcomed.  

《史记》记载。  
The *Records* says  
周天子赏了胙肉。  
that the Son of Heaven granted sacrificial meat,  
并告诉楚王。  
and told the king of Chu  
去镇住南方夷越。  
to keep the southern Yi and Yue in order  
不要侵犯中国。  
and not invade the central states.  
也就是说。  
In other words,  
南边这块。  
the south  
算是默认交给楚国了。  
was tacitly（默认地） acknowledged as Chu's sphere.  
于是楚国此后。  
After that, Chu  
专心向南扩张。  
focused on southern expansion  
地盘越来越大。  
and its territory grew ever larger.  
到这时候。  
By this point,  
一国两制不但成了事实。  
one country, two systems had become a fact,  
还得到了中央确认。  
it had also won recognition from the center.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`并列句`、`宾语从句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `If he had already broken away and now came back speaking of one country, two systems, that began to look like a return, and the mother state was expected to open its arms.`。<br>
难点拆解：`If ...` 先设定楚国“已经分出去又回来示好”的条件；后面用并列把“看成回归”和“周王室态度转软”连起来；中间 `speaking of one country, two systems` 用现在分词补充说明楚国是以什么姿态回来的。<br>
常用短语：
- `slip quietly into`：悄悄潜入
- `in vain`：白白地，徒然
- `open its arms`：张开怀抱
- `be acknowledged as`：被承认为

### 美女是躲不过去的祸水
### Beauty Is the Disaster You Cannot Escape

宫廷内乱解决了。  
The palace struggle had been settled.  
一国两制也实现了。  
One country, two systems had also become reality.  
那美女还是祸水吗？  
So was beauty still a bringer of disaster?  
俗话说。  
As the saying goes,  
是福不是祸。  
If it is fortune, it is not disaster.  
是祸躲不过。  
If it is disaster, you cannot escape it.  
美女这种祸水。  
A beautiful woman of this sort  
就是躲不过。  
is exactly the kind you cannot escape.  

这一回的倒霉鬼。  
This time the unlucky victim  
是楚文王的弟弟。  
was the younger brother of King Wen of Chu,  
也是成王的叔叔。  
and the uncle of King Cheng,  
王子善。  
Wangzi Shan,  
也叫子元。  
also known as Ziyuan.  
自从楚文王死后。  
Ever since King Wen died,  
子元便接任令尹。  
Ziyuan had taken over as Lingyin,  
而斗伯比。  
while Dou Bobi  
则退到了二线。  
had retired to the second line.  
子元觊觎文夫人。  
Ziyuan had coveted Lady Wen  
早不是一天两天了。  
for a very long time.  
从前哥哥还在。  
While his elder brother was alive,  
他最多也只能想想。  
he could do no more than dream.  
如今哥哥死了。  
Now the brother was dead,  
嫂子又成了寡妇。  
and the sister-in-law was a widow.  
他那颗寂寞的心。  
To his mind,  
难道不该。  
did not such a lonely heart  
有个男人去安慰？  
need a man's comfort?  
子元起了贼心。  
Ziyuan let lust take hold.  
有贼心本身。  
But a wicked desire by itself  
倒还没那么可怕。  
is not the worst thing.  
最可怕的是。  
The worst thing  
有贼心却没贼胆。  
is having the desire but not the nerve.  
那时候斗伯比还活着。  
At that time Dou Bobi was still alive,  
子元很怕他。  
and Ziyuan feared him deeply.  
所以一直不敢妄动。  
So he dared do nothing.  

为什么他怕斗伯比？  
Why did he fear Dou Bobi so much?  
这得先说楚国的格局。  
To answer that, one has to explain Chu's political structure.  
从楚武王的祖父开始。  
Starting with the grandfather of King Wu of Chu,  
楚国国君才渐渐。  
the rulers of Chu  
开始有正式谥号。  
began to receive formal posthumous titles.  
楚武王的祖父熊仪。  
King Wu's grandfather Xiong Yi  
谥号叫若敖。  
was posthumously titled Ruo'ao.  
他的儿子熊坎。  
His son Xiong Kan  
谥号叫霄敖。  
was titled Xiao'ao.  
霄敖的儿子熊旬。  
Xiao'ao's son Xiong Xun  
谥号叫鼢冒。  
was titled Fenmao.  
而若敖的另一个儿子。  
But another son of Ruo'ao  
掌了楚国军权。  
came to hold Chu's military power.  
从那以后。  
From that time on,  
军权就在这一支。  
military authority remained in that branch  
代代相传。  
from generation to generation.  
这个家族。  
That clan  
就叫若敖氏。  
was known as the Ruo'ao lineage.  
有时候。  
At times,  
这一族的族长。  
the head of that lineage  
干脆也直接叫若敖。  
was simply called Ruo'ao as well.  
在楚国。  
In Chu,  
若敖氏的地位。  
the standing of the Ruo'ao clan  
仅次于楚王。  
ranked just below the king.  
可真论军事力量。  
Yet in raw military power,  
他们甚至还压过王室。  
they could even overshadow the royal house.  
若敖氏因受封在斗邑。  
Because the clan had been enfeoffed in Dou,  
便以斗为姓。  
it took Dou as its surname.  
斗伯比。  
Dou Bobi  
正是这一族的首领。  
was the head of that clan.  

后来有一年。  
Then one year,  
成王十五岁。  
when King Cheng was fifteen,  
斗伯比死了。  
Dou Bobi died.  
子元觉得。  
Ziyuan felt  
机会终于来了。  
his chance had finally come.  
大概算一算。  
By rough reckoning,  
就算文夫人十五岁嫁给息侯。  
even if Lady Wen had married the Marquis of Xi at fifteen  
十六岁又被楚王抢走。  
and been taken by the king of Chu at sixteen,  
到了这时候。  
she would by now  
也至少三十三岁了。  
have been at least thirty-three.  
可她哪只是风韵犹存。  
But she was not merely still attractive.  
那简直还在盛年。  
She was fully in her prime（盛年）.  
斗伯比死了。  
Dou Bobi was dead,  
成王又小。  
and King Cheng was still young,  
可子元还是没胆。  
yet Ziyuan still lacked courage.  
怎么办？  
What then?  
子元偏偏还有点诗人气。  
Ziyuan also fancied himself something of a poet.  
他决定先感动文夫人。  
He decided to move Lady Wen's heart first  
让她主动投怀送抱。  
and make her come willingly into his arms.  
换成今天的话。  
In modern terms,  
就是想泡她。  
he meant to flirt her into an affair.  

他在后宫旁边。  
He built a residence beside the women's quarters  
给自己修了座房子。  
and moved in.  
随后养了一群。  
Then he kept a troupe  
歌男舞女。  
of singers and dancers.  
他每天干什么？  
What did he do every day?  
唱情歌。  
He sang love songs.  
唱些什么？  
And what kind of songs?  
无非都是那种。  
The sort that were all basically  
“我爱你”“你寂寞我心疼”。  
"I love you" and "you are lonely and I ache for you."  
反正就是。  
In short,  
专唱给隔壁的文夫人听。  
they were sung specifically for Lady Wen next door.  
他就盼着哪一天。  
He kept hoping that one day  
文夫人会被感动。  
she would be moved,  
会忍不住。  
lose control,  
然后翻墙过来。  
and climb over the wall  
和自己偷情。  
to meet him in secret.  
过了一阵。  
After a while,  
文夫人确实忍不住了。  
Lady Wen really did lose her patience.  
可不是因为感动。  
Not because she was moved,  
而是因为。  
but because  
外面的流氓情歌。  
those vulgar love songs outside  
昼夜不歇。  
went on day and night  
吵得她根本睡不好。  
and kept her from resting.  

于是文夫人派人。  
So Lady Wen sent someone  
去找子元。  
to Ziyuan  
转告他一番话。  
with a message.  
大意就是。  
The meaning was simple:  
先君在世时。  
when the late king danced,  
那是为了练兵备战。  
it was to train for war.  
如今你倒好。  
And what about you now?  
外头那么多敌人。  
There are enemies everywhere,  
你不去收拾。  
yet you do not deal with them.  
反倒跑到寡妇隔壁。  
Instead you stay beside a widow's quarters  
天天唱这些下流歌。  
singing low songs day after day.  
这也太不像话了。  
It was shameful and absurd（荒唐的）.  
子元挨了骂。  
Ziyuan was scolded.  
却一点不恼。  
Yet he was not angered in the least.  
反倒还有些高兴。  
He was almost pleased.  
在他看来。  
To his mind,  
文夫人这不是骂他。  
Lady Wen was not rebuking him,  
而是在提醒他。  
she was hinting to him  
自己喜欢。  
that what she liked  
是有抱负的男人。  
was a man of ambition（抱负）.  
于是子元决定。  
So Ziyuan decided  
要做件像样的事。  
to do something impressive  
来讨她欢心。  
to win her favor.  
做什么？  
And what?  
楚文王不是。  
Had not King Wen only marched  
只打到随国吗？  
as far as Sui?  
那我就打到郑国去。  
Then I will march all the way to Zheng.  
一个男人。  
When a man  
若是为女人发了狂。  
goes mad for a woman,  
真是什么事。  
there is almost nothing  
都干得出来。  
he cannot be driven to do.  
而为了女人发狂。  
And to go mad for a woman  
最终又丢掉性命。  
and finally lose one's life -  
子元不是第一个。  
Ziyuan was not the first,  
也绝不会是最后一个。  
and certainly would not be the last.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`时间状语从句`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `When a man goes mad for a woman, there is almost nothing he cannot be driven to do.`。<br>
难点拆解：`When a man goes mad for a woman ...` 是时间兼条件色彩很强的结构，用来概括整段逻辑；后面的 `there is almost nothing he cannot be driven to do` 看起来有双重否定，其实意思是“几乎什么都干得出来”；整段前面又用很多并列短句，把子元一步步走偏的过程推得很快。<br>
常用短语：
- `in one's prime`：正值盛年
- `move someone's heart`：打动某人的心
- `in secret`：私下里，暗中
- `be driven to do`：被逼得去做，做到某种程度

## 第十六章 孔父嘉的妙计
## Chapter 16: Kong Fujia's Clever Stratagem

楚成王六年。  
In the sixth year of King Cheng of Chu,  
也就是前666年。  
that is, 666 BCE,  
子元率六百乘战车。  
Ziyuan led six hundred chariots  
去讨伐郑国。  
against Zheng.  
六百乘是什么概念？  
What did six hundred chariots mean?  
放在那个年代。  
In that age,  
几乎就是天文数字。  
it was almost an astronomical（天文数字般的） number.  
对楚国来说。  
For Chu,  
这也差不多是。  
it was more or less  
全部兵力了。  
its whole military strength.  

兵虽多。  
The force was huge,  
子元却没胆真打。  
but Ziyuan had no nerve to fight.  
他在郑国待了一天。  
He stayed before Zheng for a single day.  
一听说齐、鲁、宋。  
The moment he heard that Qi, Lu, and Song  
都要来救郑。  
were on their way to help Zheng,  
当夜就撤军了。  
he pulled out that very night.  
《春秋》写得很短。  
The *Spring and Autumn Annals* puts it very briefly:  
“诸侯救郑。  
"The lords came to rescue Zheng,  
楚师夜遁。”  
and the Chu army fled by night."  

遁是什么意思？  
What does `dun` mean here?  
说白了就是逃跑。  
Put plainly, it means to run away.  
遁已经够丢人。  
Fleeing is shameful enough.  
夜遁就更狼狈。  
Fleeing at night is downright disgraceful（狼狈的）.  
楚国立国这么多年。  
Chu had stood for many years.  
胜仗打过。  
It had won victories.  
败仗也打过。  
It had suffered defeats.  
可夜遁这种事。  
But a nighttime flight like this  
还是头一回。  
was a first.  

本来想显得阳刚。  
He had meant to look manly,  
结果阳刚没了。  
but all the manliness vanished,  
只剩窝囊。  
and only humiliation（窝囊） remained.  
回到楚国以后。  
After he returned to Chu,  
子元还硬说。  
Ziyuan still insisted  
自己大胜郑国。  
that he had won a great victory over Zheng.  
可人人都知道。  
But everyone knew  
这一趟丢尽了脸。  
the whole expedition had been a public embarrassment.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`时间状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `The moment he heard that Qi, Lu, and Song were on their way to help Zheng, he pulled out that very night.`。<br>
难点拆解：`The moment ...` 是时间状语从句，表示“一听到就立刻”；里面的 `that Qi, Lu, and Song were on their way ...` 又是 `heard` 的宾语从句；后面的 `he pulled out that very night` 是主句结果，整句把子元的心虚写得很直接。<br>
常用短语：
- `on one's way to`：正在前往
- `pull out`：撤出，撤军
- `put plainly`：说白了
- `a public embarrassment`：公开的丢脸事

### 冤死的子元
### Ziyuan's Bitter End

又过了些日子。  
Some time passed.  
子元眼看美人。  
Ziyuan saw that the beauty  
还是不上钩。  
still would not take the bait.  
他索性搬进了后宫。  
So he simply moved into the inner palace.  
可人是住进去了。  
But although he had moved in,  
胆子还是没有。  
his courage was still nowhere to be found.  
他还是不敢下手。  
He still did not dare make a move.  
他还在等。  
He kept waiting  
文夫人来勾他。  
for Lady Wen to lure him first.  
这样的男人。  
A man like that  
文夫人怎么会看上？  
was never going to impress Lady Wen.  

一连三天。  
For three straight days,  
子元都住在宫里。  
Ziyuan stayed in the palace.  
可他就是不敢。  
Yet he simply would not dare  
去调戏文夫人。  
to lay a hand on Lady Wen.  
活像柳下惠。  
He made himself look like some chaste（守礼的） sage,  
其实不过是胆小。  
when in truth he was only timid（胆小的）.  
那时成王才十七岁。  
King Cheng was only seventeen then,  
也不敢惹子元。  
and he too dared not provoke Ziyuan.  
只能装作没看见。  
So he could only pretend not to notice.  

大夫斗廉看不下去了。  
The grandee Dou Lian could not stand it anymore.  
他直接进宫。  
He went straight into the palace  
去找子元。  
to confront Ziyuan.  
“令尹啊。  
"Prime Minister,  
你不能这样。  
you cannot go on like this.  
你怎么能住这儿？  
How can you live here?  
这不是胡来吗？  
Is this not sheer misconduct（胡来）?  
赶快搬出去。”  
Move out at once."  
斗廉正在气头上。  
Dou Lian was furious,  
说话自然不客气。  
so his words were anything but polite.  

“去你妈的。  
"To hell with you.  
你算什么东西？  
Who do you think you are?  
这是我们的家事。  
This is our family matter.  
关你什么事？”  
What business is it of yours?"  
子元勃然大怒。  
Ziyuan flew into a rage  
立刻叫人。  
and immediately had his men  
把斗廉关了起来。  
lock Dou Lian up.  

文夫人听说后。  
When Lady Wen heard of it,  
知道事情已经。  
she knew the matter had  
不能再拖了。  
gone too far to delay.  
其实她一直在等。  
In truth, she had been waiting  
等子元先下手。  
for Ziyuan to make the first move,  
自己好半推半就。  
so that she could yield by halves  
也留住体面。  
and still save appearances.  
谁知这个废物。  
But this useless man  
不但软。  
was not only spineless,  
还闹出夜遁的笑话。  
he had already disgraced himself with that night retreat.  
照这样看。  
At this rate,  
就算真跟了他。  
even if she did take him,  
以后也靠不住。  
he would never be reliable.  
既然如此。  
Since that was so,  
不如办了他。  
she might as well get rid of him.  

文夫人悄悄派人。  
Lady Wen secretly sent word  
去找斗伯比的儿子。  
to Dou Bobi's son,  
斗谷于菟。  
Dou Guyutu.  
她告诉他。  
She told him  
子元图谋不轨。  
that Ziyuan was plotting rebellion（谋反）,  
还想奸污国母。  
and even meant to violate the queen mother.  
叫他立刻起兵。  
She ordered him to raise troops at once  
除掉奸人。  
and remove the villain.  

斗谷于菟立刻联合。  
Dou Guyutu at once rallied  
整个若敖氏家族。  
the whole Ruo'ao clan.  
半夜时分。  
Around midnight,  
他率家兵杀进后宫。  
he led the clan guards into the inner palace.  
那时子元正在梦里。  
Ziyuan was dreaming then  
和文夫人幽会。  
that he was meeting Lady Wen in secret.  
他猛地惊醒。  
He jolted awake,  
只见刀光一闪。  
saw a flash of steel,  
就身首异处了。  
and in an instant his head was off his body.  
磨磨蹭蹭一辈子。  
He had hesitated all his life,  
死时倒痛快。  
yet died quickly enough.  

第二天。  
The next day,  
成王宣布。  
King Cheng announced  
子元谋反伏诛。  
that Ziyuan had been executed for rebellion,  
并且满门抄斩。  
and his entire house had been wiped out.  
腥味是闻到了。  
He had smelled the meat,  
肉却一口没吃上。  
but never got a bite.  
最后赔进去的。  
What he lost in the end  
却是全家的命。  
was his whole family's life.  
子元这下场。  
That was Ziyuan's end,  
真是又蠢又惨。  
foolish and miserable all at once.  

### 虎喂大的私生子
### The Illegitimate Child Nursed by a Tigress

杀了子元之后。  
After Ziyuan was killed,  
谁来当令尹？  
who would become Lingyin?  
成王先说。  
King Cheng first said,  
“斗廉。  
"Dou Lian,  
你忠心耿耿。  
you are steadfastly（坚定地） loyal.  
你来吧。”  
You take the post."  
斗廉却推辞了。  
But Dou Lian refused.  
“大王。  
"Your Majesty,  
我不行。  
I am not fit for it.  
要让国家富强。  
If the state is to grow strong  
百姓富足。  
and the people prosper（富足）,  
只有一个人。  
there is only one man  
能做到这件事。  
who can do it.  
那就是斗谷于菟。  
That man is Dou Guyutu.  
让他当令尹吧。”  
Let him be Lingyin."  
于是斗谷于菟。  
So Dou Guyutu  
成了楚国令尹。  
became Lingyin of Chu.  
成王为了表示尊敬。  
To show his respect,  
又尊称他为子文。  
King Cheng also honored him with the name Ziwen.  

子文是什么人？  
What sort of man was Ziwen?  
那是楚国的大名人。  
He was one of Chu's great names,  
而且是真正的大名人。  
and truly deserved the fame.  
中原人一向。  
People of the Central Plain  
瞧不起南方蛮子。  
usually looked down on the southern "barbarians."  
可对子文。  
But when it came to Ziwen,  
诸侯几乎一致敬重。  
the feudal lords respected him almost unanimously（全体一致地）.  
这又带出一个问题。  
That brings back an old question:  
私生子是不是。  
do illegitimate sons somehow  
特别容易出人才？  
turn out remarkably gifted?  
前面说过急子。  
Earlier we met Jizi of Wei.  
后面还会说到孔子。  
Later we will come to Confucius.  
现在要说的。  
For now,  
就是私生子子文。  
our subject is Ziwen, another illegitimate son.  

当年。  
Back in the day,  
斗伯比的父亲。  
Dou Bobi's father  
娶了郧国国君的女儿。  
married a daughter of the ruler of Yun.  
父亲死后。  
After his father died,  
斗伯比跟母亲。  
Dou Bobi went back with his mother  
回到了郧国。  
to Yun.  
那时郧国国君。  
The ruler of Yun at the time  
正是他的舅舅。  
was his maternal uncle.  

斗伯比年轻英俊。  
Dou Bobi was young and handsome,  
出身名门。  
born to a noble house,  
肚里也有学问。  
well educated,  
嘴上又会说。  
and smooth of tongue.  
没过多久。  
It did not take long  
他就和表妹。  
for him and his cousin  
看对了眼。  
to fall for each other.  
来来回回几次。  
After a few meetings,  
两人就睡到一起了。  
they ended up in bed.  
那时候。  
In those days,  
哪有什么避孕手段。  
there were hardly any contraceptives（避孕手段） to speak of.  
两个年轻人。  
The two young lovers  
只顾着快活。  
cared only for pleasure,  
结果表妹怀孕了。  
and the cousin ended up pregnant.  

舅妈一看。  
When the aunt saw what had happened,  
急得没主意。  
she had no idea what to do.  
这事不敢告诉国君。  
She dared not tell the ruler of Yun.  
只好偷偷把女儿。  
So she hid her daughter  
藏了起来。  
in secret.  
等孩子生下来。  
When the child was born,  
是个男孩。  
it was a boy.  
可这孩子。  
Yet the child  
也不敢留下。  
could not be kept either.  
这个外婆一狠心。  
So the grandmother hardened her heart  
把外孙扔进了山里。  
and abandoned her grandson in the hills.  

偏偏事情很巧。  
But fate had other plans.  
没过多久。  
Not long after,  
郧国国君进山打猎。  
the ruler of Yun went hunting in the mountains.  
正好看见一只老虎。  
He happened to see a tiger,  
还是母虎。  
and it was a tigress.  
怎么知道？  
How could he tell?  
因为它正在喂奶。  
Because she was nursing.  
喂谁？  
Nursing whom?  
不是小虎。  
Not a cub,  
而是个孩子。  
but a human child.  
郧国国君当场。  
The ruler of Yun at once  
吓得浑身发抖。  
trembled all over in fright.  
还打什么猎？  
How could he keep hunting?  
他慌忙回宫。  
He rushed back to the palace.  

“你怎么抖成这样？  
"Why are you shaking like that?  
猎物呢？”  
Where is your game?"  
夫人问他。  
his wife asked.  
“还打什么猎。  
"What game?  
山里出了怪事。”  
Something uncanny（怪异的） happened in the hills."  
他把经过。  
He told her  
全说了一遍。  
the whole story.  
夫人一听就哭了。  
The moment she heard it, she burst into tears.  
“你哭什么？”  
"Why are you crying?"  
国君问。  
the ruler asked.  
夫人只得招了。  
His wife had to confess.  
“不瞒你说。  
"To tell you the truth,  
那只虎喂的孩子。  
the child the tigress was nursing  
就是咱们外孙。”  
is our grandson."  

“什么？”  
"What?"  
郧国国君立刻带兵。  
The ruler of Yun immediately led men  
又冲进山里。  
back into the hills.  
他赶跑了老虎。  
He drove the tigress away  
把孩子抱了回来。  
and carried the child home.  
夫人一看。  
The moment his wife saw him,  
果然就是外孙。  
she knew it truly was her grandson.  
外婆给他穿的小棉袄。  
The little padded coat his grandmother had made  
还穿在身上。  
was still on him.  
到了这一步。  
After that,  
谁还敢再扔这孩子？  
who would dare abandon the child again?  
老虎都来当奶妈。  
A tigress had served as his wet nurse（奶妈）.  
这命太贵了。  
Such a life had to be precious.  
“快。  
"Quickly.  
准备婚礼。”  
Prepare the wedding."  
郧国国君连忙下令。  
The ruler of Yun gave the order at once.  

第二天。  
The next day,  
斗伯比就成亲了。  
Dou Bobi got married,  
娶的正是表妹。  
and the bride was that very cousin.  
那时候她。  
At the time,  
还在坐月子。  
she was still in her confinement after childbirth.  
至于这个孩子。  
As for the child,  
为了记住这段故事。  
to commemorate the whole affair,  
他取名斗谷于菟。  
he was named Dou Guyutu.  
为什么这么叫？  
Why that name?  
楚人把喂奶叫谷。  
In the Chu tongue, `gu` meant "to nurse,"  
把老虎叫于菟。  
and `yutu` meant "tiger."  
这个名字的意思。  
So the name literally meant  
就是虎娘养大的。  
"raised by a tiger mother."  
平常骂人会说。  
People may curse others as  
“狗娘养的”。  
"son of a bitch,"  
可“虎娘养的”。  
but "raised by a tiger mother"  
在这里反而是夸人。  
was praise instead.  

子文当了令尹之后。  
After Ziwen became Lingyin,  
先做了一件大事。  
the first thing he did was something major.  
他搞了场国有化改革。  
He launched a reform of nationalization（国有化）.  
也可以叫土地革命。  
You could also call it a land revolution.  
子文认为。  
Ziwen believed  
楚国虽然地大物博。  
that although Chu was vast and rich in resources,  
却是民富国穷。  
its people were rich while the state was poor,  
臣强君弱。  
its ministers strong while its ruler was weak.  
中央的力量。  
Central power  
还是不够。  
was still too thin.  
于是他下令。  
So he gave an order:  
所有贵族。  
all nobles  
都要拿出一半土地。  
must hand over half their land  
交给国家。  
to the state.  
子文先拿自己开刀。  
Ziwen began with himself  
第一个交出。  
and was the first  
自家一半土地。  
to surrender half his estate.  
他先做了榜样。  
Once he had set the example,  
谁还敢不跟？  
who dared refuse?  

土改之后。  
After the land reform,  
子文又改了官场规矩。  
Ziwen also reformed official routine.  
他规定官员。  
He required officials  
每天按时上班。  
to report on time every day  
不准偷懒。  
and not slack off.  
那时没有打卡钟。  
There were no punch clocks then,  
子文就自己。  
so Ziwen himself  
每天第一个上朝。  
arrived first at court every morning  
盯着大家。  
and watched everyone else.  

接下来。  
After that,  
他又改官员选拔。  
he reformed official selection as well.  
有才干的人。  
Capable men  
都能被提拔。  
could now be promoted.  
以前那种。  
The old habit  
老子英雄儿好汉。  
of heroic fathers producing heroic sons,  
老子当官儿子接班。  
of fathers in office handing posts to sons,  
多少被打破了。  
was broken to some extent.  
子文因此发现。  
Because of that,  
又重用了不少。  
he discovered and employed  
能干、肯干、苦干的人。  
many officials who were competent, diligent（勤勉的）, and hardworking.  

就这样。  
In this way,  
楚国在成王手里。  
under King Cheng,  
也在子文这套班子手里。  
and under Ziwen's governing team,  
一步步强起来了。  
Chu grew stronger step by step.  
楚国先放一边。  
We can leave Chu there for the moment.  
再回头看看郑国。  
Let us turn back to Zheng  
看看它冒充中央军。  
and see what came  
后来闹成了什么样。  
of its posing as the Central Army.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`让步状语从句`、`并列句`、`宾语从句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `Ziwen believed that although Chu was vast and rich in resources, its people were rich while the state was poor, and its ministers were strong while its ruler was weak.`。<br>
难点拆解：`that ...` 后面整段都是 `believed` 的宾语从句；里面先用 `although Chu was vast and rich in resources` 做让步，再用两个并列对比 `people were rich while the state was poor` 和 `ministers were strong while its ruler was weak`，把楚国的问题说得很清楚。<br>
常用短语：
- `rich in resources`：物产丰富
- `hand over`：交出
- `begin with oneself`：先从自己做起
- `step by step`：一步一步地

### 中央军抗戎
### The Central Army Faces the Rong

郑庄公接受了。  
Duke Zhuang of Zheng accepted  
祭足的建议。  
Jizu's advice.  
他决定冒充王命。  
He decided to act in the king's name  
去讨伐宋国。  
and campaign against Song.  
消息一发出去。  
Once the invitations went out,  
回应算是半好半坏。  
the response was half good and half bad.  
齐国和鲁国答应了。  
Qi and Lu agreed,  
因为郑庄公答应。  
because Duke Zhuang promised  
战后一起分宋国。  
to divide Song with them afterward.  
可卫、陈、蔡这些国家。  
But Wei, Chen, Cai, and the others  
要么看不见好处。  
either saw no benefit in it  
要么跟宋有约。  
or had treaties with Song,  
于是都拒绝了。  
and so all refused.  
不管怎么说。  
Still,  
郑国这支人马。  
Zheng's force  
现在算是中央军了。  
was now the "Central Army."  

中央军刚整好装备。  
The Central Army had barely finished readying its gear,  
战前动员也做完了。  
and the prewar mobilization was done,  
意外却来了。  
when something unexpected happened.  
北边的戎人。  
Rong tribes from the north  
来犯郑国。  
invaded Zheng.  
郑庄公一想。  
Duke Zhuang thought it over.  
讨宋之前。  
Before marching on Song,  
不如先拿戎人。  
he might as well use the Rong first  
来磨一磨刀。  
to whet his blade.  

郑国边境上。  
At Zheng's frontier,  
别人怕戎人。  
others feared the Rong,  
郑庄公却不怕。  
but Duke Zhuang did not.  
他怕什么？  
What did he fear then?  
他怕一开打。  
He feared that once battle began,  
对方就四散逃跑。  
the enemy would scatter and run.  
“他们有骑兵。  
"They have cavalry,  
也有步兵。  
and they have infantry.  
我们靠的是战车。  
We depend on chariots.  
真打起来。  
If the fighting starts for real,  
他们若散进田里。  
and they scatter into the fields,  
我们根本追不上。  
we will never catch them.  
过一阵子。  
Then after a while,  
他们又会回来。”  
they will come back again."  
这正是郑庄公。  
That was exactly  
最发愁的地方。  
what worried Duke Zhuang.  
战车在平地好用。  
Chariots worked well on open ground,  
可人一钻进高粱地。  
but once men slipped into the sorghum,  
就只能眼看着。  
all you could do  
他们逃走。  
was watch them flee.  

这时公子突说话了。  
At that point Prince Tu spoke up.  
“戎人的毛病。  
"The Rong's weakness  
就在一个乱字。  
lies in their disorder.  
看见好处。  
When they see profit,  
他们就抢着往前冲。  
they rush forward to seize it.  
看见不妙。  
When things turn bad,  
谁也不救谁。  
no one saves anyone.  
人人只顾自己跑。  
Each man runs for himself.  
不如诈败一次。  
Why not feign defeat once,  
把他们引进包围圈？  
draw them into an encirclement（包围圈）?  
后面的戎人一看。  
If the Rong in the rear see  
前面的中计了。  
that the ones ahead are trapped,  
一定不会来救。  
they certainly will not come to help.  
那样一来。  
If that happens,  
进了圈套的戎人。  
the Rong inside the trap  
就能一口吃掉。”  
can be wiped out in one stroke."  
公子突是谁？  
Who was Prince Tu?  
他是郑庄公的二儿子。  
He was Duke Zhuang's second son,  
也是最像他的儿子。  
and also the son most like him.  
这主意确实高。  
The idea was indeed brilliant.  
郑庄公立刻照办。  
Duke Zhuang adopted it at once.  

第二天。  
The next day,  
郑军按公子突的计策。  
the Zheng army followed Prince Tu's plan  
故意装败。  
and deliberately feigned defeat.  
戎军没什么脑子。  
The Rong were not subtle（精细的） thinkers.  
果然追了上来。  
Sure enough, they gave chase.  
一追就进了圈套。  
And in chasing, they ran straight into the trap.  
后面的戎人一看。  
When the Rong in the rear saw that,  
果然掉头就跑。  
they turned and fled at once.  
谁还管前面的人。  
Who cared about the men ahead?  
是不是自家兄弟。  
Whether they were brothers,  
是不是亲戚。  
or in-laws,  
都顾不上了。  
none of that mattered now.  
一切都在公子突。  
Everything happened exactly  
预料之中。  
as Prince Tu had predicted.  
郑军大获全胜。  
Zheng won a sweeping victory.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`时间状语从句`、`宾语从句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `If the Rong in the rear see that the ones ahead are trapped, they certainly will not come to help.`。<br>
难点拆解：`If ...` 是条件状语从句，先设定“后面的戎人看到前面中计”这个条件；里面的 `that the ones ahead are trapped` 又是 `see` 的宾语从句；主句 `they certainly will not come to help` 则给出结果，很适合记住英语里“条件 + 判断”的表达。<br>
常用短语：
- `for real`：真的，动真格地
- `give chase`：追击
- `at once`：立刻
- `wipe out in one stroke`：一下子消灭掉

### 讨伐宋国
### The Campaign Against Song

大胜戎军之后。  
After crushing the Rong,  
郑国军队的士气。  
the morale of Zheng's army  
一下高了起来。  
soared.  
半年之后。  
Half a year later,  
也就是前715年。  
that is, 715 BCE,  
郑、齐、鲁三国军队。  
the armies of Zheng, Qi, and Lu  
在宋国的老挑。  
met at Laotiao in Song.  
中央军讨宋。  
The Central Army's campaign against Song  
正式开始了。  
had formally begun.  
明面上的理由。  
Its public excuse  
是宋国多年。  
was that Song had for years  
不去朝觐周天子。  
failed to pay court to the Zhou king.  
其实这理由很虚。  
In truth, the excuse was thin.  
那时候根本没人。  
At that time almost no one  
去朝觐周王。  
paid court to the Zhou ruler.  
谁去谁才傻。  
Only a fool would have done it.  

郑庄公亲自带队。  
Duke Zhuang himself led Zheng.  
齐国派了夷仲年。  
Qi sent Yizhong Nian,  
那是国君的弟弟。  
the ruler's younger brother.  
鲁国派了公子翚。  
Lu sent Prince Hui（翚）,  
也是国君的弟弟。  
who was likewise the ruler's brother.  
三国兵马扑上去。  
The forces of the three states lunged forward  
像狼一样凶。  
like wolves.  
没费多大工夫。  
It took little effort  
就拿下了郜城。  
to seize Gao  
和防城。  
and Fang.  

宋殇公这下慌了。  
Duke Shang of Song panicked.  
照这个势头。  
At this rate,  
宋国真可能。  
Song might truly  
保不住了。  
fail to survive.  
那要不要投降？  
So should he surrender?  
在这件事上。  
On that point,  
宋殇公反倒清醒。  
Duke Shang was perfectly clear-headed.  
他很明白。  
He understood perfectly well  
郑庄公是什么样的人。  
what kind of man Duke Zhuang was.  
那是个有仇必报。  
He was a man who always repaid grudges  
下手又狠的人。  
and struck without mercy.  
现在投降。  
To surrender now  
几乎等于送死。  
would be almost the same as asking to die.  

关键时候。  
At that crucial moment,  
孔父嘉站出来了。  
Kong Fujia stepped forward.  
“主公。  
"My lord,  
我有一计。  
I have a plan.  
可以让三国军队。  
It can make the armies of all three states  
不战自退。”  
withdraw without another battle."  
孔父嘉是谁？  
Who was Kong Fujia?  
他是宋国司马。  
He was Song's Sima（司马）,  
也就是国防部长。  
in effect the minister of war.  
“快讲。”  
"Speak at once."  

“郑庄公来打我国。  
"Duke Zhuang has attacked us  
几乎把精锐都带来了。  
with almost all of Zheng's elite troops.  
如今主公在这里。  
If Your Lordship holds out here  
坚守城池。  
and keeps the city,  
我带两百乘战车。  
I will lead two hundred chariots  
悄悄出城。  
secretly out of the city,  
去联结卫国、蔡国。  
link up with Wei and Cai,  
再偷袭郑都。  
and then strike Zheng's capital.  
郑军一定回救。  
The Zheng army will certainly march back to save it.  
郑军一撤。  
Once Zheng withdraws,  
齐军和鲁军。  
the armies of Qi and Lu  
也只能撤走。  
will have to withdraw as well.  
我这一招。  
This move of mine  
就叫围郑救宋。”  
is called besieging Zheng to save Song."  
这主意确实厉害。  
The plan was indeed brilliant.  
后来孙膑的。  
Sun Bin's later  
围魏救赵。  
"besiege Wei to rescue Zhao"  
说穿了。  
to tell the truth,  
不过也是。  
was no more than  
这招的一个翻版。  
a variation on this move.  
“老孔。  
"Old Kong,  
你真有才。”  
you truly are gifted."  
宋殇公立刻照办。  
Duke Shang of Song acted on it at once.  

趁着夜色。  
Under cover of night,  
孔父嘉率宋军主力。  
Kong Fujia led Song's main force  
悄悄开拔。  
quietly out of the city.  
同时快马去卫国。  
At the same time, riders sped to Wei  
请求援兵。  
to ask for reinforcements.  
卫国果然出兵。  
Wei did send troops.  
卫宣公出了两百乘车。  
Duke Xuan of Wei provided two hundred chariots  
派右宰丑领军。  
and sent Youzai Chou to command them.  
两国军队会合后。  
After the two armies joined forces,  
便一起逼近荥阳。  
they advanced together on Xingyang.  

荥阳的守将。  
The defender of Xingyang  
是郑国太子公子忽。  
was Zheng's crown prince, Prince Hu.  
他一面坚守。  
He held the city fast  
一面急报求援。  
while urgently sending for help.  
郑庄公接到急报。  
When Duke Zhuang received the urgent report,  
只得立刻撤军。  
he had no choice but to withdraw at once.  

夷仲年和公子翚。  
Yizhong Nian and Prince Hui  
都不明白怎么回事。  
could not understand what had happened.  
两人都来问。  
Both came and asked,  
“老郑。  
"Old Zheng,  
打得好好的。  
things were going well.  
怎么说撤就撤？”  
Why retreat all of a sudden?"  
郑庄公嘴上。  
Duke Zhuang, of course,  
当然要说漂亮话。  
had to dress it up in fine words.  
“两位公子。  
"My two princes,  
宋国虽然有罪。  
though Song has done wrong,  
我们教训它一下。  
it is enough to teach it a lesson  
让它记住教训。  
and make it remember.  
也就可以了。  
That should suffice.  
何必把人逼绝？  
Why force a man to the wall?  
治病救人嘛。  
We should cure the illness and save the man.  
再说。  
Besides,  
宋君也是公爵。  
the ruler of Song is a duke.  
真要灭了他。  
If we truly destroyed him,  
我们心里。  
we ourselves  
也有些不忍。”  
would find it hard to stomach."  
他说得很体面。  
He made it sound lofty and decent,  
就是不肯承认。  
but refused to admit  
自己是被逼撤军。  
that he had been forced to retreat.  
夷仲年和公子翚。  
After hearing him,  
听完都觉得。  
Yizhong Nian and Prince Hui both felt  
这位老郑真厚道。  
that this old Zheng was a very decent fellow.  

“这样吧。  
"How about this?  
虽然这次讨宋。  
Though this campaign against Song  
也是奉周王之命。  
was carried out in the Zhou king's name,  
替天行道。  
to uphold justice,  
可两位今日。  
the righteous zeal（正义热情） you two showed today  
见义勇为。  
in standing up for what is right,  
又嫉恶如仇。  
and in hating evil like a personal foe,  
实在令人佩服。  
is deeply admirable.  
如今拿下两城。  
Now that two cities have been taken,  
齐国拿郜城。  
Qi may take Gao,  
鲁国拿防城。  
and Lu may take Fang.  
如何？”  
What do you say?"  
郑庄公开始分赃了。  
Duke Zhuang had begun to divide the spoils.  
夷仲年和公子翚。  
The two princes  
一听更感动。  
were even more moved when they heard this.  
郑国出力最多。  
Zheng had done the most fighting,  
结果自己一分不要。  
yet claimed none of the reward for itself.  
这是什么精神？  
What kind of spirit was that?  
这就是无私奉献。  
It was the spirit of selfless devotion（无私奉献）.  

夷仲年最受感动。  
Yizhong Nian was moved the most.  
他决定让出郜城。  
He decided to give Gao away.  
当然。  
To be fair,  
郜城本来也不挨齐国。  
Gao did not border Qi anyway.  
“老郑。  
"Old Zheng,  
郜城我不要。  
I don't want Gao.  
还是你拿吧。”  
You take it."  
“我也不要。”  
"I don't want it either."  
郑庄公硬是不要。  
Duke Zhuang flatly refused.  
几番推让之后。  
After several rounds of polite refusal,  
郑庄公说。  
Duke Zhuang said,  
“那这样。  
"Then let it be this way.  
你不要。  
If you don't want it,  
我也不要。  
and I don't want it,  
那就都给鲁国吧。”  
then let both cities go to Lu."  
结果两座城。  
In the end, both cities  
都归了鲁国。  
went to Lu.  
公子翚为什么。  
Why did Prince Hui  
没有跟着推让？  
not refuse in turn?  
难道他没感动？  
Was he not moved?  
当然也感动。  
Of course he was.  
可感动归感动。  
But being moved is one thing,  
冲动归冲动。  
and acting rashly is another.  
他没有冲动。  
He did not lose his head.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`时间状语从句`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `If Your Lordship holds out here and keeps the city, I will lead two hundred chariots secretly out of the city, link up with Wei and Cai, and then strike Zheng's capital.`。<br>
难点拆解：这里先用 `If Your Lordship holds out here and keeps the city` 提出条件；后面主句一口气并列了三个动作 `lead ... out`、`link up with ...`、`strike ...`，非常适合拿来练“一个主句带多个并列谓语”的结构。<br>
常用短语：
- `hold out`：坚守
- `link up with`：与……会合
- `have no choice but to`：别无选择只能
- `divide the spoils`：分战利品

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`宾语从句`、`原因状语从句`、`祈使句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `Never imagine that because the tiger is the wolf's enemy, the tiger will save you.`。<br>
难点拆解：句子开头的 `Never imagine ...` 是祈使句；里面 `that ... the tiger will save you` 是 `imagine` 的宾语从句；而 `because the tiger is the wolf's enemy` 又嵌在从句内部，说明人为什么会产生这种错觉。这个结构很适合练“主句里套宾语从句，从句里再套原因状语”的层次。<br>
常用短语：
- `for no good reason`：无缘无故
- `barged in`：闯进去
- `make up one's mind`：下定决心
- `guard against`：提防，防备

## 第十七章 美男也是祸水
## Chapter 17: Handsome Men Bring Trouble Too

打完宋国。  
After beating Song,  
吞了戴国。  
swallowing Dai,  
又顺手重创了。  
and smashing the main forces  
卫国和宋国主力。  
of Wei and Song along the way,  
郑庄公当然高兴。  
Duke Zhuang of Zheng was naturally delighted.  
更让他高兴的是。  
What pleased him even more  
他摸出了一套。  
was that he had found  
成功模式。  
a winning formula（模式）.  
说白了。  
Put simply,  
就是拉上齐国和鲁国。  
it meant pulling Qi and Lu into the alliance.  
庄公决定。  
Duke Zhuang decided  
照着这个法子。  
to use the same method  
再打一仗。  
for another campaign.  
这回打谁？  
Whom this time?  
许国。  
Xu.  
这个小国。  
This petty state  
总爱和郑国作对。  
was always making trouble for Zheng.  
现在正是。  
Now was exactly  
灭它的机会。  
the time to wipe it out.  
于是黄金搭档。  
So the golden partnership（黄金搭档）  
又一次凑齐了。  
came together once again.  
郑庄公和齐鲁两位公子。  
Duke Zhuang and the two princes from Qi and Lu  
再次联手出兵。  
joined forces once more.  
和伐宋不同的是。  
The only difference from the campaign against Song  
这一回不用。  
was that this time  
假传王命。  
there was no need to fake the king's order.  
打这么个小国。  
For a state this small,  
还犯不着。  
there was no need  
冒充中央军。  
to pose as the Central Army.  

### 超男选秀
### The Super-Boy Tryouts

仗打得多了。  
Once you have fought enough wars,  
手下猛将也就多了。  
great generals pile up under you.  
谁来当前锋。  
Then choosing the vanguard（先锋）  
反而成了难题。  
becomes the hard part.  
这也算。  
That too counts as  
幸福的烦恼。  
a happy trouble.  
偏偏庄公。  
And Duke Zhuang,  
还是个讲公道的人。  
was a man who cared about fairness.  
凡事都想端平。  
He wanted to treat everyone evenly.  
一碰上难题。  
Whenever a problem came up,  
他就找祭足。  
he turned to Jizu.  
那天祭足正在家里。

and asked point-blank,

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`主语从句`、`并列谓语`、`定语从句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `Whoever can take the banner down, carry it in hand, and still walk as steadily as usual will have the chariot and the seal of the vanguard as well.`。<br>
难点拆解：句首的 `Whoever ...` 整体充当主语，意思是“凡是能做到这一点的人”；中间连续用了三个并列动作 `take`、`carry`、`walk`，把比赛要求一口气列出来；后面的 `will have ...` 才是主句结论。<br>
常用短语：
- `turn to someone`：去找某人帮忙
- `point-blank`：直截了当地
- `treat everyone evenly`：一碗水端平
- `limited edition`：绝版

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`时间状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `By the time the thought flashed through his mind, the arrow in his hand was already in flight.`。<br>
难点拆解：`By the time ...` 是时间状语从句，表示“等到他刚想到这里时”；后面的主句 `the arrow ... was already in flight` 写出动作已经发生，带出一种“来不及收手”的感觉；前面整段又用很多短并列句，把子都的嫉恨推进得很快。<br>
常用短语：
- `at that very moment`：就在那个时候
- `look up`：抬头看
- `to hell with`：去他的
- `in flight`：飞行中，已经射出

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`让步状语从句`、`不定式结构`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `Even the finest archer, trying from below to drive an arrow clean through a man on the wall, would need astonishing strength and bone-deep hatred.`。<br>
难点拆解：`Even the finest archer` 先做让步强调，表示“就算是最强的射手也一样”；中间 `trying from below ...` 是补充说明射箭的具体情境；主句核心是 `would need ...`，把祭足推理的重点落在“这不是普通人做得到的”上。<br>
常用短语：
- `from behind`：从后面
- `work backward`：倒推，逆向推断
- `bone-deep`：刻骨的
- `drag out the killer`：把凶手揪出来

庄公听完。  
Duke Zhuang heard that  
当场大怒。  
and flew into a rage on the spot.  
其实他自己。  
In truth, he himself  
也早猜到。  
had already guessed  
就是公孙子都。  
that it was Gongsun Zidu.  
“杀。”  
"Kill him."  
庄公毫不客气。  
Duke Zhuang did not hesitate.  
自己的亲弟弟。  
He had never spared even his own brothers,  
都能下手。  
so a cousin meant even less.  
何况只是堂弟。  
All the more so since Zidu was only a cousin.  
可祭足拦住了他。  
But Jizu stopped him.  
“主公，不可。”  
"My lord, you must not."  
祭足讲了两条理由。  
Jizu gave two reasons.  
第一。  
First,  
这事死无对证。  
the case lacked living witnesses.  
子都若死不认账。  
If Zidu flatly denied it,  
你杀了他。  
and then you killed him,  
反倒成全了他。  
you would only make him look innocent.  
说不定还会变成。  
He might even become  
史上最冤的大将。  
the most wronged general in history.  
第二。  
Second,  
子都是公族。  
Zidu belonged to the ruling clan,  
有战功。  
had military merit,  
还是个大美男。  
and was also a famously handsome man.  
真把他杀了。  
If you truly killed him,  
许多不明真相的人。  
many who knew nothing of the truth

would surely resent it.  
甚至还会说。  
Some might even say  
他是因为太出众。  
he had been killed  
才遭人嫉妒。  
simply because others envied him.  
“那怎么办？”  
"Then what do we do?"  
“我有办法。”  
"I have a way."  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `If Zidu flatly denied it, and then you killed him, you would only make him look innocent.`。<br>
难点拆解：这里前面两个动作 `Zidu flatly denied it` 和 `you killed him` 被并列放进条件里，后面主句 `you would only make him look innocent` 给出反效果；整句很适合记“如果你这样做，结果反而会怎样”的表达。<br>
常用短语：
- `flatly deny`：一口否认
- `look innocent`：显得无辜
- `military merit`：战功
- `know nothing of the truth`：不了解真相

三个月一晃就过。

他话还没说完。

祭足在旁边。

### 郑庄公死了
### Duke Zhuang Nears the End

人都会死。  
Everyone dies.  
英雄也不例外。  
Heroes are no exception.  
春秋的一号男主。  
The leading man of the Spring and Autumn age  
如今也到了。  
had now reached  
这一关。  
that point.  
从出场到谢幕。  
From entrance to exit,  
郑庄公的形象。  
Duke Zhuang's image  
几乎一直很完整。  
had remained almost flawless（无懈可击的）.  
他基本无遗策。  
He was almost never without a plan,  
也几乎战无不胜。  
and he was hardly ever defeated.  
他又很会用人。  
He also knew how to use talent,  
手下尽是能人。  
so able men gathered under him.  
可再厉害的人。  
But no matter how formidable（厉害的） a man is,  
也得死。  
he too must die.  
郑庄公四十三年。  
In Duke Zhuang's forty-third year,  
也就是前701年。  
that is, 701 BCE,  
他五十六岁。  
he was fifty-six.  
五十六就死。  
Dying at fifty-six  
今天看嫌早。  
seems early to us today.  
可放在那时。  
But for that age,  
也算正常。  
it was fairly normal.  
古人说。  
The ancients said  
人生七十古来稀。  
that to reach seventy was rare.  
那时能活到五十六。  
In those days, reaching fifty-six  
已经不算短命。  
was not especially short-lived.  

顺便说一句。  
As an aside,  
上古长寿的人。  
earlier long-lived rulers  
也不是没有。  
did exist.  
彭祖传说。  
Peng Zu was said in legend  
活了七百多岁。  
to have lived over seven hundred years,  
不过那只是传说。  
but that is only legend.  
《史记》里。  
In the *Records of the Grand Historian*,  
尧大约活到。  
Yao seems to have lived to about  
一百三十岁。  
one hundred and thirty.  
舜活到一百岁。  
Shun lived to one hundred.  
周文王至少八十。  
King Wen of Zhou was at least eighty.  
周穆王也有。  
King Mu of Zhou reached  
一百零五岁。  
one hundred and five.  
可不管别人多长寿。  
But no matter how long others lived,  
郑庄公只能到。  
Duke Zhuang would go only to  
五十六岁。  
fifty-six.  
在觉得自己。  
When he felt  
真的快不行之前。  
that he truly did not have long left,  
他把祭足找来。  
he summoned Jizu  
商量后事。  
to discuss what should come after.  

“老祭。  
"Old Jizu,  
看样子我不行了。  
it looks as though I am finished.  
我这十一个儿子里。  
Among my eleven sons,  
谁继位最合适？”  
who is most fit to succeed me?"  
祭足先是一愣。  
Jizu was startled.  
世子不是公子忽吗？

世子继位。  
For the crown prince to succeed  
不是顺理成章？

如今庄公这么问。  
Since Duke Zhuang asked this now,  
意思就不简单了。  
his meaning was not simple.  
祭足是什么人？

他转念就明白。  
He understood at once  
庄公其实是。  
that Duke Zhuang was in fact  
对公子忽。  
not fully satisfied  
不太满意。  
with Prince Hu.  
可祭足还是说。  
Even so, Jizu said,  
“当然是公子忽。  
"Of course Prince Hu.  
公子忽能文能武。  
Prince Hu is skilled in letters and arms,  
为人也谨慎。  
and careful in conduct.  
还有谁。  
Who else  
比他更合适？”  
could be more suitable?"  
祭足知道。  
Jizu knew  
这答案未必。  
that the answer would not necessarily  
能让庄公开心。  
please Duke Zhuang.  
可这时候。  
But at this moment,  
他必须挺忽。  
he had to back Hu.  
因为忽的母亲邓曼。  
Because Hu's mother, Deng Man,  
当年正是他。  
had been brought to Duke Zhuang  
帮庄公娶回来的。  
through Jizu's help.  
从情分上说。  
In terms of personal feeling,  
他也更希望。  
he genuinely hoped  
公子忽接班。

庄公听后说道。  
Duke Zhuang replied,  
“老祭。  
"Old Jizu,  
实话跟你说。  
to tell you the truth,  
忽是不错。  
Hu is indeed good.  
我也喜欢他。  
I like him too.  
可你知道。  
But do you know  
政治家最要紧的。  
what matters most in a politician?  
是什么吗？  
What is it?  
是心要黑。  
The heart must be dark,  
手要狠。  
and the hand must be ruthless.  
忽太善了。  
Hu is too kind.  
反倒是突。  
By contrast, Tu  
虽不如哥哥能干。  
is not as capable as his elder brother,  
但做事果断。  
yet he is decisive,  
下手也硬。  
and hard when he acts.  
我更看好他。”  
I think more highly of him."  
庄公看人。  
Duke Zhuang's eye for people  
其实一向很准。  
was usually very accurate.  
老二公子突。  
His second son, Prince Tu,  
本事差一点。  
might have been a little less capable,  
可性格确实。  
but his temperament  
更像他。  
truly resembled his father's more.  
祭足却答道。  
Jizu, however, answered,  
“不可。  
"That must not be.  
主公难道忘了。  
Has Your Lordship forgotten

what happened in Song and Lu?  
接班人。  
The heir  
一定要立长子。  
must be the eldest son,  
这样众人才服。”  
or the people will not submit."  
其实祭足心里也清楚。

公子突。  
that Prince Tu  
也许更像。  
might be closer to

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`宾语从句`、`并列句`、`表语结构`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `The heart must be dark, and the hand must be ruthless.`。<br>
难点拆解：这句话前面先用 `what matters most in a politician` 提出问题，后面直接用两个并列短句作回答；`must be dark` 和 `must be ruthless` 都是非常强硬的表语结构，语气很像郑庄公本人。<br>
常用短语：
- `to tell you the truth`：说实话
- `be skilled in letters and arms`：能文能武
- `think highly of`：更看重，更看好
- `a fit ruler`：合格的君主

庄公想了又想。  
Duke Zhuang thought it over again and again.  
最后还是接受了。  
In the end he accepted  
祭足的意见。  
Jizu's advice.  
“这样的话。  
"If so,  
只能先把突。  
the only thing to do is send Tu  
送去姥姥家了。  
to his grandmother's side first.  
不然一定生乱。  
Otherwise trouble will surely come.  
只是这样做。  
Even so,  
恐怕也未必。  
I fear this still may not  
真能避祸。”  
prevent disaster."  
第二天。  
The next day,  
公子突就被送出郑国。  
Prince Tu was sent out of Zheng  
去了宋国。  
to Song.  
因为他的外祖家。  
His mother's family  
本就是宋国大族。  
was a great clan of Song.  
有人会问。  
Someone may ask,  
郑宋不是仇国吗？  
were Zheng and Song not enemies?  
把儿子送去。  
Was sending a son there  
不就等于送人质？  
not the same as handing over a hostage?  
可那是以前。  
But that was before.  
此一时彼一时。  
Times had changed.  
到了这会儿。  
At this moment,  
郑国和宋国。  
Zheng and Song  
好得不得了。  
were on excellent terms,  
简直像同志加兄弟。  
almost like comrades and brothers.  
为什么会这么好？  
Why were they suddenly so close?  
这就得先说。  
To explain that,  
还要回到。  
we have to go back to  
宋国九年前。  
what happened in Song  
发生的那件事。  
nine years earlier.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`不定式结构`、`原因状语从句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `If so, the only thing to do is send Tu to his grandmother's side first.`。<br>
难点拆解：`If so` 把前面“既然只能立长子”的结论接过来；后面的 `the only thing to do is ...` 是很常见的不定式结构，表示“唯一能做的事就是……”；再往后又补出 `Otherwise ...`，把不这样做的后果也顺手交代清楚。<br>
常用短语：
- `think it over`：反复考虑
- `the only thing to do`：唯一能做的事
- `hand over a hostage`：送出人质
- `on excellent terms`：关系非常好

## 第十八章 做人的难题
## Chapter 18: The Dilemma of Decency

宋国有两个实权人物。  
Song had two real power brokers（实权人物）.  
一个是司马孔父嘉。  
One was Sima Kong Fujia.  
另一个是太宰华督。  
The other was Grand Steward Hua Du.  
前者管军。  
The former ran the army.  
后者管政。  
The latter ran the government.  
说到这里。  
At this point,  
还请孔姓和华姓读者。  
readers surnamed Kong or Hua  
先保持一点敬意。  
might show a little respect.  
因为后来的孔姓和华姓。  
For the later Kong and Hua lines  
都能往上追到他们。  
could both trace themselves back to these men.  
两人都是宋国公族。  
Both belonged to Song's ruling clan.  
本姓都姓子。  
Their original clan name was Zi.  

在这两个人里。  
Of the two,  
宋殇公更重用孔父嘉。  
Duke Shang of Song relied more heavily on Kong Fujia.  
理由很简单。  
The reason was simple.  
宋殇公喜欢打仗。  
Duke Shang liked war.  
他在位十年。  
In ten years on the throne,  
对外打了十一仗。  
he fought eleven foreign campaigns.  
单单对郑国。  
Against Zheng alone,  
就打了三次。  
he fought three times.  
原因还是为了公子冯。  
And all for the sake of Prince Feng.  
爱打仗未必就是坏事。  
Loving war is not always evil.  
真能抢回东西。  
If you can actually seize something back,  
也算给大家谋福利。  
at least the people get some benefit.  
问题是宋国打仗。  
The trouble was that when Song fought,  
几乎就没赢过。  
it hardly ever won.  
可以说。  
You could say  
屡败屡战。  
it kept fighting after defeat,  
又屡战屡败。  
and kept being defeated after fighting.  
男人上了战场。  
When men went to war,  
不是战死。  
they were either killed,  
就是被俘。  
or captured  
再去给别国当奴隶。  
and turned into slaves in other states.  
女人留在家里。  
The women left at home  
不是成了寡妇。  
either became widows  
就是被恶霸糟蹋。  
or were seized by local bullies.  
败仗一多。  
The more defeats came,  
税负也越来越重。  
the taxes grew heavier and heavier.  
老百姓都快活不下去了。  
Ordinary people could barely go on living.  
全国怨气冲天。  
Resentment（怨气） boiled across the state.  
而大家最恨的。  
And the man they hated most  
就是孔父嘉。  
was Kong Fujia.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`时间状语从句`、`并列句`、`并列谓语`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `When men went to war, they were either killed, or captured and turned into slaves in other states.`。<br>
难点拆解：`When men went to war` 是时间状语从句，先交代“男人一上战场”这个背景；后面用 `either ... or ...` 列出两种惨局，`captured and turned ...` 里又有并列动作，把战争失败后的后果压得很重。<br>
常用短语：
- `for the sake of`：为了
- `hardly ever`：几乎从不
- `turn into`：变成
- `boil across`：在各处沸腾起来

### 做人要厚道
### Be Decent

华督最郁闷的。  
What troubled Hua Du most  
倒不是民不聊生。  
was not the misery of the people.  
而是自己这个太宰。  
It was that he, the grand steward,  
地位竟比不过。  
somehow ranked below  
一个国防部长。  
the minister of war.  
有一天。  
One day,  
他的郁闷到了极点。  
his frustration hit its peak.  
为什么？  
Why?  
因为他在路上。  
Because on the road  
碰见了一个女人。  
he caught sight of a woman.  
这女人是真美。  
She was stunning.  
华督一路看着她。  
Hua Du followed her with his eyes  
直到人影都没了。  
until she vanished from sight.  
他忍不住说了。  
He could not help saying  
三个字。  
three words:  
“美而艳。”  
"Beautiful and alluring（艳丽的）."  
什么意思？  
What did that mean?  
就是不只好看。  
It meant she was not only pretty  
还很性感。  
but deeply sensual（性感的） as well.  
可再漂亮。  
But no matter how beautiful,  
他也吃不到嘴里。  
he could not have her.  
因为这个女人。  
Because that woman  
偏偏是孔父嘉的老婆。  
was Kong Fujia's wife.  
地位比不过人家。  
His rank was lower.  
老婆也比不过人家。  
Even his woman lost to the other man's.  
华督这口气。  
That was a bitterness  
真是咽不下去。  
Hua Du simply could not swallow.  

这一年。  
That year  
是宋殇公十年。  
was the tenth year of Duke Shang of Song.  
十年到了。  
Ten years called for a celebration.  
有的国家放假。  
Some states marked that with a holiday,  
有的国家发钱。  
some with money,  
有的国家阅兵。  
and some with a military review.  
宋国选的是阅兵。  
Song chose a grand review.  

孔父嘉担任。  
Kong Fujia served as  
阅兵总指挥。  
the review's commander.  
他从鲁国订的新战车。  
The new chariots he had ordered from Lu  
也刚刚到货。  
had just arrived.  
这次阅兵。  
At this review,  
它们也要亮相。  
they were to make their debut（亮相）.  
这些日子。  
So these days,  
孔父嘉把精力。  
Kong Fujia poured most of his energy  
全压在练兵上。  
into drilling the army.  
而且练得极狠。  
And he drilled them hard.  
宋军的口号是。  
The slogan in Song's army ran:  
“生命不息。  
"As long as life does not stop,  
战斗不止。  
the fighting does not stop.  
不灭郑国。  
If Zheng is not destroyed,  
何以家为？”  
what home can we speak of?"  
军中怨气。  
Resentment in the ranks  
已经到了快炸的时候。  
was near the point of explosion.  

华督一眼看出。  
Hua Du saw the opportunity at once.  
为了地位。  
For rank,  
也为了女人。  
and for the woman,  
他决定狠狠干一票。  
he decided to make a major move.  

宋军里很快。  
Soon a story began to spread  
传开一个消息。  
through the Song army.  
说孔父嘉又要。  
Kong Fujia, it said, was about  
带着大家去打郑国。  
to lead them against Zheng again.  
每个人一听。  
Everyone who heard it  
都是从骨头里发冷。  
felt fear in their bones.  
打郑国这种事。  
Campaigns against Zheng  
向来是有去无回。  
had always been one-way journeys.  
那不是送战车。  
It was nothing but delivering chariots  
就是送奴隶。  
and slaves to the enemy.  
奇怪的是。  
The strange part was  
谁也不怀疑。  
that nobody doubted  
这消息是真是假。  
whether it was true.  
因为这种消息。  
Rumors like this  
事后往往都能应验。  
had a habit of proving true afterward.  
尤其是被孔父嘉。  
Especially once Kong Fujia  
亲口辟谣以后。  
personally denied them,  
大家就更确信了。  
people trusted them even more.  
在宋国。  
In Song,  
国君的话。  
the ruler's words  
常常拿来骗人。  
were often taken as lies,  
反倒小道消息。  
while street rumors  
才更像真的。  
felt more trustworthy.  

“胡说。  
"Nonsense.  
我们怎么会打郑国？  
Why would we attack Zheng?  
我们从不侵略别人。”  
We never invade others."  
果然。  
And sure enough,  
孔父嘉又出来辟谣了。  
Kong Fujia came out and denied it again.  
这一下。  
That did it.  
所有人都认定。  
Everyone became certain  
所谓阅兵。  
that the so-called review  
就是为打郑国做准备。  
was merely preparation for war with Zheng.  
于是士兵们。  
So the soldiers  
都开始准备后事。  
began preparing for the worst.  
该写遗书的写遗书。  
Those who needed wills wrote wills.  
该讨债的赶紧讨债。  
Those owed money hurried to collect it.  
没娶老婆的。  
The unmarried rushed off  
赶紧找一夜情。  
to grab one last night of pleasure.  
有老婆的。  
Those with wives  
就抓紧造孩子。  
hurried to make children.  

可谁也没想到。  
Yet nobody imagined  
这一回的小道消息。  
that this time the rumor  
居然真是谣言。  
was actually false.  
造谣的人。  
And the man who spread it  
正是华督。  
was Hua Du himself.  
说来也可笑。  
It is ironic, really.  
一个人若骗人太多。  
Once a man has lied too often,  
等他真说真话时。  
when he finally speaks the truth,  
别人反倒不信了。  
no one believes him.  

离阅兵越近。  
The nearer the review came,  
军营里的气氛。  
the mood in camp  
就越绷越紧。  
grew tighter and tighter.  
紧到只要。  
It was so taut  
有人点上一把火。  
that if someone struck a spark,  
整座宋国。  
the whole state of Song  
都会烧起来。  
would go up in flames.  
而点火的人。  
And the man ready  
已经准备好了。  
to strike that spark was already in place.  

“弟兄们。  
"Brothers,  
我们不能等死。  
we cannot just sit and wait to die.  
我们死了也就算了。  
If we die, that is one thing.  
老婆孩子凭什么。  
But why should our wives and children  
跟着遭罪？”  
suffer with us?"  
军营里有人。  
A soldier in camp  
高声喊了出来。  
shouted it aloud.  
“对啊。  
"Exactly.  
打什么郑国？  
Why fight Zheng?  
我们这不是去送死吗？”  
Isn't this just marching to our deaths?"  
马上有人应和。  
Others answered at once.  
“那怎么办？”  
"Then what do we do?"  
大家围上来问。  
Men crowded in and asked.  
“打郑国。  
"Fighting Zheng  
本来就是孔部长的主意。  
was Minister Kong's idea.  
华总理并不想打。  
Premier Hua doesn't want war.  
那我们为什么不去。  
So why don't we go  
找华总理说清楚？”  
and tell Premier Hua exactly how we feel?"  
于是满营士兵。  
So soldiers from the whole camp  
一股脑去了。  
poured toward  
总理府。  
the prime minister's residence.  

华督很和气地。  
Hua Du received them  
接待了他们。  
with a kindly face.  
士兵们情绪很激动。  
The soldiers were highly agitated（激动的）.  
一个劲要求。  
They demanded again and again  
华总理阻止这场蠢仗。  
that Premier Hua stop this foolish war.  
“大家先别急。  
"Everyone, calm down.  
孔部长要打郑国。  
Minister Kong wants to attack Zheng  
也是为了宋国颜面。”  
for Song's honor as well."  
华督这话一出。  
The moment Hua Du said that,  
等于已经承认。  
he had effectively admitted  
孔父嘉真要打郑国。  
that Kong Fujia really meant to attack Zheng.  
“他要颜面。  
"He wants honor,  
就让我们送命？  
and we have to die for it?  
他嘴皮子一碰。  
He just moves his lips,  
我们的命就悬着。  
and our lives hang by a thread.  
华总理。  
Premier Hua,  
你得替我们做主。”  
you have to stand up for us."  
士兵们越喊越高。  
The soldiers shouted louder and louder.  
“大家小声些。  
"Keep your voices down.  
这些话若让孔部长听到。  
If Minister Kong hears talk like this,  
你们全都得死。”  
you'll all die."  
华督装出一副。  
Hua Du put on  
非常小心的样子。  
a face of extreme caution.  
“反正都要死。  
"If we're going to die anyway,  
还怕什么？”  
what is there left to fear?"  
士兵们的怒火。  
The soldiers' anger  
一下又被挑起来。  
flared up again.  
华督这时站起身。  
At that point Hua Du rose.  
他咬牙跺脚瞪眼。  
He clenched his teeth, stamped his foot, and glared.  
又扫视众人一圈。  
Then he swept his eyes over them all.  
“唉。  
"Alas.  
民心不可违。  
The will of the people cannot be defied.  
民意不可挡。  
Public feeling cannot be stopped.  
若大家真要。  
If you all truly mean  
为国除害。  
to remove this scourge（祸害） for the state,  
那本相也只能。  
then I, as your prime minister,  
顺从众愿了。”  
can only bow to the common will."  
华督摆出一副。  
Hua Du struck a pose  
大义灭亲的样子。  
of righteous sacrifice.  
其实是在一步步。  
In truth he was step by step  
把大家往前推。  
pushing them forward.  
“为民除害。  
"Remove the scourge for the people.  
铲除奸人。”  
Destroy the traitor."  
群情一下沸腾。  
The crowd exploded.  
原本还没想。  
At first they had not meant  
非杀孔父嘉不可。  
to kill Kong Fujia at all.  
到了这时。  
By now,  
却觉得不杀不行。  
they felt they had to.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `If you all truly mean to remove this scourge for the state, then I, as your prime minister, can only bow to the common will.`。<br>
难点拆解：`If you all truly mean ...` 先给出条件；中间的 `to remove this scourge for the state` 是不定式，说明“真要做什么”；后面的主句 `can only bow to the common will` 表面是在顺从民意，其实是把责任推给众人。<br>
常用短语：
- `be on the point of`：快要到……的时候
- `have a habit of`：总是会，常常会
- `hang by a thread`：命悬一线
- `bow to the common will`：顺从众人的意愿

人群离开总理府。  
The mob left the prime minister's residence.  
在华总理带领下。  
Led by Premier Hua,  
一窝蜂杀向。  
they swarmed toward  
国防部长府。  
the minister of war's mansion.  
门一撞开。  
They burst through the gate  
见人就砍。  
and hacked at whoever they saw.  
孔父嘉急忙出来。  
Kong Fujia rushed out  
想看发生了什么。  
to see what was happening.  
结果当场。  
He was cut down on the spot  
被砍成了肉酱。  
and chopped into pulp.  
杀完孔父嘉。  
After killing Kong Fujia,  
大家再找华总理。  
the crowd looked again for Premier Hua.  
可人早没了。  
He was already gone.  
华总理去哪了？  
Where had Premier Hua gone?  
他抢了孔父嘉的老婆。  
He had seized Kong Fujia's wife  
悄悄回府了。  
and quietly gone back to his own residence.  
孔父嘉一家。  
Kong Fujia's household  
几乎全被杀光。  
was almost wiped out.  
只逃出一个孩子。  
Only one child escaped.  
谁？  
Who?  
孔父嘉的儿子木金父。  
Kong Fujia's son Mujinfu.  
那时他还小。  
He was still young then.  
被家臣抱着。  
A household retainer carried him out  
逃到了鲁国。  
and fled to Lu.  
传到第六代。  
By the sixth generation,  
就是孔子。  
that line would produce Confucius.  
这里还得多说一句。  
One more thing should be added here.  
《左传》记这件事。  
The *Zuo Commentary* records this affair  
写法相当平。  
in a strikingly neutral tone（中性的）.  
若《左传》真是孔子写的。  
If the *Zuo Commentary* was truly written by Confucius,  
那这份度量。  
that breadth of mind（度量）  
实在叫人佩服。  
is deeply impressive.  
后人替孔子避讳。  
Later ages tried to shield Confucius's honor.  
往往就爱丑化华督。  
So they often vilified（丑化） Hua Du more fiercely,  
甚至还说。  
even claiming  
孔父嘉的美妻被抢后。  
that Kong Fujia's beautiful wife,  
上吊死了。  
after being taken, hanged herself.  
和孔子的豁达比。  
Compared with Confucius's own largeness of spirit,  
那种写法。  
such retellings  
真差得远。  
fall far short.  

华督带兵杀了孔父嘉。  
Hua Du had led troops to kill Kong Fujia.  
宋殇公一听。  
When Duke Shang of Song heard it,  
当场大怒。  
he flew into a rage.  
“老孔。  
"Old Kong,  
带兵去剿叛匪。”  
take troops and wipe out the rebels."  
他一拍桌子。  
He slapped the table.  
拍完才想起。  
Only after that did he remember  
老孔已经死了。  
that Old Kong was already dead.  
“来人。  
"Come with me.  
跟我走。  
Come with me.  
把华督给我砍了。”  
We'll hack Hua Du down."  
到了这个时候。  
Even at this point,  
他还没去想。  
he still never paused to ask  
孔父嘉为什么。  
why Kong Fujia  
会被自己人砍死。  
had been hacked to death by his own side.  
他带着侍卫。  
He led a band of guards  
亲自赶去。  
and personally rushed off  
孔父嘉家里。  
to Kong Fujia's house  
要杀华督。  
to kill Hua Du.  

殇公赶到时。  
When Duke Shang arrived,  
那些杀了孔父嘉的士兵。  
the soldiers who had killed Kong Fujia  
正不知道。  
had no idea  
下一步该怎么办。  
what to do next.  
一看殇公来了。  
When they saw Duke Shang arrive,  
心里又恨又怕。  
they felt both fear and hatred.  
想杀他。  
They wanted to cut him down,  
又没人敢先动。  
but no one dared move first.  
就在犹豫时。  
While they hesitated,  
华督也赶来了。  
Hua Du arrived as well.  
他心里明白。  
He knew perfectly well  
孔父嘉一死。  
that once Kong Fujia was dead,  
殇公绝不会放过自己。  
Duke Shang would never spare him.  
唯一的法子。  
The only way out  
就是连殇公。  
was to get rid of  
一起办掉。  
Duke Shang as well.  
安顿好美人后。  
After settling the beauty safely away,  
他又赶回来。  
he hurried back  
鼓动大家。  
to stir the men  
去杀殇公。  
into killing Duke Shang.  

狭路相逢。  
When enemies meet in a narrow path,  
未必是勇者胜。  
it is not always the braver man who wins.  
往往是谁先下手。  
More often it is whoever strikes first  
谁就占先。  
who takes the advantage.  
这时的士兵。  
At that moment the soldiers  
就像一锅开水。  
were like a pot of boiling water.  
谁先端起来。  
Whoever lifted that pot first  
就能泼向对面。  
could hurl it at the other side.  
如果宋殇公这时能喊。  
If Duke Shang had shouted at that moment,  
“弟兄们。  
"Brothers,  
华督谋反。  
Hua Du is rebelling.

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`虚拟语气`、`条件状语从句`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `If he had spared Prince Feng back then, there would have been no years of war, and Hua Du would never have killed him.`。<br>
难点拆解：这里用的是典型的过去虚拟条件句：`If he had spared ...` 表示“如果当初放过”；后面的 `there would have been ...` 和 `would never have killed ...` 都是在说“本来可以避免的结果”。这种句型很适合记“后悔过去”的表达。<br>
常用短语：
- `wipe out the rebels`：剿灭叛乱者
- `which way the wind is blowing`：风向怎么吹，局势往哪边倒
- `thankless wolf`：白眼狼
- `from the board`：从局里被清除掉

### 做人不能太厚道
### But Don't Be Too Decent

上面说过。  
So much for saying  
做人太不厚道不行。  
that you cannot be too shameless.  
可太厚道。  
But being too decent  
一样也不行。  
can be just as bad.  
所以做人。  
Which is why being a decent person  
真是件难事。  
is a hard business indeed.  
郑庄公临死前。  
Before Duke Zhuang died,  
专门提醒过。  
he gave a special warning  
要防宋国和鲁国。  
about what had happened in Song and Lu.  
宋国的事说完了。  
Song's case is already told.  
那鲁国呢？  
What about Lu?  

鲁国这件事。  
What happened in Lu  
还比宋国早两年。  
came two years earlier than Song's affair.  
主角也差不多。  
The leading roles were much the same:  
国防部长、总理和国君。  
the minister of war, the prime minister, and the ruler.  
鲁国的国防部长。  
Lu's minister of war  
叫公子翚。  
was Prince Hui,  
又叫羽父。  
also known as Yufu.  
鲁国几次出兵。  
In several campaigns by Lu,  
都是他带兵。  
he had led the troops himself.  
他打了不少胜仗。  
He won quite a few victories,  
也抢回不少。  
and brought back plenty of  
地盘、财宝和女人。  
land, treasure, and women.  
功劳一大。  
Once his merit grew large,  
事业心也更大了。  
his ambition grew with it.  
最近他总觉得。  
Lately he had begun to feel  
自己怎么也该。  
that he ought by rights  
弄个总理当当。  
to have a turn as prime minister.  
可这话若直接。  
But if he said that outright  
去跟鲁隐公要。  
to Duke Yin of Lu,  
就太像跑官了。  
it would look too much like begging for office.  
怎么办？  
What then?  
公子翚想了个法子。  
Prince Hui thought up a plan.  
不过说这法子前。  
But before we tell it,  
还得先补一段旧事。  
we need one bit of older history.  

当年鲁惠公。  
Back then Duke Hui of Lu  
的正妻叫孟子。  
had a principal wife named Mengzi.  
不是孔孟那个孟子。

可孟子死得早。  
But Mengzi died early  
也没留下儿子。  
and left no son.  
后来声子成了夫人。  
Later Shengzi became consort  
生下息摄。  
and gave birth to Xishe,  
也就是后来的隐公。  
the future Duke Yin.  
再后来。  
Later on,  
宋武公生了个女儿。  
Duke Wu of Song had a daughter.  
她叫仲子。  
She was called Zhongzi.  
她一生下来。  
The moment she was born,  
手上就带着四个字。  
four characters were marked on her hand:  
“为鲁夫人。”  
"To be the wife of Lu."  
《左传》开篇。  
The very opening of the *Zuo Commentary*  
写的就是这件事。  
records this very matter.  
上天既然做媒。  
Since Heaven itself had played matchmaker,  
谁还敢不从？  
who would dare disobey?  
于是鲁宋两国。  
So Lu and Song  
成了亲家。  
became in-laws.  
十五年后。  
Fifteen years later,  
宋武公把仲子。  
Duke Wu of Song sent Zhongzi  
送到鲁国。  
to Lu  
嫁给息摄。  
to marry Xishe.  
谁知鲁惠公。  
But Duke Hui of Lu  
先伸了手。

他一看这个儿媳。  
The moment he saw the girl,  
实在太美了。  
he found her far too beautiful.  
怎么办？  
What did he do?  
他学了卫宣公。  
He copied Duke Xuan of Wei  
那一套。  
to the letter  
把儿媳收进自己房里。  
and took his son's bride into his own bed.  
惠公很宠她。

后来又把她。  
Later he even raised her  
立成了夫人。  
to the rank of consort.  
没多久。  
Not long after,  
她生了个儿子。  
she bore him a son,  
叫姬允。  
named Ji Yun.  
惠公喜欢得很。  
Duke Hui loved the boy dearly  
便把他立为世子。  
and made him crown prince.  
可偏偏公子允。  
But before Prince Yun  
还很小的时候。

惠公就死了。

群臣一商量。

公子允太小。

还是先让长子。  
So they let the elder son  
出来继位吧。

于是鲁隐公登基。

可全鲁国都知道。  
But all Lu knew  
这位子本来。

是该给公子允的。

转眼十一年过去。

公子允已经十七了。  
Prince Yun was already seventeen.  

公子翚据此判断。  
From this Prince Hui drew a conclusion.  
隐公如今。  
By now Duke Yin  
心里一定在防着。  
must surely be guarding against  
公子允。  
Prince Yun.  
隐公嘴上说。  
Duke Yin might say aloud  
以后要把位子还给他。

可真坐上王位的人。  
but once a man truly sits on a throne,  
哪舍得放手？

公子翚觉得。  
Prince Hui felt sure  
隐公一定想。

于是他去见隐公。  
So he went to see Duke Yin.  
“主公。  
"My lord,  
祖国人民选择了您。

让我当太宰。”

公子翚把话说得很明。

他觉得。  
He believed

可他想错了。  
But he was wrong.  
“子翚啊。  
"Yufu,  
你这就不知道了。  
you do not understand.  
当初这位子。  
The throne was originally  
本来就是公子允的。  
Prince Yun's to begin with.

如今他大了。  
Now that he is grown,

I mean to  
把位子还给他。  
return it to him.  
我已经在菟裘。  
I have already had  
修了别墅。  
a retreat built in Tuyou.  
等他满十八岁。  
When he turns eighteen,  
我就退位。  
I will step down  
去那里清静过日子。”  
and live there in quiet."  
隐公说这话时。  
When Duke Yin said this,  
笑得很自然。  
he smiled quite naturally.  
显然不是撒谎。  
He was clearly not lying.  
公子翚也知道。  
Prince Hui also knew  
隐公确实在菟裘。  
that Duke Yin really had been building  
修那座别墅。  
that retreat in Tuyou.  
现在回头想。  
Thinking back on it now,  
若隐公真想杀公子允。  
if Duke Yin had truly meant to kill Prince Yun,  
又怎么会。  
how could he possibly  
让他活到现在？  
have let him live this long?  
其实隐公还有话。  
In truth Duke Yin had more in mind  
没说出口。  
than he said aloud.  
他一直很看重。  
He thought highly of  
公子翚的本事。  
Prince Hui's ability.  
原本都想。  
He had even meant  
提拔他做总理。  
to raise him to the premiership.  
后来转念一想。

Better to leave that favor

for Prince Yun.

对新君更忠心。

隐公太厚道了。  
Duke Yin was too decent.  
真是厚道过了头。

撞上不厚道的人。

the ending is often grim.  
而不厚道的人。

公子翚是一个。

公子允也是一个。  
Prince Yun was another.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`虚拟语气`、`反问句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `If Duke Yin had truly meant to kill Prince Yun, how could he possibly have let him live this long?`。<br>
难点拆解：这里前半句是过去虚拟条件 `If ... had truly meant ...`；后半句用反问 `how could he possibly ...`，语气比直接说 `he would not have let him live` 更强；这种写法很适合表达“反过来一想，根本说不通”。<br>
常用短语：
- `to the letter`：照着原样，丝毫不差
- `hold it in trust`：代为掌管
- `think highly of`：很看重
- `to a fault`：到了过头的程度

公子翚从隐公那里。  
When Prince Hui came back from Duke Yin,  
回来后越想越慌。  
he grew more uneasy the more he thought.  
俗话说。  
As the saying goes,  
疏不间亲。  
an outsider should not meddle between close kin.  
这次挑拨没成。  
His attempt at sowing discord had failed.  
隐公会怎么看自己？  
How would Duke Yin now see him?  
更要命的是。  
Worse still,  
以后公子允若即位。  
if Prince Yun later took the throne,  
再知道了这件事。  
and learned of this conversation,  
自己这条命。  
his own life  
多半就没了。  
would most likely be finished.  
公子翚越想。  
The more Prince Hui thought,  
越觉得不对。  
the worse it felt.  
眼下只剩一条路。  
There was only one road left now:  
反过来去劝。  
turn around and persuade  
公子允先杀隐公。  
Prince Yun to kill Duke Yin first,  
杀人灭口。  
and silence the danger forever.  
厚道人骗不动。  
Since the decent man could not be fooled,  
那就去骗。  
he would fool  
不厚道的人。  
the indecent one instead.  

“公子啊。  
"My prince,  
宫中绝密。

先把您除掉呢。”

公子允一听。  
Prince Yun heard this  
吓得话都结巴了。

“什什什什么？  
"W-w-what?  
大哥要杀我？”

“我我我。  
"I-I-I...  
现在逃去宋国。  
If I flee to Song now,  
还来得及吗？”  
can I still make it?"  
他的意思就是。  
What he meant was  
马上要跑去。  
that he wanted to run at once  
外祖家避难。  
to his mother's people for refuge（避难）.  
“不行。  
"No.  
鲁强宋弱。  
Lu is strong and Song is weak.  
宋国护不住你。”  
Song cannot protect you."  
“那怎么办？”  
"Then what do I do?"  
“他不仁。  
"If he is not kind,  
就别怪你不义。  
then he cannot blame you for not being righteous.  
公子啊。  
My prince,  
我公子翚一向。  
I, Prince Hui, have always  
深明大义。  
stood on the side of justice,  
扶危济困。

这样吧。

and finish him."  
“大哥。  
"Brother,  
拜托你了。

若我真能上位。

you will be prime minister."  
到了这时。  
At that point,  
公子允只想活命。

哪还想得到。

自己正在。  
that he was  
被公子翚忽悠。

刺杀方案。

当晚就赶出来了。  
was drawn up that very night.  
公子翚知道。

三天后。

祭祀钟巫。

钟巫是谁？  
Who was Zhongwu?  
当年隐公还没即位。  
Back before Duke Yin took the throne,

Lu had once fought Zheng.  
他被俘之后。  
After he was captured,  
曾关在郑国大夫。  
he had been held in the house  
尹大夫家里。  
of a Zheng grandee named Master Yin.  
“老尹。  
"Old Yin,  
放了我吧。  
let me go.  
你在郑国也混得一般。

跟我去鲁国。  
Come with me to Lu.  
我一定报答你。”  
I will repay you."  
隐公拿好处诱他。  
Duke Yin tempted him with reward.  
尹大夫也动了心。  
Master Yin was tempted.  
可他说。  
But he said,  
“先问我家的神。  
"Let me ask my family spirit first.  
神说行。  
If the spirit says yes,  
那才行。”  
then yes it is."  
他家的神。  
The spirit he meant  
就是钟巫。  
was Zhongwu.  
供在自家庙里。

于是两人一同。  
So the two men together  
进庙求签。

结果大吉。  
The omen was highly favorable.  
于是尹大夫。  
So Master Yin  
带着全家。  
took his whole family  
跟隐公跑去了鲁国。  
and fled with Duke Yin to Lu.  
隐公是个。  
Duke Yin was the kind of man  
很记恩的人。  
who remembered kindness.  
后来他不仅。  
Later he not only  
格外照顾尹大夫。  
took special care of Master Yin,  
还在城外。  
he also built, outside the city,  
专门修了一座。  
a special  
钟巫庙。  
shrine to Zhongwu.  
并且每年。  
And every year  
都去祭一次。  
he went to sacrifice there once.  

隐公每次去祭钟巫。  
Whenever Duke Yin went to sacrifice to Zhongwu,  
都会住在妫大夫家。  
he stayed at the house of Lord Gui.  
公子翚的办法。  
Prince Hui's plan  
就是先派刺客。  
was to send assassins ahead  
装成杂役。  
disguised as servants,  
躲进杂物房。  
hide them in a storage room,  
等到夜里。  
and then at night  
再潜进隐公卧房。  
slip into Duke Yin's chamber  
下手。  
and strike.  
公子翚实在太懂。  
Prince Hui understood Duke Yin  
隐公这个人了。  
far too well.  
一切都被他算中。  
He guessed everything right.  
隐公不喜欢排场。  
Duke Yin disliked display.  
也很体恤手下。  
He was considerate of his men.  
所以每次出门。  
So whenever he went out,  
都只轻车简从。  
he traveled with a light escort.  
住下时也只是。  
And when he lodged,  
在妫大夫家。  
he stayed only  
客房里休息。  
in a guest room at Lord Gui's house.  
到了夜里。  
Night came.  
隐公睡下了。  
Duke Yin went to sleep.  
侍卫们也歇了。  
The guards also rested.  
院外只剩。  
Only Lord Gui's household guards  
妫家家丁巡守。  
still patrolled outside the courtyard.  
谁也没想到。  
No one guessed  
刺客早已。  
that the assassins were already  
埋伏在院里。  
lying in wait inside.  
到了半夜。  
At midnight,  
刺客动手了。  
the assassins struck.  

隐公就这样死了。  
Duke Yin died just like that.  
简直连想象空间。  
The story hardly leaves us  
都不给留。  
room to imagine more.  
公子允顺利接班。  
Prince Yun succeeded without trouble  
成了鲁桓公。  
and became Duke Huan of Lu.  
他上台第一件事。  
The first thing he did on taking power  
就是任命公子翚。  
was to appoint Prince Hui  
做总理。  
prime minister.  
公子翚随后。  
Prince Hui then  
把脏水全泼给。  
threw all the blame onto  
妫大夫。  
Lord Gui.  
又以弑君罪。  
On the charge of regicide（弑君）,  
把他全家处死。  
he had Lord Gui's whole family executed.  
整个鲁国都知道。  
All Lu knew  
真相是怎么回事。  
what the truth was.  
可没人敢说。  
But no one dared speak.  
其实隐公。  
In fact Duke Yin  
想保住自己。  
had several ways to save himself.  
第一。  
First,  
听公子翚的。  
he could have listened to Prince Hui  
先杀公子允。  
and killed Prince Yun first.  
第二。  
Second,  
杀公子翚。  
he could have killed Prince Hui.  
第三。  
Third,  
提拔公子翚。  
he could have promoted Prince Hui.  
可这三样。  
Yet of those three choices,  
隐公一样都没做。  
Duke Yin chose none.  
他太厚道了。  
He was too decent.  
他只替别人想。  
He thought only for others,  
从没替自己想。  
never for himself.  
所以说。

a person cannot be too decent.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`不定式结构`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `If Duke Yin one day suddenly announced abdication first, he would be ruined.`。<br>
难点拆解：`If Duke Yin ... announced ...` 是条件状语从句；主句 `he would be ruined` 直接说出公子翚为什么急着动手；中间的 `announced abdication first` 既交代动作，也交代时间先后，读起来有很强的逼迫感。<br>
常用短语：
- `sow discord`：挑拨离间
- `be played by someone`：被某人耍了
- `cast lots`：求签，抽签
- `lie in wait`：埋伏等待

## 第十九章 朋友靠不住
## Chapter 19: Friends Cannot Be Trusted

一代英豪终于走了。  
A great hero of his age was finally gone.  
郑庄公在位四十三年。  
Duke Zhuang of Zheng had ruled for forty-three years.  
他在中原一带。  
Across the Central Plain,  
几乎横行无敌。  
he had been almost unstoppable（不可阻挡的）.  
人人都怕他。  
Everyone feared him.  
如今他终于。  
Now at last  
鞠躬尽瘁了。  
he had worked himself to the bone and died.  
公子忽继位。  
Prince Hu succeeded him  
就是郑昭公。  
as Duke Zhao of Zheng.  

公子忽其实。  
Prince Hu was actually  
是个有为青年。  
a promising young man.  
他在诸侯中。  
Among the feudal lords,  
名声非常好。  
his reputation was excellent.  
他能力强。  
He was capable,  
性格宽厚。  
generous in temperament,  
人品也一流。  
and first-rate in character.  
有证据吗？  
Is there evidence?  
当然有。  
Of course.  

齐僖公有两个女儿。  
Duke Xi of Qi had two daughters.  
两个都是绝色美女。

前文说过。  
As said earlier,  
大女儿像林青霞。  
the elder was like Brigitte Lin.  
那小女儿呢？

也找个同城美人比一下。  
To use another beauty from the same city,  
可以说像范冰冰。  
think of Fan Bingbing.  
齐僖公作为大国国君。  
As the ruler of a great state,  
选女婿时。  
Duke Xi, when choosing sons-in-law,  
并不只看门第。  
did not care only about family rank.  
他只有一个标准。  
He had only one standard:  
人品好。  
good character,  
有事业心。  
and ambition（事业心）.  
他把大女儿。  
He married his elder daughter  
嫁给卫国世子急子。  
to Jizi, the heir of Wei.  
那也是个好青年。  
Jizi too was a fine young man.  
可惜大女儿。  
Unfortunately, before the elder daughter  
还没到急子手里。  
could even reach Jizi,  
就被急子的爹。  
she was seized by Jizi's father,  
卫宣公霸占了。  
Duke Xuan of Wei.  
生米成了熟饭。  
The rice was already cooked.  
齐僖公也只能。  
Duke Xi of Qi  
认了倒霉。  
could only swallow the bad luck.  

二女儿怎么办？  
What about the younger daughter?  
齐僖公看中了。  
Duke Xi set his eyes on  
郑国世子公子忽。  
Prince Hu, the heir of Zheng.  
于是很多年前。  
So many years earlier,  
他派人去郑国提亲。  
he sent people to Zheng to propose marriage.  
按理说。  
By all normal logic,  
这么漂亮的女儿。  
with a daughter this beautiful,  
再加上齐大郑小。  
and with Qi large while Zheng was small,  
又是人家主动求亲。  
plus the fact that Qi had come asking,  
公子忽就该。  
Prince Hu should have  
赶紧答应。  
accepted at once  
欢天喜地娶回来。  
and joyfully brought her home.  
可公子忽拒绝了。  
But Prince Hu refused.  
他的理由是。  
His reason was:  
“人各有偶。  
"Everyone has a proper match.  
齐大。  
Qi is too great.  
非吾偶也。”  
It is not my match."  
后面还引了《诗经》。  
He even quoted the *Book of Songs*:  
“自求多福。”  
"Seek your own good fortune."  
这话什么意思？  
What did he mean?  
意思就是。  
He meant:  
每个人都有。  
everyone has  
适合自己的对象。  
a partner suited to himself.  
齐国太大。  
Qi is too powerful.  
齐国公主。  
A princess of Qi  
不适合我。  
is not suited to me.  
我要追求。  
I want to pursue  
自己的幸福。  
my own happiness.  
何必非吃。

齐国的软饭？  
Qi's support?  

后来北戎侵齐。  
Later the northern Rong invaded Qi.  
郑庄公派公子忽。  
Duke Zhuang sent Prince Hu  
和大将高渠弥。  
and the general Gao Qumi  
去齐国增援。  
to reinforce Qi.  
一仗打下来。  
After one battle,  
戎兵被全歼。  
the Rong troops were wiped out.  
公子忽的表现。  
Prince Hu's performance  
堪称完美。

齐僖公越看越喜欢。  
Duke Xi liked him more and more.  
恨不得把他绑起来。  
He almost wanted to tie him up  
直接送进洞房。  
and send him straight into the bridal chamber.  
于是又一次求婚。  
So he proposed marriage again.  
公子忽又拒绝了。  
Prince Hu refused again.  
高渠弥劝他。  
Gao Qumi tried to persuade him,  
他也不听。  
but he would not listen.  
齐僖公这下面子。  
Duke Xi lost considerable face  
很挂不住。  
over this,  
心里也很生气。  
and he was angry.  

回国后。  
After they returned home,  
祭足也来劝公子忽。  
Jizu also came to persuade Prince Hu.  
“公子啊。  
"My prince,  
现在的国际形势。  
the current international situation  
是这样的。  
looks like this.  
如果我们有齐国。  
If we had Qi  
这种亲戚撑腰。  
as a powerful in-law behind us,  
还怕谁？  
whom would we fear?  
再说国内形势。  
And look at the domestic situation.  
也越来越复杂。  
It is growing more complicated.  
虽说你是老大。  
True, you are the eldest,  
也是世子。  
and you are the heir,  
可公子突、公子危。  
but Prince Tu, Prince Wei,  
还有公子仪。  
and Prince Yi  
都盯着国君宝座。  
are all watching the throne.  
你若有齐国后援。  
If you had Qi as backing,  
谁还敢轻举妄动？”  
who would dare act rashly?"  
“不。  
"No.  
我不吃软饭。”  
I will not live off a powerful marriage."  
公子忽很倔。  
Prince Hu was stubborn.  
后来他娶了。  
Later he married  
陈国的公主。  
a princess of Chen.  
他觉得这样。  
That made him feel  
才心安理得。  
at ease with himself.  
公子忽有骨气。  
Prince Hu had backbone.  
可骨气有什么用？  
But what use is backbone?  
软饭这种东西。  
Support through marriage  
该吃的时候。  
is something that, when the time comes,  
还是要吃的。  
you may have to accept.  
你没看见吗？  
Have you not seen  
多少人正是靠它。  
how many people have risen high  
飞黄腾达。  
by relying on it?  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`反问句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `If you had Qi as backing, who would dare act rashly?`。<br>
难点拆解：`If you had Qi as backing` 是条件状语从句，设定“有齐国撑腰”的情况；后面的 `who would dare ...?` 是反问句，语气比直接说 `no one would dare` 更强，也更符合祭足劝人的口吻。<br>
常用短语：
- `set one's eyes on`：看中，盯上
- `by all normal logic`：按常理说
- `lose face`：丢面子
- `act rashly`：轻举妄动

### 好朋友公子冯
### Good Friend Prince Feng

郑昭公登基后。  
After Duke Zhao of Zheng took the throne,  
按规矩。  
according to custom,  
要派使者。  
he had to send envoys  
去各个友邦通报。  
to friendly neighboring states  
以取得承认和支持。  
to gain recognition and support.  
去宋国的使者。  
The envoy sent to Song  
是上卿祭足。  
was the chief minister Jizu himself.  
也就是总理亲自出访。

一来宋国是友邦。  
First, Song was a friendly state.  
二来公子突在宋国。

昭公便请祭足。  
So Duke Zhao asked Jizu  
顺便向宋庄公致意。  
to convey greetings to Duke Zhuang of Song  
请他好好照顾公子突。  
and ask him to treat Prince Tu well.  
毕竟那是自己的弟弟。  
After all, Tu was his younger brother.  

本来是好心。  
It began as goodwill.  
可昭公没有想到。  
But Duke Zhao never imagined  
这事会办成坏事。  
that it would turn into disaster.  
连祭足这样。  
Even a strategist as clever  
足智多谋的人。  
as Jizu  
也没想到。  
could not have imagined  
自己会遇到。  
what absurdity（荒唐事）  
怎样荒唐的事。  
he was about to encounter.  
宋国。  
Song  
就是个荒唐国家。  
was simply an absurd state.  

公子突在宋国。  
Prince Tu was unhappy  
过得很不爽。  
in Song.  
虽然舅舅和外祖家。  
His uncle and his mother's family  
对他还算不错。  
treated him fairly well.  
他享受大夫待遇。

手里的钱也够花。  
and he had enough money to spend.  
可他还是不甘心。  
But he was still unwilling to accept his fate.  
“舅舅。  
"Uncle,  
帮我想想办法。  
think of a way for me.  
让我回郑国。  
Help me return to Zheng  
当国君吧。”  
and become ruler."  
公子突求舅舅帮忙。  
Prince Tu begged his uncle for help.  
他舅舅在宋庄公那里。  
His uncle was favored  
很得宠。  
by Duke Zhuang of Song.  
于是便去汇报。  
So he went to report the matter.  
宋庄公一听。  
Duke Zhuang of Song heard it  
先是拒绝。  
and first refused.  
“这不好吧。  
"That would not be right.  
忽哥可是我朋友。  
Brother Hu is my friend.  
我在郑国时。  
When I was in Zheng,  
他很照顾我。  
he took good care of me.  
我们一起喝酒。  
We drank together,  
一起泡妞。  
chased women together.  
我怎么能忘恩负义？”  
How could I repay kindness with betrayal?"  
公子突的舅舅。  
Prince Tu's uncle  
只好去找华督商量。  
had no choice but to consult Hua Du.  
华督一听。  
When Hua Du heard it,  
反而很高兴。  
he was delighted.  
“这么好的事。  
"Such a fine opportunity.  
怎么能拒绝？”  
How can we refuse?"  

华督去见宋庄公。  
Hua Du went to see Duke Zhuang.  
开门见山就说。  
He came straight to the point.  
“主公。  
"My lord,  
听说你拒绝了公子突？”  
I hear you refused Prince Tu."  
“是啊。  
"Yes.  
忽哥是我哥们儿。  
Brother Hu is my buddy,  
朋友加兄弟。”  
a friend and a brother."  
华督问。  
Hua Du asked,  
“主公听过一句话吗？  
"Has my lord heard the saying:  
只有永远的利益。  
there are only lasting interests,  
没有永远的兄弟。”  
not lasting brothers?"  
“听过。  
"I have.  
说得很好。”  
It is very well said."  
华督接着说。  
Hua Du continued,  
“眼下是我们。  
"Right now,  
欠郑国人情。  
we owe Zheng a favor.  
逢年过节。  
At every holiday,  
还得给他们送礼。  
we still have to send them gifts.  
可若我们帮公子突。  
But if we help Prince Tu  
回郑国做国君。  
return to Zheng as ruler,  
那就是郑国。  
then Zheng  
欠我们人情。  
will owe us a favor.  
以后就该。  
From then on,  
他们给我们送礼。  
they should send gifts to us.  
这么好的事。  
Why refuse  
为什么要拒绝？”  
such a good deal?"  
宋庄公一拍脑袋。  
Duke Zhuang suddenly saw the point.  
“对啊。  
"Exactly.  
华总理说得对。  
Premier Hua, you are right.  
怪不得百姓都说。  
No wonder the people say  
你是人民的好总理。  
you are the people's good prime minister.  
那就答应他。”  
Then let us agree."  
华督又问。  
Hua Du asked again,  
“不怕公子忽说你。  
"Aren't you afraid Prince Hu will say  
不够朋友？”  
you are not a true friend?"  
宋庄公摆摆手。  
Duke Zhuang waved his hand.  
“朋友算什么。  
"What are friends worth?  
朋友就是拿来出卖的。

别说朋友。

先谈条件。”

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`宾语补足语`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `If we help Prince Tu return to Zheng as ruler, then Zheng will owe us a favor.`。<br>
难点拆解：`If we help ...` 是条件状语从句，里面的 `return to Zheng as ruler` 是宾语补足语式的表达，说明“帮他回去并当国君”；主句 `Zheng will owe us a favor` 把华督的利益算盘说得很直白。<br>
常用短语：
- `come straight to the point`：开门见山
- `owe someone a favor`：欠某人人情
- `sell out`：出卖
- `settle the terms`：谈好条件

第二天。  
The next day,  
华督把公子突叫来。  
Hua Du summoned Prince Tu.  
他和宋庄公一起。  
Together with Duke Zhuang of Song,  
三个人当面谈判。  
the three negotiated face to face.  
最后为了回国称君。

公子突签下了。  
Prince Tu signed  
《丁亥条约》。  
the Treaty of Dinghai.  
条约内容非常狠。  
Its terms were savage.  
宋国要在适当时机。  
Song would, at a suitable time,  
帮助公子突回郑。  
help Prince Tu return to Zheng.  
作为回报。  
In return,  
郑国要给宋国。  
Zheng would hand over to Song  
三座城。  
three cities,  
白璧百双。  
a hundred pairs of white jade discs,  
黄金万镒。  
ten thousand yi of gold,  
外加每年三万钟谷子。  
plus thirty thousand zhong of grain every year.  
这哪是条约？  
What kind of treaty was that?  
这简直是。  
It was simply  
丧权辱国。  
a humiliating surrender（丧权辱国）.  
宋庄公是真黑。  
Duke Zhuang of Song was truly ruthless.  

想当年公子冯。  
Back when Prince Feng  
落难在郑国。  
was stranded in Zheng,  
郑国待他多好？  
how well had Zheng treated him?  
后来他回宋。  
Later, once he returned to Song  
当上宋庄公。  
and became Duke Zhuang,  
郑国也没向他。  
Zheng never demanded  
要什么回报。  
any repayment.  
如今他却不仅。  
Yet now he not only  
要出卖最好的朋友。  
meant to betray his best friend,  
破坏郑国的安定。

he also meant  
掏个精光。  
to strip Zheng bare.  
这就叫。

恩将仇报。

All his life Duke Zhuang of Zheng  
看人很准。

可唯一没有看透的。

偏偏就是公子冯。

却没想到。  
but never guessed

he was such a vicious one.  
真可谓。  
It was truly  
打了一辈子雁。  
a lifetime of hunting geese,

only to have one's own eyes pecked out by one in the end.  

祭足到了宋国。  
Jizu arrived in Song.  
华督和宋庄公。  
Hua Du and Duke Zhuang

先关起来。

门外的保安。

“老祭。  
"Old Jizu,  
不成敬意啊。”

华督笑着说。

“这似乎。  
"This hardly seems  
不像待客之道吧。”

祭足压着火说。

he is better suited than Prince Hu

赶走公子忽。  
drive Prince Hu out,  
迎公子突回去。  
and welcome Prince Tu back  
做郑国国君。”  
as ruler of Zheng."  
华督说得很直。

反正屋里也没外人。

祭足一听。

and refused at once.  
“不行。  
"No.  
那不是谋反吗？

我家主公。  
My lord

不行。  
No.  
你另请高明吧。”  
Find someone else."  
祭足心里。  
Inside, Jizu was cursing  
把公子冯骂了个遍。  
Prince Feng up and down.

he bit.  

可华督接着。

and now showed the blade.  
“老祭。

and the women turned into camp prostitutes.  
怎么选。

“好吧。  
"All right.  
听你的。”

“我祭足若不立公子突。

### 要债、拖欠、催债
### Debt, Delay, and Collection

在那个年代。  
In those days,  
发过誓的人。  
a man who had sworn an oath  
是不能赖的。  
could not simply deny it.  
骗人可以。  
You could deceive people,  
骗鬼神不行。  
but not gods and spirits.  
祭足从宋国回来。  
Jizu returned from Song,  
同行的人里。  
and hidden among his entourage  
混着公子突和雍纠。  
were Prince Tu and Yong Jiu.  
两天后。  
Two days later,  
祭足忽然号称。  
Jizu suddenly announced  
自己病了。  
that he had fallen ill,  
还放出了。  
and even sent out  
病危消息。  
word that he was near death.  
于是郑国群臣。  
So all Zheng's high officials  
都去总理府探病。  
went to the prime minister's residence to visit him.  
可总理根本没病。  
But the prime minister was not ill at all.  

“诸位。  
"Gentlemen,  
公子突无罪被流放到宋国。  
Prince Tu was exiled to Song without guilt.  
如今宋国上下都义愤填膺。  
Now people across Song were filled with righteous anger（义愤）.  
眼看就要出兵。  
It is on the verge of sending troops  
来打郑国。  
against Zheng.  
而郑国眼下。  
Yet Zheng at present  
还沉浸在先君之丧里。  
is still immersed in mourning for the late ruler.  
肯定不是宋国对手。  
It is certainly no match for Song.  
那怎么办？  
So what should we do?  
古人说得好。  
As the ancients well said,  
社稷为重。  
the altars of state come first,  
君为轻。  
the ruler comes second.  
如今只有一个法子。  
There is only one way now:  
请主公让位。  
ask our lord to yield the throne  
给公子突。  
to Prince Tu.  
诸位看如何？”  
What do you gentlemen say?"  
祭足是开门见山。  
Jizu could not have been more direct.  
屋外这时。  
Outside, meanwhile,  
已经能听见。  
you could already hear  
兵器碰撞声。  
the clatter of weapons  
和士兵低语声。  
and the low murmurs of soldiers.  
高渠弥第一个。  
Gao Qumi was the first  
跳出来支持。  
to jump out in support.  
“我同意。  
"I agree.  
公子突。  
Prince Tu  
比他哥哥更适合。  
is more fit than his elder brother  
管郑国。”  
to govern Zheng."  
高渠弥如今。  
Gao Qumi was now  
正是国防部长。  
minister of war.  
总理和国防部长。  
With the prime minister and the minister of war  
都表了态。  
both having spoken,  
其他人还能说什么？  
what more could the others say?  
只好全体拥护。  
They could only give unanimous support.  

“我这就带兵。  
"I'll take troops at once  
去把公子忽砍了。”  
and cut Prince Hu down."  
高渠弥说着就要起身。  
Gao Qumi said this and rose to go.  
祭足连忙拦住。  
Jizu hurried to stop him.  
“算了。  
"Enough.  
何必赶尽杀绝？  
Why exterminate him completely?  
把他赶走。  
Drive him away,  
也就够了。”  
and that will be enough."  
祭足随即派人。  
Jizu at once sent word  
去给昭公报信。  
to warn Duke Zhao.  
信上有。  
The message bore  
所有卿大夫的签名。  
the signatures of all the senior ministers.  
大意就是。  
Its meaning was simple:  
国防部政变了。  
the military has staged a coup,  
您下课了。  
you are out,  
公子突上位了。  
Prince Tu is in,  
快逃。  
run now.  
昭公于是逃了。  
So Duke Zhao fled.  
他逃去了卫国。  
He fled to Wei.  
算起来。  
By the count,  
从登基到下台。  
from his accession to his fall,  
还不到一个月。  
less than a month had passed.  
因为昭公平时宽厚。  
Because Duke Zhao had been generous by nature,  
公子危和公子仪。

原本都还留在郑国。

可如今公子突回来。

他们哪还敢留？
