一个赶紧逃蔡国。  
One fled at once to Cai,  
一个逃去了陈国。  
and the other escaped to Chen.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`虚拟语气`、`反问句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `If Prince Hu had once listened to advice, and had Qi behind him now, how would Song dare entertain such thoughts?`。<br>
难点拆解：这里先把两个条件 `had once listened ...` 和 `had Qi behind him now` 并列起来；后面用反问句 `how would Song dare ...`，把祭足的后悔和无奈一下压出来。<br>
常用短语：
- `repay kindness with betrayal`：恩将仇报
- `on the verge of`：濒临，快要
- `could not have been more direct`：不能更直接了
- `stage a coup`：发动政变

公子突登基。  
Prince Tu took the throne  
就是郑厉公。  
as Duke Li of Zheng.  
他上任第二天。  
On his second day in power,  
宋国特使就来了。  
Song's envoy arrived.  
是来祝贺吗？  
Was he there to congratulate him?  
不是。  
No.  
是来要债的。  
He had come to collect the debt.  
这时候。  
At that moment,  
郑厉公后悔了。  
Duke Li regretted everything.  
他翻出《丁亥条约》。  
He took out the Treaty of Dinghai  
越看越冒汗。  
and sweated more the longer he looked.  
这不是卖国条约吗？

这不是要被后人骂吗？

死了以后。  
After death,  
还敢去见老爹吗？

郑国每一寸土地。

东征西讨得来的。

“老祭。  
"Old Jizu,  
快想想办法。”

厉公把祭足请来。

一起商量。

祭足一看条约。

也吓出一身汗。  
and broke into a cold sweat himself.  
“这条约。  
"You dared sign  
你也敢签？”  
a treaty like this?"

卖国条约等着自己。

弄回郑国。

“别说这些了。  
"Enough of that.  
先想办法。”  
Find a way first."  
两人商量了一整天。  
The two men discussed it for a whole day  
最后达成共识。  
and finally reached a plan.  
第一。  
First,  
城池一座不给。  
not one city would be handed over.  
宁失千金。

不失寸土。  
than one inch of land.

把三座城的贡赋。

秋收之后。  
after the autumn harvest,  
再交给宋国。  
and only then.  
对宋国就说。

刚刚登基。  
that the new reign had just begun,  
若立刻割城。

国内恐怕会乱。

第二。  
Second,  
白璧和黄金。  
the jade discs and gold  
先给三分之一。  
would be paid one-third first,  
其余以后补。  
with the rest made up later.  
第三。  
Third,  
每年给宋国的谷子。  
the yearly grain payment to Song  
从明年开始算。  
would begin from the following year.  
于是郑国。  
So Zheng  
把自己的方案。  
sent its proposal  
送给宋庄公。  
to Duke Zhuang of Song,  
同时送去。  
along with  
白璧三十双。  
thirty pairs of white jade discs  
黄金三千镒。  
and three thousand yi of gold.  

宋庄公看完。  
Duke Zhuang read it  
当场大怒。  
and exploded in anger.  
他破口大骂。  
He cursed loudly,  
说公子突忘恩负义。  
calling Prince Tu an ingrate（忘恩负义的人）.  
“在宋国时。  
"When he was in Song,  
他像条狗。  
he was like a dog.

he refused to honor his debt?"  
第二天。  
The next day,  
宋国追账的人。

就到了郑国。  
arrived in Zheng.  
他们说。  
They declared  
不按协议执行。  
that unless Zheng honored the agreement,  
就坚决不走。

简单说。

就是坐地泡。  
they sat down and refused to budge（挪动）.  

面对坐地泡。

该怎么办？

祭足想来想去。

and could only say,  
“这样吧。  
"How about this?  
请齐国和鲁国国君。

to come forward  
替我们转圜斡旋。”

事到如今。  
At this point,  
也只能死马当活马医。  
they could only treat a dead horse as if it were still alive.

with state gifts

特使先到齐国。  
The envoy first arrived in Qi

and explained his purpose.  
齐僖公一听。  
Duke Xi of Qi heard him

and slapped the table in fury.  
“公子忽有什么错？  
"What wrong did Prince Hu commit?  
为什么废了他？

to punish Zheng

在齐僖公心里。  
In Duke Xi's heart,  
公子忽虽然。  
even though Prince Hu  
两次拒婚。

The gifts stayed.  
使者滚蛋。

郑国使者。  
Zheng's envoy

可到了鲁国。  
But in Lu  
他却很受欢迎。

Besides, Lu and Zheng

鲁桓公约宋庄公见面。

两国元首。  
The two rulers  
在扶钟举行会晤。

寒暄之后。

"Old Song,  
我看就算了吧。

郑国确实有困难。”  
Zheng truly has difficulties."  
宋庄公不肯。

又不是粮食。

当然。

hoping it can stand in for the three cities."  
宋庄公一看。

into deep frustration.

郑国债务问题。

宋庄公烦透了。

他对鲁国使者说。

“你知不知道。

how annoyed I already am?

两个字。

拒绝。  
refused.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`虚拟语气`、`条件状语从句`、`倒装`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `Had he known it existed, he would never have dared bring Prince Tu back to Zheng.`。<br>
难点拆解：`Had he known ...` 是省略 `if` 的倒装虚拟条件句，等于 `If he had known ...`；后面的 `would never have dared ...` 表示“当初绝不敢”，非常适合表达事后懊悔。<br>
常用短语：
- `honor the agreement`：履行协议
- `go back on one's word`：说话不算话
- `on someone's behalf`：代表某人
- `be fed up`：烦透了

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`时间状语从句`、`不定式结构`、`并列谓语`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `Hua Du was just preparing to leave the city and surrender, when a subordinate rushed in with news.`。<br>
难点拆解：`was just preparing ... when ...` 是常见结构，表示“正要做某事，突然发生另一件事”；前面把“投降”写到临门一脚，后面突然来一句撤军，制造出明显的转折和悬念。<br>
常用短语：
- `put aside past grievances`：不计前嫌
- `on one's own`：只能靠自己
- `on the brink of`：濒临
- `rush in with news`：冲进来报告消息

## 第二十章 老婆更靠不住
## Chapter 20: Wives Are Even Less Reliable

就在郑鲁联军攻宋时。  
While the Zheng-Lu allied army was attacking Song,  
齐国也出兵了。  
Qi also launched a campaign  
去打纪国。  
against Ji.  
纪国是个小国。  
Ji was a small state  
根本不敢硬扛。  
and did not dare resist head-on.  
纪侯只好一边赔礼。  
The Marquis of Ji could only apologize,  
一边连祖宗都骂。  
even cursing his own ancestors as scoundrels（恶棍）.  
可齐僖公还是不肯放过。  
But Duke Xi of Qi still would not let him go.  
他非要把纪侯抓回来。  
He insisted on dragging the Marquis of Ji back,  
洗干净再下锅。  
washing him clean and dropping him into the pot.  

纪侯比宋庄公还怕。  
The Marquis of Ji was even more frightened than Duke Zhuang of Song.  
他一边拼命守城。  
He defended his city desperately  
一边快马求救。  
and sent urgent riders for help.  
他去找谁？  
Whom did he ask?  
鲁桓公。  
Duke Huan of Lu.  
为什么找他？  
Why him?  
因为两家是亲戚。  
Because the two houses were related.  
纪侯是他的姐夫。  
The Marquis of Ji was his brother-in-law.  

鲁桓公正要再攻宋。  
Duke Huan was just preparing another assault on Song  
纪国使者就到了。  
when Ji's envoy arrived.  
他接过求救信。  
He took the plea for help  
一看就打了个哆嗦。  
and shivered the moment he read it.  
信上怎么写？  
What did the letter say?  
“舅子，快救命。  
"Brother-in-law, save me.  
你再晚一点。  
If you come any later,  
姐夫我就成回锅肉了。”  
your brother-in-law will end up as twice-cooked pork."  

鲁桓公立刻决定。  
Duke Huan decided at once  
改道去救纪国。  
to turn his army toward Ji.  
郑厉公却有些纳闷。  
Duke Li of Zheng was puzzled.  
“派个使者不就行了？”  
"Wouldn't sending an envoy be enough?"  
他为什么奇怪？  
Why was he puzzled?  
因为鲁桓公。  
Because Duke Huan  
一边是纪侯的亲戚。  
was kin to the Marquis of Ji,  
一边又是齐僖公女婿。  
yet was also Duke Xi of Qi's son-in-law.  
原来齐僖公那个。  
As it happened, Duke Xi's younger daughter,  
像范冰冰的小女儿。  
the one compared to Fan Bingbing,  
最后没嫁郑昭公。  
had not ended up marrying Duke Zhao of Zheng.  
退而求其次。  
Qi had settled for second best  
嫁给了鲁桓公。  
and married her to Duke Huan of Lu.  
说来说去。  
When all was said and done,  
齐僖公和纪侯。  
Duke Xi of Qi and the Marquis of Ji  
也算拐弯亲。  
were connected by marriage too.  

鲁桓公叹着气说。  
Duke Huan sighed and said,  
“别提了。  
"Don't even mention it.  
就这桩旧仇。  
Over this old grudge,  
我替姐夫向老丈人。  
I have pleaded with my father-in-law  
求过不知多少次情。  
for my brother-in-law more times than I can count.  
也讲过多少次。  
I have repeated again and again  
冤冤相报何时了。  
that vengeance only breeds vengeance.  
可他老人家就是不听。  
But the old man refuses to listen.  
就算老丈人再亲。  
Even if my father-in-law is family,  
也不能眼看姐姐守寡。  
I can't just watch my sister become a widow.  
这一仗。  
This time,  
齐鲁非打不可。”  
Qi and Lu will have to fight."  
郑厉公听了。  
Duke Li heard this  
倒挺讲义气。  
and showed real loyalty.  
“鲁哥够意思。  
"Brother Lu, that's true loyalty.  
你帮我打宋。  
You helped me hit Song.  
我也不能袖手旁观。  
I can't stand by and do nothing.  
走，我们一起去打齐国。”  
Come on, we'll go fight Qi together."  
就这么着。  
And so,  
鲁郑联军不打宋了。  
the Lu-Zheng alliance stopped attacking Song  
改去打齐国。  
and turned instead against Qi.  
说来说去。  
Which means, in the end,  
齐国原本不想帮宋国。  
Qi had never meant to help Song,  
却偏偏帮了他们大忙。  
yet wound up doing Song an enormous favor.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`让步状语从句`、`并列句`、`不定式结构`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `Even if my father-in-law is family, I can't just watch my sister become a widow.`。<br>
难点拆解：`Even if ...` 是让步状语从句，表示“就算……也……”；后面的 `I can't just watch ...` 是主句，直接说出鲁桓公的底线；`become a widow` 用不定式后的结果补语把“姐姐会变成寡妇”说得很直白。<br>
常用短语：
- `resist head-on`：正面硬扛
- `turn one's army toward`：调转兵锋去打
- `settle for second best`：退而求其次
- `stand by and do nothing`：袖手旁观

### 痛击老丈人
### Taking On the Father-in-Law

鲁郑联军到了纪国。

就在城外扎营。  
and pitched camp outside the city.  
对面就是齐军大营。

安营之后。  
Once the camps were set,  
鲁桓公请齐僖公。

阵前对话。  
to speak before the lines.  
齐僖公一见面就问。

“你来干什么？”  
"What are you doing here?"  
鲁桓公还想讲道理。  
Duke Huan still tried to reason with him.  
“老丈人。  
"Father-in-law,  
我看还是算了吧。  
I think you should let this go.  
古人不是说。  
Did not the ancients say  
冤冤相报……”  
that vengeance only breeds vengeance..."  
他连“何时了”。  
He had not even reached  
都还没说出口。  
the words "when will it end"  
齐僖公就骂起来了。  
before Duke Xi began cursing.  
“报个屁。  
"To hell with that.  
杀父之仇。  
A father's killer  
尚且不能不报。  
must be avenged already.  
何况我们这仇。  
And our feud  
还往上追了七代。  
goes seven generations back.  
你有种就别叫。  
If you have any guts,  
我老丈人。  
don't call me your father-in-law.  
咱们战场上见。”  
Let's settle this on the battlefield."  
鲁桓公脸色。  
Duke Huan's face  
顿时很难看。  
went dark at once.  
“好。  
"Fine.  
既然这样。  
If that is how it is,  
那就战场上见。”  
then we meet on the battlefield."  

鲁桓公回营后。  
After Duke Huan returned to camp,  
一面派人进城。  
he sent men into the city  
去安抚纪侯。  
to reassure the Marquis of Ji,  
一面和郑厉公。  
and sat down with Duke Li  
商量怎么打齐军。  
to plan how to fight Qi.  
两人商量了一夜。  
The two discussed it through the night.  
第二天一早。  
At dawn the next morning,  
他们出营一看。  
they stepped out of camp and looked,  
都被吓了一跳。  
and both were startled.  
就一夜工夫。  
In only one night,  
齐营旁边。  
beside Qi's camp,  
竟又多出。  
there had appeared  
三座大营。  
three new camps.  
鲁桓公和郑厉公。  
Duke Huan and Duke Li  
都倒吸了一口凉气。  
both sucked in a sharp breath.  
齐军兵力。  
Had Qi's strength  
难道一夜翻了三倍？  
tripled overnight?  
这仗还怎么打？  
How were they supposed to fight now?  
可等看清旗号。  
But once they saw the banners clearly,  
两个人却笑了。  
both men laughed.  
原来新来的三营。  
The three new camps  
分别是卫、燕、宋。  
belonged to Wei, Yan, and Song.  
卫国和燕国。  
Wei and Yan  
都是齐国叫来助战的。  
had both been invited by Qi.  
卫国国内正乱。  
Wei was in chaos at home  
本来就是来应付。  
and had only come for show.  
燕国远在北面。  
Yan lay far to the north,  
战力也稀松。  
and its fighting power was slack.  
它来凑热闹。  
It had come mostly  
主要是想让齐国。  
to keep Qi willing  
以后帮自己挡狄人。  
to help block the Di tribes later.  
宋国则是主动来的。  
Song, by contrast, had come of its own accord.  
它算盘打得很响。  
Its calculations were loud and clear.  
在别人地盘上。  
If Qi defeated Zheng and Lu  
帮齐国打败鲁郑。  
on someone else's soil,  
不就等于齐国。  
would that not be the same  
帮自己打鲁郑吗？  
as Qi fighting Zheng and Lu for Song?  
还能顺手。  
And at the same time  
再落齐国一个人情。  
it would win Song a favor from Qi.  
齐僖公对这盘算盘。  
Duke Xi was delighted  
也很满意。  
with the whole arrangement.  
可在鲁郑两君眼里。  
But in the eyes of the rulers of Lu and Zheng,  
这三国军队。  
those three armies  
不过就是三坨狗屎。  
were little more than three heaps of dung.  
成事不足。  
They could not help anything succeed,  
败事有余。  
but were more than capable of ruining things.  
尤其宋军和卫军。  
Song and Wei especially  
简直像专门来。  
looked as though they had come  
送人头的一样。  
for the sole purpose of being humiliated.  
原先打齐国。  
Before seeing them,  
他们还没有十足把握。  
Lu and Zheng had not been fully sure.  
如今反倒更有把握。  
Now they felt surer than ever.  
后来事实证明。  
Later events proved  
他们看得一点没错。  
that they had judged the situation exactly right.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`时间状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `Once they saw the banners clearly, both men laughed.`。<br>
难点拆解：`Once ...` 是时间状语从句，表示“等到一看清楚”；后面主句 `both men laughed` 很短，但前面大量铺垫把气氛先压紧，再突然反转，所以效果很强；中间还有 `that ...` 这一层隐含信息，解释为什么他们转而有了把握。<br>
常用短语：
- `pitch camp`：扎营
- `suck in a sharp breath`：倒吸一口凉气
- `of one's own accord`：主动地
- `for show`：做样子，应付一下

大战很快开始。  
The battle began soon after.  
南面是鲁郑联军。  
On the southern side stood Lu and Zheng.  
北面依次是。  
Opposite them, from left to right,  
宋、齐、燕、卫。  
stood Song, Qi, Yan, and Wei.  
鲁军先冲。  
Lu charged first.  
鲁桓公身先士卒。  
Duke Huan led from the front.  
还没真正接战。  
Before the lines had fully closed,  
燕军和卫军。  
the men of Yan and Wei  
心里就已经想跑。  
were already thinking of escape.  
鲁桓公看出来了。  
Duke Huan saw it at once.  
他大吼。  
So he roared,  
“燕国、卫国的人听着。  
"Men of Yan and Wei, listen.  
大家都姓姬。  
We all bear the surname Ji.  
姬姓不打姬姓。  
Ji should not fight Ji.  
识相的就快滚。”  
If you know what's good for you, clear out."  
卫国和燕国。  
Wei and Yan  
本来就想逃。  
had already wanted to run.  
一听这话。  
The moment they heard that,  
好像突然有了。  
they seemed suddenly to have found  
逃跑的理论依据。  
a perfect theory for retreat.  
连场面话都不说。  
Without wasting a single word,  
扭头就跑。  
they turned and fled.  

另一边。  
On the other flank,  
郑军已经同齐宋交手。  
Zheng had already engaged Qi and Song.  
没打几个回合。  
After only a few exchanges,  
鲁军又从侧翼。  
the Lu forces  
杀了过来。  
came crashing in from the flank.  
宋军最怕的。  
The troops Song feared most  
本来就是郑军和鲁军。  
were exactly the armies of Zheng and Lu.  
这一下。  
So at that point  
它哪还敢再顶？  
how could it possibly stand?  
掉头就跑。  
It turned and ran.  
宋军这一跑。  
And once Song broke,  
把齐军阵型。  
it smashed Qi's formation  
冲得乱七八糟。  
into complete disorder（混乱）.  
齐军也只好跟着败。  
Qi had no choice but to collapse with it.  
所以说。  
That is why  
郑厉公和鲁桓公。  
Duke Li and Duke Huan  
一看见宋、卫、燕。  
had grown more confident the moment they saw Song, Wei, and Yan.  
就更有底了。  
  
后来《孙子兵法》里。  
Later, in *The Art of War*,  
有一条叫。  
there would be a principle called  
“乱军引胜”。  
"victory drawn from a disordered enemy."  
说的就是这类事。  
It is this sort of thing it means.  
还好郑厉公和鲁桓公。  
To their credit, Duke Li and Duke Huan  
网开一面。  
did not press things too far.  
他们没有穷追。  
They did not pursue to the bitter end.  
所以齐军虽然败了。  
So although Qi was defeated,  
损失还不算太重。  
its losses were not too severe.  
齐僖公也只能。  
Duke Xi could only  
带着残兵败将。  
take his beaten remnants  
回国去了。  
back home.  
鲁军和郑军。  
The Lu and Zheng armies  
解了纪国之围。  
lifted Ji's siege  
也各自回国。  
and then returned to their own states.  
从此以后。  
From that point on,  
郑国和鲁国结盟。  
Zheng and Lu became allies,  
齐国和宋国结盟。  
while Qi and Song became allies,  
双方都恨对方入骨。  
and each side hated the other to the bone.  

伐纪失败后。  
After the failed campaign against Ji,  
齐僖公心里。  
Duke Xi of Qi  
郁闷得要命。  
was miserable beyond words.  
女婿居然这样。  
His own son-in-law  
对着自己动刀兵。  
had actually taken up arms against him.  
真是天理何在。  
What justice was there in the world?  
他越想越气。  
The more he thought, the angrier he became.  
越气越想。  
And the angrier he became, the more he thought.  
最后硬是气病了。  
At last he literally made himself ill  
没多久就死了。  
and died not long after.  
齐僖公一死。  
Once Duke Xi died,  
儿子诸儿继位。  
his son Zhuer succeeded him  
这就是齐襄公。  
as Duke Xiang of Qi.  
齐襄公有两大爱好。  
Duke Xiang of Qi had two great hobbies.  
一个是打架。  
One was fighting.  
谁都敢打。  
He would fight anyone.  
而且特别记仇。  
And he never forgot a grudge.  
说白了。  
Put simply,  
就是个街头混子。  
he was a street thug（混子） with a throne.  
另一个爱好。  
His other hobby  
就是好色。  
was lust.  
跟谁都能乱来。  
He would fool around with anyone.  
他有个亲叔叔。  
He had a paternal uncle  
叫夷仲年。  
named Yizhong Nian.  
前文提到过。  
We have met him before.  
夷仲年有个儿子。  
Yizhong Nian had a son  
叫公孙无知。  
named Gongsun Wuzhi.  
名字虽然奇怪。  
The name was odd,  
人却很招人喜欢。  
but the boy himself was likable.  
齐僖公很喜欢他。  
Duke Xi was fond of him.  
后来干脆。  
Later he even went so far  
把他的待遇。  
as to raise his privileges（待遇）  
提到世子级。  
to crown-prince level.  
小时候。  
When they were young,  
公孙无知还真挺无知。  
Gongsun Wuzhi really was rather witless.  
他老跟堂哥诸儿打架。  
He was always fighting his cousin Zhuer.  
按说小孩打架。  
Children fight;  
摸摸头也就过去了。  
normally you pat them on the head and move on.  
可诸儿记住了。  
But Zhuer remembered it.  
等到自己做了襄公。  
Once he became Duke Xiang,  
他头一件事。  
the first thing he did  
就是把无知的待遇。  
was to lower Wuzhi's privileges（待遇）  
给降下去。  
back down again.  
无知很恼火。  
Wuzhi was furious,  
却也没办法。  
but there was nothing he could do.  
为什么要提这一段？  
Why mention this episode?  
因为后面还要用到。  
Because it will matter later.  

### 祭女
### Jizu's Daughter

郑鲁大胜齐宋卫燕后。  
After Zheng and Lu defeated Qi, Song, Wei, and Yan,  
郑厉公总算。  
Duke Li of Zheng was finally  
不用整天看。  
free from the sight  
宋国使臣坐地泡了。  
of Song's envoys camping in place.  
古人说得好。  
As the ancients put it well,  
苟无外患。  
if there are no troubles from without,  
必有内忧。  
there will be worries within.  
对外打赢了。  
Once the foreign war was won,  
内部矛盾。  
internal tensions  
自然就成了。  
naturally became  
主要矛盾。  
the main contradiction（主要矛盾）.  
郑国现在。  
That was exactly  
就是这样。  
what Zheng now faced.  

厉公从一开始。  
From the very beginning,  
其实就不喜欢祭足。  
Duke Li had never truly liked Jizu.  
因为他知道。  
He knew  
祭足和公子忽关系近。  
that Jizu was close to Prince Hu.  
只不过那时候。  
But back then  
他还得靠祭足。  
he still needed Jizu  
出谋划策。  
to make plans  
对付外敌。  
and handle foreign threats.  
如今宋国被打趴。  
Now Song had been knocked flat,  
祭足的用处。  
Jizu seemed  
看起来没那么大了。  
less indispensable（不可或缺的）.  
厉公便开始觉得。  
So Duke Li began to feel  
祭足功高震主。  
that Jizu's功劳 overshadowed the throne,  
而且对自己。  
and that he showed  
很不恭敬。  
too little deference to him.  
有一天。  
One day Duke Li thought,  
“这老东西。  
"That old fellow  
肯定还在暗中。  
must still be secretly  
跟公子忽勾连。  
in contact with Prince Hu.  
说不准哪天。  
Some day soon,  
他就会帮公子忽。  
he may help Prince Hu  
杀回来。  
come back and strike.  
那我不就惨了？”  
Then I will be finished, won't I?"  
他越想越怕。  
The more he thought, the more afraid he became.  
越想越觉得。  
The more he thought, the more certain he felt  
这事马上就要发生。  
that it was about to happen.  
厉公不是昭公。  
Duke Li was not Duke Zhao.  
他更狠。  
He was harsher,  
也更果断。  
and more decisive.  
于是他立刻。  
So he immediately  
把雍纠叫来。  
sent for Yong Jiu.  
雍纠是他表弟。  
Yong Jiu was his cousin.  
原本是宋国人。  
He was originally from Song.  
跟着厉公来郑后。  
After coming to Zheng with Duke Li,  
成了郑国大夫。  
he had become a Zheng grandee  
也是厉公最亲信的人。  
and Duke Li's most trusted man.  
“老弟。  
"Brother,  
祭足这老家伙。  
that old man Jizu  
对我很不敬。  
shows me little respect.  
我想除掉他。  
I want him removed.  
你看怎么办？”  
What do you suggest?"  
厉公自己。  
Duke Li himself  
其实不敢亲手杀祭足。  
did not dare kill Jizu with his own hands.  
毕竟祭足势力太大。  
Jizu's influence was simply too great.  
想想三代郑公以来。  
Think of how many ideas he had supplied  
这老家伙出过多少主意。  
through the reigns of three dukes of Zheng,  
朝里又有多少人。  
and how many men at court  
是他的老部下。  
were his old followers.  
雍纠一听就说。  
Yong Jiu answered at once,  
“我看也是。  
"I think so too.  
你不杀他。  
If you don't kill him,  
迟早他会杀你。”  
sooner or later he will kill you."  
雍纠这么积极。  
Yong Jiu spoke with such zeal  
也是有私心。  
for a reason.  
他对祭足的相位。  
He had ideas of his own  
本来也有点想法。  
about Jizu's post as prime minister.  
两人一商量。  
The two discussed it  
最后定了个法子。  
and settled on a plan.  
雍纠在东郊。  
Yong Jiu had built  
新修了一座别墅。  
a new villa in the eastern suburbs.  
过几天。  
In a few days,  
他请祭足去参观。  
he would invite Jizu to inspect it  
顺便指点装修。  
and offer advice on the furnishings.  
到时候再设宴。  
Then he would lay a banquet  
请祭足吃饭。  
for Jizu  
顺手在酒菜里。  
and slip poison  
下上毒。  
into the food and drink.  
毒死祭足。  
That would kill Jizu.  
厉公听了满意。  
Duke Li liked the plan.  
他拍着雍纠肩膀。  
Patting Yong Jiu on the shoulder,  
对他说。  
he told him,  
“干掉祭足。  
"Get rid of Jizu,  
你就是总理。”  
and you become prime minister."  
雍纠也来劲了。  
Yong Jiu was fired up.  
“为了国家。  
"For the state,  
我也只能。  
I will have to  
大义灭亲了。”  
put righteousness above kin."  
两人越说越高兴。  
The more they talked, the more pleased they became.  
干脆喝了几杯。  
So they drank a few cups  
提前庆功。  
to celebrate in advance.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`时间状语从句`、`并列句`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `If you don't kill him, sooner or later he will kill you.`。<br>
难点拆解：`If you don't kill him` 是很直接的条件状语从句；后面的 `sooner or later he will kill you` 则把后果说得毫不遮掩；这种句子短、硬、狠，很符合厉公和雍纠这一段的气质。<br>
常用短语：
- `make plans`：出谋划策
- `knock flat`：打趴下
- `be in contact with`：和……有联系
- `slip poison into`：往……里下毒

雍纠高兴坏了。  
Yong Jiu was delighted.  
一身酒气。  
He reeked of wine,  
哼着小调。  
humming a vulgar（粗俗的） little tune  
就回家了。  
as he headed home.  
“你还知道回家？  
"You still remember home?  
怎么不死在外面？”  
Why didn't you just die outside?"  
老婆在家大发脾气。  
His wife was raging at home.  
她是谁？  
Who was she?  
祭女。  
Jizu's daughter.  
当初宋庄公和祭足。  
Back when Duke Zhuang of Song and Jizu  
签协议时。  
made their agreement,  
其中有一条。  
one clause（条款）  
就是把女儿。  
was that Jizu would marry off his daughter  
嫁给雍纠。  
to Yong Jiu.  
这件事上。  
On this matter,  
祭足没有赖账。  
Jizu did not default（赖账）.  
其实他还愿意。  
In fact, he was quite willing,  
因为女儿脾气太烈。  
because his daughter had such a fierce temper  
在郑国真没人敢娶。  
that in Zheng almost no one dared marry her.  

“这这这……”  
"W-w-well..."  
雍纠一下慌了。  
Yong Jiu panicked at once.  
他是真怕老婆。  
He was genuinely afraid of his wife,  
而且是骨子里怕。  
afraid of her deep in his bones.  
这也不怪他。  
You could hardly blame him.  
祭女的性子。  
Jizu's daughter had a temper  
和她爹一模一样。  
just like her father's,  
又强又硬。  
strong and hard.  
“这什么这？  
"What do you mean, well?  
还哼小调。  
And humming songs?  
跑去找女人了？”  
Did you go chasing women?"  
祭女步步紧逼。  
She pressed him hard.  
“没，没有。”  
"N-no."  
“没有你高兴什么？  
"If not, why are you so happy?  
升官了？”  
Got promoted?"  
“嘿嘿。  
"Heh.  
让你猜中了。  
You guessed it.  
我要升官了。”  
I'm getting promoted."  
雍纠喝多了。  
Yong Jiu had drunk too much.  
又想哄老婆高兴。  
He also wanted to please his wife,  
一不留神。  
and before he knew it,  
就说漏了嘴。  
he let it slip.  
“升什么官？”  
"Promoted to what?"  
“这，这……”  
"Well, well..."  
他开始遮掩。  
He started to dodge.  
还故意岔开话题。  
He even tried to change the subject.  
“你看。  
"Look.  
天多蓝啊。”  
How blue the sky is."  

祭女很聪明。  
She was very sharp.  
她立刻盘算起来。  
She began calculating at once.  
不算郑厉公。  
Leaving Duke Li aside,  
雍纠现在。  
Yong Jiu already  
在郑国排第三。  
ranked third in Zheng.  
要是再升。  
If he rose any further,  
只可能是。  
it could only be  
国防部长。  
minister of war  
或者总理。  
or prime minister.  
可雍纠连战车。  
But Yong Jiu could barely  
都站不稳。  
stand steady on a chariot.  
看见耗子。  
He even jumped  
都能吓一跳。  
at the sight of a mouse.  
国防部长没戏。  
So minister of war was out.  
那就只剩总理。  
That left only prime minister.  
可总理是谁？  
And who was prime minister?  
她爹。  
Her father.  
祭女看出问题了。  
She saw the problem at once.  
她没听说过。  
She had heard nothing  
父亲要辞职。  
about her father resigning（辞职）.  
而且她也知道。  
And she also knew  
父亲不可能辞职。  
he would never resign.  

“蓝你个头。  
"Blue sky, my foot.  
快说。  
Speak.  
升什么官？”  
What promotion is it?"  
“这，这……”  
"Well, well..."  
雍纠越吞吞吐吐。  
The more Yong Jiu stammered,  
祭女越确信。  
the more certain she became  
自己猜对了。  
that she had guessed right.  
她知道。  
She knew  
再逼也问不出来。  
pressing harder would get nothing.  
真要逼出来了。  
And if she really forced it out,  
那也多半是。  
that would probably be  
自己被灭口的时候。  
the moment she got silenced for good.  
“算了。  
"Forget it.  
你不说我也知道。  
Even if you don't say it, I know.  
你要当上卿了。  
You're going to become chief minister.  
如果我没猜错。  
If I have guessed right,  
你和郑侯。  
you and the Marquis of Zheng  
是要对付我爹。”  
are planning to move against my father."  
她叹了口气。  
She sighed.  
“俗话说。  
"As the saying goes,  
嫁鸡随鸡。  
if you marry a chicken, follow the chicken;  
嫁狗随狗。  
if you marry a dog, follow the dog.  
你们真要动我爹。  
If you really mean to strike at my father,  
我也没办法。  
there is nothing I can do.  
鲁侯都打老丈人了。  
The Lord of Lu even fought his father-in-law,  
他夫人不也没话说？  
and his wife said nothing, didn't she?  
再说了。  
Besides,  
我和我爹。  
my father and I  
也没多少感情。  
were never that close.  
自从我娘生下我。  
Ever since my mother gave birth to me,  
我爹就只疼小妾。  
my father has cared only for his concubine（妾）.  
半年都不跟我们母女。  
He can go half a year  
说一句话。”  
without saying a word to us."  
祭女说了一大串。  
She went on and on,  
意思只有一个。  
but the meaning was simple:  
你想怎么干。  
whatever he wanted to do,  
她都支持。  
she would support it.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`宾语从句`、`现在进行时表示计划`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `If I have guessed right, you and the Marquis of Zheng are planning to move against my father.`。<br>
难点拆解：`If I have guessed right` 是条件状语从句；`are planning to ...` 用现在进行时表示已经在推进的计划；`move against my father` 是比较含蓄但很常见的“对我爹下手”。<br>
常用短语：
- `let it slip`：说漏嘴
- `change the subject`：岔开话题
- `move against`：对……下手
- `for good`：彻底地

雍纠高兴坏了。  
Yong Jiu was thrilled.  
他没想到。  
He had never expected  
祭女平时那么凶。  
that though Jizu's daughter was fierce every day,  
关键时候。  
at the crucial moment  
竟这么明白事理。  
she could be so righteous（明大义的）.  
“老婆。  
"Wife,  
既然你这么说。  
since you put it that way,  
那我就告诉你实话。”  
I'll tell you the truth."  
雍纠一高兴。  
In his excitement,  
又借着酒劲。  
with wine still talking,  
把他和厉公的计策。  
he told her, point by point,  
一五一十全说了。  
the whole plan he and Duke Li had made.  
“早说啊。  
"You should have said that earlier.  
既然这样。  
In that case,  
我替你去请我爹。”  
I'll invite my father for you."  
“太好了。  
"Wonderful.  
太好了。”  
Wonderful."  
雍纠真高兴。  
Yong Jiu was genuinely happy.  
他一直怕祭足。  
He had always feared Jizu.  
最怕自己去请人时。  
What terrified（极害怕的） him most was inviting the man himself  
一不小心露了馅。  
and accidentally giving the game away.  
那不是找死吗？  
Would that not be courting death?  
当天夜里。  
That night,  
兴高采烈的雍纠。  
the overjoyed Yong Jiu  
在床上疯了一阵。  
went wild in bed.  
祭女心里想。  
Jizu's daughter thought to herself,  
“你尽管折腾吧。  
"Go on and wear yourself out.  
雍家能不能留后。  
Whether the Yong house leaves an heir  
就看你今晚的本事了。”  
depends on what you manage tonight."  

### 雍纠之死
### The Death of Yong Jiu

第二天。  
The next day,  
祭女回了娘家。  
Jizu's daughter went back to her parents' home.  
名义上。  
Officially,  
她是替雍纠。  
she was going on Yong Jiu's behalf  
去请祭足。  
to invite Jizu  
参观别墅。  
to inspect the villa（别墅）.  
一到家。  
As soon as she got home,  
她先给父亲问安。  
she greeted her father,  
随后就把母亲。  
then pulled her mother  
拉到角落里。  
into a corner  
说悄悄话。  
for a private talk.  
“娘。  
"Mother,  
我问你。  
let me ask you.  
爹和老公。  
Between a father and a husband,  
哪一个更亲？”  
which one is closer?"  
“都亲。”  
"Both are close,"  
她娘说。  
her mother said.  
“哪个更亲？”  
"Which one is closer?"  
她娘想了想。  
Her mother thought for a moment.  
“爹亲。”  
"Father."  
“为什么？”  
"Why?"  
“你想啊。  
"Think about it.  
要是你爹死了。  
If your father dies,  
你就没爹了。  
you have no father anymore.  
就算娘再给你找一个。  
Even if I find you another one,  
那也是假的。  
he would only be fake.  
可老公不一样。  
But a husband is different.  
老公死了。  
If your husband dies,  
还能再找。  
you can always find another.  
说不定下一个。  
For all you know, the next one  
还更好呢。”  
may even be better."  
这话说得。  
Those words were  
又短又狠。  
brief and sharp.  
她娘也聪明。  
Her mother was clever too.  
已经看出来。  
She had already sensed  
女儿有事。  
that her daughter had something serious to say.  
《左传》原话是。  
The original line in the *Zuo Commentary* says:  
“人尽夫也。  
"Any man may be a husband,  
父一而已。”  
but a father is only one."  
后来“人尽可夫”这话。  
The idiom `ren jin ke fu`  
就是从这里来的。  
came from here.  

“好。”  
"All right."  
祭女原本还有点犹豫。  
Jizu's daughter had still been hesitating.  
毕竟做寡妇。  
After all, becoming a widow（寡妇）  
并不好受。  
was no easy thing.  
如今听娘分析透了。  
But once her mother laid it all out,  
她就下了决心。  
she made up her mind.  
后面的事很简单。  
What followed was simple.  
祭女把一切。  
Jizu's daughter told  
全告诉了祭足。  
Jizu everything.  
“这个混账。  
"That bastard.  
他是活腻了。”  
He must be tired of living."  
祭足咬牙切齿。  
Jizu ground his teeth in rage.  
以他的智商和老辣。  
With his brains and experience,  
对付雍纠这种。  
dealing with a pampered（娇生惯养的） young lord  
公子哥。  
like Yong Jiu  
实在太容易。  
was absurdly easy.  
“孩子别怕。  
"Child, don't be afraid.  
爹再给你找个好的。”  
Father will find you a better one."  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`条件状语从句`、`并列句`、`对比结构`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `If your father dies, you have no father; but if your husband dies, you can find another.`。<br>
难点拆解：前后两个 `if` 都是条件状语从句；中间用 `but` 做强烈对比；后半句的 `find another` 省掉了 `husband`，但意思非常清楚，口语里很常见。<br>
常用短语：
- `think about it`：你想想看
- `for all you know`：说不定
- `make up one's mind`：下定决心
- `be tired of living`：活腻了

第二天。  
The following day,  
祭足去看别墅。  
Jizu went to see the villa.  
雍纠心里。  
Yong Jiu felt  
又高兴又紧张。  
both excited and nervous.  
高兴的是。  
He was excited  
老丈人上钩了。  
because his father-in-law had taken the bait.  
紧张的是。  
He was nervous  
他一见老丈人。  
because whenever he saw the old man,  
就先发怵。  
his courage failed him first.  
酒席早备好了。  
The banquet had long been prepared.  
雍纠请老丈人上座。  
Yong Jiu invited his father-in-law to the seat of honor  
又陪了几句。  
and added a few flattering（奉承的） words,  
什么“您气色真好”之类。  
things like "You look wonderfully well."  
然后开始敬酒。  
Then he raised a toast.  
“爹。  
"Father,  
先敬您一杯。  
let me offer you a cup first.  
祝您福如东海。  
May your blessings be as vast as the Eastern Sea,  
寿比南山。”  
and your life as long as the Southern Mountain."  
雍纠双手捧杯。  
Holding the cup in both hands,  
把酒递过去。  
he offered the wine.  
祭足看了一眼。  
Jizu glanced at it  
却不伸手。  
but did not reach for it.  
“爹。  
"Father,  
这可是我特地。  
this is a fine wine  
从楚国弄来的好酒。  
I specially had imported（进口的） from Chu.  
叫七里香。  
It's called Qilixiang.  
您尝尝。”  
Please taste it."  
雍纠已经紧张得。  
Yong Jiu was so nervous  
说话都不利索了。  
that even his speech had grown clumsy.  
“七里香？  
"Qilixiang?  
不是七步倒吧？”  
Not Seven-Step-Fall, is it?"  
“爹，您说笑了。”  
"Father, you jest."  
雍纠心里咯噔一下。  
Yong Jiu's heart skipped a beat.  
为什么？  
Why?  
因为酒里的毒。  
Because the poison（毒药） in the wine  
正是厉公托人。  
was exactly what Duke Li  
从楚国弄来的。  
had someone bring from Chu.  
名字就叫。  
Its name was  
七步倒。  
Seven-Step-Fall.  
意思就是。  
It meant that  
人喝下这种毒。  
once a man drank it,  
七步之内。  
within seven steps  
必定倒地身亡。  
he would collapse and die.  
“你喝。”  
"You drink it."  
祭足喝道。  
Jizu barked.  
“这，爹……”  
"F-Father..."  
雍纠已经。  
Yong Jiu had  
连整话都说不出来了。  
lost the ability to speak in full sentences.  
他心里只剩后悔。  
All that remained in him was regret.  
“喝不喝？”  
"Will you drink or not?"  
“爹，这……”  
"Father, I..."  
到了这时。  
By this point,  
雍纠已经知道。  
Yong Jiu knew  
事情败露了。  
the plot was exposed.  
还没等他。  
Before he could  
跪下求饶。  
drop to his knees and beg,  
祭足已经。  
Jizu had already  
一拍桌子。  
slapped the table  
大喝一声。  
and shouted,  
“来人！”  
"Men!"  
总理府的侍卫。  
Guards from the prime minister's residence  
一下冲了进来。  
rushed in at once  
把雍纠踹倒在地。  
and kicked Yong Jiu to the ground.  
“爹，我是被逼的。  
"Father, I was forced into it.  
饶命啊！”  
Spare my life!"  
到了生死关头。  
Only at the edge of death  
雍纠的嘴。  
did Yong Jiu  
才终于利索了。  
finally find his voice.  
祭足皱了皱眉。  
Jizu frowned.  
摆了摆手。  
He waved a hand.  
意思很明白。  
The meaning was clear.  
动手。  
Do it.  
侍卫们当然不客气。  
The guards were not polite.  
他们捏着雍纠鼻子。  
They pinched Yong Jiu's nose  
把那杯酒。  
and poured that cup of wine  
硬灌了下去。  
straight down his throat.  
别说。  
To be fair,  
楚国进口的毒药。  
that imported poison from Chu  
还真不错。  
really was effective.  
雍纠一步都没跑。  
Yong Jiu did not even take one step.  
倒不是因为毒发快。  
Not because the poison struck that fast,  
而是他。  
but because  
早就吓瘫了。  
he had already gone limp with fear.  
没过多久。  
Before long,  
他眼鼻口全流血。  
blood streamed from his eyes, nose, and mouth,  
人就死了。  
and he died.  

语法说明：<br>
语法标记：`时间状语从句`、`过去完成时`、`并列谓语`。<br>
句子主干：主句核心是 `Before Yong Jiu could kneel and beg for mercy, Jizu had already slapped the table and called for men.`。<br>
难点拆解：`Before Yong Jiu could ...` 是时间状语从句；主句里的 `had already slapped ... and called ...` 用过去完成时，表示祭足的动作先于“下跪求饶”这个过去时点发生；`slapped` 和 `called` 是并列谓语。<br>
常用短语：
- `take the bait`：上钩
- `seat of honor`：上座
- `beg for mercy`：求饶
- `go limp with fear`：吓瘫

后来人们在大水坑里。  
Later, people found his body  
发现了雍纠尸体。  
in a large water pit.  
厉公派人打捞上来。  
Duke Li had it hauled up.  
不用细看也知道。  
No close look was needed  
他是中毒死的。  
to see that he had died of poison.  
厉公知道坏了。  
Duke Li knew things had gone wrong.  
再一打听。  
After asking around,  
才知道雍纠没毒成老丈人。  
he learned that Yong Jiu had failed to poison his father-in-law  
反倒被祭足毒死。  
and had instead been poisoned by Jizu  
还扔进了水坑。  
and thrown into the pit.  
“唉，这个废物。”  
"Alas, what a useless man."  
厉公叹了口气。  
Duke Li sighed.  
怎么办？  
What now?  
跑吧。  
Run.  
再不跑。  
If he did not run now,  
连自己也得没命。  
he would lose his own life as well.  
往哪儿跑？  
Where could he flee?  
宋国不能去了。  
Song was no longer an option.  
算来算去。  
After weighing it all,  
还是蔡国吧。  
Cai seemed best.  
就这样。  
And so  
厉公逃去了蔡国。  
Duke Li fled to Cai.  
那一年。  
That year  
是厉公四年。  
was Duke Li's fourth year,  
也就是前697年。  
that is, 697 BC.  
后来厉公知道。  
Later, Duke Li learned  
雍纠是被老婆。  
that Yong Jiu had been  
给老婆卖了。  
betrayed（出卖） by his own wife.  
他又长叹一声。  
He let out another long sigh.  
“这个蠢货。  
"That fool.  
这世上最不可靠的。  
The least reliable thing in this world  
就是老婆啊。”  
is a wife."
